# R4 — Teacher workspace (v0.10.0)

The release that moves the teacher from the old `/admin` web tree to the deck's Teacher workspace
at `/teacher/*`. Every screen that exists today is rebuilt to `Quizzivy Teacher.dc.html`, the
backend gains what those screens draw and the server lacks, in-app notifications arrive for the
teacher and the student, a test can be imported from pasted text, the rich content editor
becomes the way a prompt and an explanation are written, and every legacy hashed join code is
rotated (D5).

R4 runs after R3 (v0.9.0, released 2026-10-04). It changes no permission rule and no API prefix, beyond T-R4.49's contract steps (the legacy
role leaves the contract and the `/admin` alias goes).
It adds operations, each declaring an `x-permission` from `70-redesign-overview.md` §4.1 and each
added to R2's isolation suite in the PR that adds it, with an `x-resource` entry for every uuid
it takes in its path, query or body (T-R2.16).

If v0.9.1 has not shipped, T-R4.49 carries its T-R3.1–T-R3.3 unchanged.

Modules the deck draws that ship later stay invisible here: Calendar and Attendance (R8),
Messages and Shared with me (R7), Courses and Vocabulary (R10), Gradebook and Reports (R9), the
Admin console (R5). No nav item, palette entry, menu item or button points at them (70 §2 rule 5).

**Deliverable:** the Teacher workspace from the deck, for every module that exists today, served
at `/teacher/*` inside the new `TeacherLayout`, with the backend it needs: profile and
preferences, signed-in devices, in-app notifications in both consoles, assignment review options
and per-student overrides, a grading queue, question level and skill, media rename and replace,
class schedule and room, dashboard figures, a version diff, imports from pasted text and alt
text for a question's image. The builder, the bank's Question editor and the import review
write prompts with one content editor, in the deck's frame. Every legacy join code is rotated
at release, with an in-app notice to its teacher.

**Exit criteria:** every `/teacher/*` route matches the deck at 360, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440, in
light and dark, with the sidebar expanded, collapsed and as a drawer (T-R4.51); the old teacher
tree is deleted and `router-chunks.test.ts` passes with its regexes updated, not weakened; no
active join code in production uses the legacy lookup; the pasted-text count fixture passes in
the Go and the web suite (T-R4.56, T-R4.57); the five canaries are green and untouched;
every CI step of 70 §8 is green in GitHub CI on every pull request, and once locally, in order,
at T-R4.52; released as `v0.10.0`, and Neon compute hours are measured for the week after (PR-7).

**Schema changes** (numbers assigned at merge; none indexes a populated table, so none is
`-- +goose NO TRANSACTION`; every new column on a populated table is nullable or has a constant
default, so the R3 binary keeps inserting during the rolling deploy without a fill trigger). In
these migration notes and the tests they name, "R3" means the previous release, v0.9.0:

**Order of work.** The backend tasks (T-R4.7–T-R4.22, T-R4.53–T-R4.56 and T-R4.62) and the
composites (T-R4.1–T-R4.3) wait for no shell and no screen and start at once, at most four
builders at a time, of five agents. Among themselves each waits only for what its "Depends on"
line names; T-R4.53 lands before the tasks that register per-actor limits, T-R4.56 follows
T-R4.55, and T-R4.62 follows T-R4.16, whose diff it extends. The route rename (T-R4.5) needs
nothing and runs first; the shell (T-R4.4) follows T-R4.2a, T-R4.3a and T-R4.5b and reads its
counts from `getDashboard` until T-R4.23; T-R4.6 follows the shell and T-R4.13. The content editor
(T-R4.63, then T-R4.64) follows T-R4.2a and T-R4.5b and needs no backend; the answer areas
(T-R4.66) follow T-R4.64; the question media block (T-R4.65) follows T-R4.35, T-R4.62 and
T-R4.66. Each screen task waits for the shell and for the backend task it names: the builder's
editor pane (T-R4.31b) and the bank's Question editor (T-R4.33) wait for T-R4.65 and T-R4.66,
and the review workspace (T-R4.38) for T-R4.63; T-R4.57 follows T-R4.36 and fills the paste side
of its Source control. Close-out (T-R4.48–T-R4.52) comes last. Branches are
`feature/t-r4-<nn>-<slug>` off `work/redesign-r4`.

**Decisions taken in this file, worth a second look.**

- **R2's contract steps ship inside R4 by default (2026-10-08).** `work/redesign-r4` holds
  00080 to 00091, so a v0.9.1 from `develop` cannot be numbered safely. T-R3.1 to T-R3.3 run as
  T-R4.49, and the v0.9.1 release checklist runs inside this one (`73-r3.md`, "Migration
  numbers"). Thuong may still ship v0.9.1 separately, after renumbering R4's merged migrations.
- **Time-based in-app notifications are materialised when the reader asks, not by a timer.**
  "Closes in 1 hour", "due tomorrow", "opened" and "result ready after close" are inserted
  idempotently by `GET /me/summary` and `GET /me/notifications`, at most once per five minutes per
  user. An in-app item only matters while its reader is in the app, and a background timer would
  wake Neon at every threshold of every assignment (the #134/#143 lesson). R7 adds the email
  channel, and with it the first in-API notification scheduler.
- **Submission notifications are one row per assignment per 15-minute bucket, upserted at submit
  time.** That is the deck's "Grouped every 15 minutes" without a flush job.
- **Legacy join codes are rotated by an idempotent job at API start-up, not by a manual
  command.** The API holds `JOIN_CODE_KEY` on every start, while an operator holds it only for
  R2's rekey procedure, supplied to `cmd/maintenance` from the offline copy (`operations.md`); a
  human step at release is one that can be forgotten; the job takes an advisory lock, audits
  each rotation as System and does nothing on its second run.
- **Extend is two operations.** Extending the window for everyone changes the assignment
  (`teaching.assignments.write`); extending or reopening for chosen students is an accommodation
  (`teaching.attempts.intervene`, the "Reset, void or extend attempts" row). We keep one
  requirement per operation here by choice, so the dialog picks the operation. Both recompute
  the deadline of attempts already in progress, which today keep the deadline they started with.
- **The Grading page explicitly finishes an attempt after its last manual answer is saved**
  (DG-64), using the existing separate Grade and Finish operations. A saved mark alone does
  not prove Finish or score release. Failed Finish remains retryable, including after reload
  through public attempt-list discovery and current review. Existing review/transcript policies
  govern visibility; the attempt page keeps its explicit button for re-grading.
- **Bulk actions reuse single-item operations through `BulkActions`**, which already confirms and
  reports partial failure. The exception is password reset: the single operation is rate-limited
  to five a minute per address (R2's `RateLimits()` entry, per actor from T-R4.53), so a
  class-sized reset would 429. It gets one bulk operation, capped at 40 students, hashing at
  most two at a time inside the global argon2 semaphore. It stays in the API rather than the
  worker: forty hashes take about ten seconds and the screen waits for the printable sheet.
- **The unpublished-changes banner comes from a diff, not from `updated_at`.** Publishing and
  "Make default" both bump `tests.updated_at` through its trigger, and a bank edit to a question
  the draft uses bumps nothing on `tests`. The diff that DG-66 needs anyway compares the draft
  with the latest version.
- **Media replace creates a new immutable asset** and re-points bank questions and draft-owned
  groups only (DG-09), and only those owned by the asset's owner. Published versions, and other
  owners' rows (bound through `scope.all` today, copies from R7), keep the old asset, which
  leaves the library but keeps serving them. `publish_snapshot_test.go` is the proof.
- **In the teacher workspace, content lists show the caller's own rows for everyone, admins
  included** (DG-53, the question R2 left to R4). Tests, bank, media, imports and assignments
  list only what the caller owns; `scope.all` still reads and edits any teacher's content by id
  and powers the Admin console's cross-teacher views. Students and Classes keep `scope.all`
  lists until R5's Admin Classes and Users take them over (T-R4.54, 70 §4.1).
- **Test cards show skills derived from their questions**, not a `tests.skills` column. Chips that
  are computed cannot drift from the content; the deck's removable chips are prototype state.
- **Nothing a teacher can do today disappears because the deck does not draw it.** The test card
  keeps a "…" menu (Duplicate, Archive, Delete); Classes keeps an Archived filter (the deck's own
  archive dialog promises "you can restore it any time"); "View results" on a student opens
  today's results sheet until R9's Gradebook; the API reference stays a Settings section for
  `system.api_reference` holders until R5 moves it to the Admin console.
- **Display time zone is per user; the server stays UTC.** The profile's time zone drives every
  date the web formats and every date the teacher types. "Today" on the dashboard and the 23:59
  expiry of a join code use the actor's zone, defaulting to `Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh`.
- **A new join code expires at 23:59 local on its last day**, as the deck draws ("24 Oct 2026,
  23:59"), rather than at the minute it was made N days earlier. A code that dies mid-lesson is
  worse than one that lasts the day.
- **Duplicate assignment has no Title field.** Assignments are named by their test; there is no
  assignment title to copy. The dialog keeps "Assign to".
- **The workspace switcher ships as a static label.** Its popover and the `workspace.admin` gate
  are built, but the "Admin console" row appears with the console in R5 (DG-53, rule 5).
- **"Paste a test" is built here, as its own import source.** The 2026-10-03 export draws it and
  D1 builds what the deck draws, so it lands beside the Imports rebuild (T-R4.55–T-R4.57). The
  text arrives through its own JSON operation, not a second body on `uploadImportSource`: that
  route streams, so the contract validator would see neither the text nor its limit.
- **A pasted test is plain text, whatever the clipboard held.** The box is a textarea and only
  its text is stored, so the deck's "formatting kept" pill variants and stage lines would be
  false and do not ship (DG-12). Recognition never takes an answer from a mark,
  so nothing a teacher needs is lost.
- **The server learns the conventions the paste page teaches, for text sources only, with one
  exception.** The rules of T-R4.56 are gated on the text evidence version, so Word and PDF
  results do not move, except for the one-line answer key, which is read for every evidence
  version (#233, decided 2026-10-04): the local corpus run may report keys that are now read,
  and nothing else. `recognition.Version` becomes `rules-v3` and `PipelineVersion`
  `word-pipeline-v3`; a run queued under v2 at the deploy fails `PIPELINE_RETIRED` and its
  teacher retries, as at the PDF release.
- **The paste page counts with the server's grammar, not the deck's script.** One fixture,
  `api/testdata/pasted-text-counts.json`, is asserted by a Go recognition test and a web unit
  test, so "Found so far" cannot contradict the review. That is what lets Start processing stay
  disabled, as drawn, while the count finds no numbered question.
- **A paste holds at most 100,000 Unicode code points, counted after NFC normalisation.** The
  page normalises before it counts and sends, so the contract's `maxLength`, which counts the
  text as sent, agrees with the counter; intake normalises again and counts the same way. The
  deck counts UTF-16 units and its patterns miss decomposed Vietnamese.
- **Status filters are the segmented control with counts; the Imports history changes layout by
  its own width.** The 2026-10-03 export replaced the filter pills and the 940px scrolling
  table. The control keeps the product's semantics, a group of `aria-pressed` buttons and no
  tablist, because a filter has no tabpanel. The history turns from a grid into cards through a
  container query at 960px, not through the deck's viewport arithmetic.
- **The To review chips and the Ready card read "need action" for a pasted import too.** The
  count covers every blocking finding, not only missing answers, so the deck's "needs an
  answer" wording does not ship in either place (DG-14).

The 2026-10-04 export redrew the builder's editor pane and the review's "Question" field. The
seven decisions below are this file's defaults for it. Thuong has not confirmed them; each has a
line in the open items, and all but the first an entry in `docs/design/gaps.md`.

- **The content editor is the field, for every teacher.** The deck draws the rich editor as the
  builder's "Prompt" and "Explanation" and as the review's "Question". Most of it is on
  `develop` already (`web/src/components/shared/content/editor/`), behind
  `VITE_RICH_QUESTION_EDITOR`, which is off. R4 gives it the deck's frame and removes the flag
  (T-R4.63, T-R4.64). Options stay plain inputs, as drawn, so `VITE_RICH_OPTION_EDITOR` stays
  off.
- **The editing mode is the form the field is stored in (DG-110).** "Rich text" is stored
  content and "Markdown" is the stored Markdown string, as the contract has them today. A
  field opens in its own form, and a new field is rich text. The two conversions happen only
  on the teacher's "Switch to Markdown" or "Apply conversion". The prototype opens every
  question in Rich text and converts a field left in Markdown without asking; with autosave
  that would rewrite stored content nobody chose to change.
- **One `QuestionEditor`, built to the builder's drawing (DG-108).** The product renders the
  builder's editor pane and the bank's Question editor with one component, and the export
  redrew it in the builder only. The prompt, the explanation, the answer areas, the grading
  note and the question media are built once (T-R4.65, T-R4.66) and shown on both pages; each
  page keeps its own frame. "Match case" stays on a gap, because the model has it.
- **A prompt and an explanation hold no media in R4 (DG-109).** The contract's
  `QuestionContent` excludes media. Adding it runs through the publish snapshot, the student
  payload and the engine, three high-risk areas at once, and nothing draws the student side.
  R4 builds the toolbar without "Image" and "Audio" and refuses a pasted or dropped file;
  audio or an image is attached as question media. The backend and the two buttons go to R6.
- **The builder's type menu offers the five types the product stores.** "Matching" and the
  "Include “Not given”" switch need R6's types (DG-60, DG-113), and a control without a backend
  stays off the screen, as the import's do (DG-67).
- **Question media keeps the model's switches (DG-111).** "Students can pause" has no model and
  is not built. "Allow skipping ahead" and "Show transcript after submitting" stay, because a
  teacher sets them today. An image's alt text is new data: one nullable column on the
  question, frozen at publish (T-R4.62). The limits are DG-63's on every screen.
- **A pasted image is left out and counted; it no longer refuses the paste (DG-115).** The deck's
  "Preview content after pasting" says how many images were left out. Today the converter
  refuses any paste that holds an image, and spec §7.1 says to reject it as one paste. T-R4.63
  changes that one case and keeps every other refusal; T-R4.50 changes the spec line.

---

### T-R4.1 — Console data composites
**Depends on:** R1 primitives; T-R4.3a for T-R4.1a
**Touches:** `web/src/components/shared/data/DataTable.tsx` (new), `web/src/components/shared/data/FacetFilter.tsx` (new), `web/src/components/shared/CardGrid.tsx` (new), `web/src/components/shared/Pager.tsx`, `web/src/hooks/usePage.ts`, `web/src/lib/pagination.ts`, `web/src/components/ui/pagination.tsx`, `web/src/components/shared/BulkActions.tsx`, `web/src/components/shared/BulkSelection.tsx`, `web/src/hooks/useBulkSelection.ts`, `web/src/components/shared/ListState.tsx`, `web/src/components/ui/table.tsx`, tests in `web/tests/units/shared/` (exists), plus `web/tests/units/ui/pager.test.tsx` and `web/tests/units/bulk-actions.test.tsx`, which pin today's Pager and BulkActions, and a new `web/tests/units/ui/table.test.tsx` for the primitive, a case file per part in `web/tests/support/deck-harness/cases/`
**Size:** M
**Delivered in parts:** three, each one branch and one pull request
**T-R4.1a:** `DataTable` and the `table` primitive's deck geometry. Depends on T-R4.3a (the content width) and, softly, on T-R4.2a (the checkbox geometry arrives through the primitive)
**T-R4.1b:** `Pager`, `usePage` (`size`), `BulkActions` as the inverted bar. Depends on T-R4.1a (same folder, `ListState`)
**T-R4.1c:** `FacetFilter`, `CardGrid`. Depends on T-R4.2a (the menu's parts); it imports nothing of `DataTable`
**As built:**
- **T-R4.1a: beyond the list,** `web/src/components/shared/data/tableLayout.ts` (the pure parts:
  thresholds, the visible set, the template, the row box) and the tests
  `web/tests/units/shared/{data-table.test.tsx,table-layout.test.ts}`. No locale key.
  `BulkSelection.tsx`, `useBulkSelection.ts` and `RowMenu.tsx` are composed as they are;
  `ListState.tsx` is not used by the table (the empty line is its own, and loading and error stay
  with the screen's `QueryStates`). None of the four was edited.
- **T-R4.1a: the props.** `DataTable<T extends { id: string }>` takes `label`, `columns`, `rows`,
  `rowSize`, `dense`, `shown`, `rowHref` or `onOpen`, `selection` with `rowName`, `menu`,
  `menuTrack` (`"44px"`), `padX` (16), `card`, `cardLayout` (`"stacked"` or `"joined"`), `framed`
  (true), `empty` and `footer`. A column is `{ id, header, track, showFrom?, align?: "end",
  cell(row, shown), aside?(row, shown) }`. The types refuse both ways to open at once and a
  selection without `rowName`. Every optional prop also takes `undefined`, so a screen writes
  `menu={can ? items : undefined}` for a role without the permission.
- **T-R4.1a: the sub-line is the screen's.** The deck's four sub-lines share no rule and no
  markup: Assignments joins two fields while either of two columns is dropped, the roster draws a
  dot, the bank an icon, Students adds a part per dropped column. So a cell is told the visible
  set (`shown`, a `ReadonlySet` of column ids) and the first column draws its own line. The set
  keeps its identity inside a band and each cell is memoised on its column, its row and that set:
  whatever else a cell draws from goes in a component of its own, and `columns` is a module
  constant or a memo.
- **T-R4.1a: the `shown` prop** replaces `showFrom` and the table's own band. Class members
  computes it from `useContentBand([520, 620, 720, 1000, 1080])`: name and Submitted always, then
  Average, then Joined via, then Joined, Joined dropped again from 1000 and back from 1080. A unit
  test checks those six sets against the deck's `left = tw × 2/3` arithmetic at every width from
  300 to 1500. The screen keeps the six sets as constants, so the identity holds.
- **T-R4.1a: a track is fixed or fractional with a minimum, never content-sized.** Every row is a
  grid of its own, so `auto` or `max-content` would give each row its own column width. DG-21 is
  met by the screen: a column whose Vietnamese text can outgrow the deck's fixed track declares
  `minmax(<deck px>, <n>fr)`. Cells carry `min-width: 0`. A cell's sized text (13px, 12.5px) is
  a block with its own `leading-normal`, because the deck's cell is the grid item itself: left
  inline, the cell's own 13.5px line makes it 1px taller than the deck's.
- **T-R4.1a: row height** is `rowSize`: `{ height }` (54, 56), `{ minHeight }` (60, 56) or
  `{ padY }` (10, the bank). The header is always 40px. `dense` takes 16px off a height or a
  minimum and halves `padY`; DG-37's "Meanwhile" cell is corrected in place to say so, with no new
  gap row. A three-line Assignments row is 57.75px, so the compact minimum of 44 does not clip it.
- **T-R4.1a: how a row opens.** What the first column's `cell` draws is wrapped in a `Link`
  (`rowHref`) or a `<button>` (`onOpen`): that is the tab stop, its keys are the element's own,
  and the first cell names the row through `aria-labelledby`. A click elsewhere on the row calls
  `.click()` on that element, so there is one path and a table with `onOpen` needs no router. A
  click with Ctrl, ⌘, Shift or Alt held does nothing off that element: `.click()` carries no
  modifier, so it would navigate in place where the link itself opens a new tab. Nor does a click
  that landed on a control inside the row (a link, button, input, label, a focusable element) or
  outside it: React sends a click in a portalled menu up through the row. The row is not
  focusable and not a button, which would swallow the row and cell roles and nest the checkbox and
  the menu in a button. So the first column's `cell` holds no control; one goes in the column's
  `aside`, drawn under it in the same cell and outside the link or button: the bank's removable
  tag chips (deck 522). The aside's text joins the row's name, and a card below 768 has no aside.
- **T-R4.1a: selection** is `useBulkSelection`'s object, whole. Every row of a selecting table
  carries `aria-selected`. A selected row is not tinted: the deck tints it on class members only,
  which T-R4.42 adds with the tone hook below.
- **T-R4.1a: `card` is optional, and there are two lists.** In the deck only Assignments and
  Students change below 768; the roster, the bank and class members stay grids at every width,
  checkboxes and menus included, so a table without `card` does too. `"stacked"` is Assignments'
  separate cards (the deck's button, line-height `normal`); `"joined"` is Students' one card of
  divided rows with the footer inside (deck 591). A card is a link, a button or plain, by the
  table's own `rowHref` or `onOpen`. A joined row draws its focus ring inside itself
  (`-outline-offset-2!`), because the card around it clips an outline drawn outside.
- **T-R4.1a: `framed={false}` and `align: "end"`** are class members' (deck 1422 to 1427): its
  table sits inside a section that is the card, and Submitted and Average are right-aligned.
- **T-R4.1a: the empty line** takes the rows' place under the header, outside the element with
  the table role, with the top border the bank draws (deck 530); the footer follows it. The deck's
  Assignments puts the line after the pager. Below 768 it sits in a card of its own, a state the
  deck does not draw. An `empty` of `null`, `false` or `""` draws no line, so a screen may write
  `empty={loading ? null : sentence}`.
- **T-R4.1a: content wider than the table scrolls sideways inside it.** The deck's card is
  `overflow: hidden`, and its own Assignments tracks need 876px at a content width of 860 to 875,
  and class members 380px on a 360 phone: the menu would be cut off and unreachable. The element
  with the table role is at least its tracks' minimum wide (`min-width: min-content`), so the
  header's background, the row borders and the hover tint span the scrolled width. Assignments
  therefore scrolls by up to 16px at a content width of 860 to 875, where the deck lets the tracks
  eat the right padding. A bare `fr` track would size to its content under this rule: write
  `minmax(0,1fr)`. The menu column's header cell is positioned, so its visually hidden name
  scrolls with the table: unpositioned, it sat outside the card and the page scrolled sideways.
- **T-R4.1a: the `table` primitive** gains, under `in-data-[scale=deck]:` only, 13.5px on a 1.5
  line; header cells 40px, 12.5px, on `--muted`, 16px side padding (their muted text and weight
  500 are today's classes); body cells padded 10px 16px with no fixed height; `--muted` on hover.
  The row's top border is drawn on the body cells, so the header row has none and the last row
  keeps its own against today's `[&_tr:last-child]:border-0`. Compiled with Tailwind to check
  that every deck rule follows its plain one at the same specificity. Both comments that cited
  the removed boards are gone.
- **T-R4.1a: left to others.** T-R4.1b passes its pager as `footer` and puts the bulk bar above
  the table. T-R4.25 adds the row tone: one more entry in `BodyRow`'s `cn()`. The checkbox and the
  row menu are T-R4.2a's, merged before this; the table does not rely on `RowMenu` stopping its
  own clicks. Every row's menu button is still named "Thao tác": naming it after its row needs a
  key and `RowMenu`'s `label`, left to the first screen.
- **T-R4.1a: compared with the deck** through the harness, light and dark, with the English
  strings: the Assignments grid (`r4-01a/assignments`), the Students grid (`students`), class
  members inside its section (`members`) and the Question bank's padded rows with the tag chips in
  the aside (`bank`), each in a container of the deck's content width at 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440
  with the sidebar expanded and collapsed, at 360, and on each side of every column threshold;
  Assignments' separate cards and Students' joined card at 360. The tracks, the box of every row
  and cell, the header band, type sizes, weights, colours, borders, paddings, gaps, the checkbox,
  the menu button, the hover fill and the cursor match. The differences: where the tracks need
  more than the card, the table scrolls and the deck clips (Assignments by 16px at a content
  width of 860, viewports 1164 expanded and 976 collapsed, by 1px at 875 and not at 859 or 876;
  class members by 50px at 360, where the deck cuts 30px and the menu with it; the bank by up to
  62px at viewports from 1024 to 1247, where the deck's thresholds ignore its 234px filter column
  and its last column is cut: T-R4.32 decides those thresholds); the harness rows of Students
  open, so they are buttons with a pointer where the deck's phone rows are plain; the status pill
  is 2px wider than the deck's with its dot in the ink colour, and the 32px avatar's initials are
  12px for the deck's 11.5px, both R1's primitives and not changed here. Seen in a browser for
  the first time: a joined row's ring shows on all four sides inside the row, its corners trimmed
  by the card on the first and last row; scrolled to its end, the header band, the row borders
  and the hover fill reach the last column, and a footer with `sticky left-0` stays in place; the
  `table` primitive on a deck surface has a 40px header at 12.5px and 500 on `--muted`, cells at
  13.5px padded 10px 16px under a top border (41.25px for one line) and `--muted` on hover. The
  states the deck does not draw were looked at in Vietnamese at 320, 360, 768 and 1280 (the
  cases `long-vi`, `empty`, `dense` and `primitive`): a long title ends in an ellipsis in the
  grid and wraps in a card; the empty line sits under the header, in a card of its own above the
  footer, or inside the joined card; a compact three-line Assignments row is its 58px of text
  with no space above or below; Tab reaches every checkbox, link, button, chip and menu, each
  with a ring, and the link's ring on a three-line row overlaps the row's borders by 3px. That
  look found the hidden header text above.
- **T-R4.1b: the deck's pager is a new file,** `components/shared/data/Pager.tsx`. Ten old pages
  keep `components/shared/Pager.tsx` (numbered links, nothing for one page) until their screens are
  rebuilt. T-R4.48 deletes with it `components/ui/pagination.tsx`, `pageWindow` and `pageCountOf`
  in `lib/pagination.ts`, the `pagination.*` keys and the Pager cases of
  `tests/units/ui/pager.test.tsx`; that file's `usePage` cases stay.
- **T-R4.1b: the props.** `Pager` takes `page`, `pageSize` and `total` as the API answered them,
  `noun(count)`, `sizes` (`PAGE_SIZES`) and `frame` (`"card"`). `noun` returns the screen's noun in
  the plural that fits the count, from the screen's own key: it is called with the total for
  "1–10 trên 312 bài giao" and with 0 for "Không có bài giao nào". Every figure is grouped for
  the locale, the positions and the page count too; the deck groups the total only.
- **T-R4.1b: it is always drawn.** One page and no rows read "Trang 1 trên 1" with all four arrows
  off. `pageRange` in `lib/pagination.ts` does the arithmetic: at least one page, the page clamped
  to the pages there are, both positions 0 for an empty list.
- **T-R4.1b: `usePageSize` is a second hook in `usePage.ts`; `usePage` and `pageHref` did not
  change.** `PAGE_SIZES` is 10, 20, 30, 50. The size is `?size=`, absent for the first size; a
  value that is not listed reads as the first. The setter replaces the history entry, removes
  `page` in the same update and keeps the rest. A screen calls the hook, sends the size as
  `limit` and puts it in its query key, not in `usePage`'s `filters`.
- **T-R4.1b: the arrows are links** to `pageHref`, so a page opens in a new tab and Back returns
  to the page before. One that cannot move points at the current page, is `aria-disabled`, at
  45% and takes no pointer. It stays in the tab order, as the old pager's do.
- **T-R4.1b: rows per page is the `Select` primitive,** `size="sm"`, restyled to the deck's
  control, with the primitive's deck classes restated under `in-data-[scale=deck]:` (the card
  fill, 13px). It is named by the visible label through `aria-labelledby`, not by a `<label>`:
  Radix's trigger opens on a label's click only until it has itself been pressed with a mouse.
  It shows the size in the URL, so it answers at once while the range waits for the API. Its list
  is the product's, at least 128px wide and aligned to the trigger's end.
- **T-R4.1b: the frame is the screen's choice at every width, and the Pager draws its own bar;**
  `DataTable`'s `footer` adds nothing. `"card"`, the default: 10px 16px, a top border and
  `sticky left-0`, so the bar stays while a wide table scrolls sideways. `"plain"`: 4px 2px, no
  border, not sticky, under a list that is not one card: Assignments below 768 (that screen's own
  `useMediaQuery`), the import history, a card grid. Students' joined card keeps `"card"` on a
  phone.
- **T-R4.1b: below 768 the Pager drops** rows per page and the first and last arrows, and nothing
  else.
- **T-R4.1b: the bulk bar has two forms, chosen by `useDeckScale()`.** Off a deck surface it is the
  old bar, byte for byte, for the eight old pages; `bulk-actions.test.tsx` pins its HTML. On one
  it is the deck's inverted bar, in plain classes kept in constants of their own. The file writes
  no `in-data-[scale=deck]:` class, which would sit in the old bar's markup too. The old bar's
  buttons are the `Button` primitive and carry that primitive's own deck classes, as they did.
- **T-R4.1b: `BulkAction` gains `icon` and `destructive`** (true when absent; false gives a plain
  confirmation, the deck's "Reset passwords"). `BulkBarButton` is the bar's button for a control a
  page passes as `children`, such as an action that opens its own form; the built-in actions
  render through it. On a deck surface the count is `bulkBar.selected` ("Đã chọn 2"), clear is a
  30px × button named by `common.clearSelection`, and `className` lands on the bar (class
  members: `mx-3 mb-2.5`). The confirmation, its list, the progress line and the failures are
  untouched.
- **T-R4.1b: `hideOnPhone`.** The bar never hides by itself. Assignments and Students pass the
  prop, because below 768 their tables are cards without checkboxes; the bank and class members
  stay grids with checkboxes and keep the bar. A confirmation open when the bar hides stays open.
  The prop is read on a deck surface only.
- **T-R4.1b: not copied as drawn, for the finisher.** The action's border is
  `border-muted-fg/35`, the nearest token to the deck's raw `rgba(127,127,127,.35)`. The bar sets
  `--focus` to `--primary-fg`, because the global ring's `--muted-fg` does not show on
  `--primary`. Its buttons take `hover:bg-primary-fg/10`, 45% when disabled and the platform's
  cursor; the deck draws no hover, no disabled state and a pointer.
- **T-R4.1b: `BulkSelectAll` no longer passes the six `indeterminate:` classes;** the checkbox
  primitive carries them since T-R4.2a. Its own commit.
- **T-R4.1b: not built.** No "go to page" field and no numbered pages: the deck
  draws neither. Tests are `shared/{data-pager,page-size}.test.tsx`; the locale namespaces are
  `pager` and `bulkBar`, after `pagination`. `useBulkSelection.ts` and `ListState.tsx` were not
  edited. The cases in `deck-harness/cases/r4-01b.tsx` are
  `pager-roster`, `pager-bank`,
  `pager-one-page`, `pager-plain`, `pager-joined`, `pager-wide-table`, `bulk-bar` and
  `bulk-bar-members`, and for the states the deck does not draw `pager-empty` and
  `bulk-bar-long-vi`.
- **T-R4.1b: compared with the deck** through all ten harness cases at 360, 768, 1024,
  1280 and 1440, light and dark, at expanded/collapsed or drawer reference widths:
  200 rendered states match the visible geometry and tokens without settled overflow.
  These isolated composites use shell-derived widths; the shell and drawer are verified
  by T-R4.4. The default card-framed phone fixture is an undrawn variant; `pager-plain`
  matches the Assignments phone pager. The raw bulk-border substitution, added focus/hover
  and Radix Select/shared Confirm replacement are the stated departures. Forty-four
  functional/motion cases cover sticky horizontal scroll, URL replace/filter preservation,
  Back/new-tab, disabled-link focus, empty/one/last pages, resize-held plain/destructive
  confirmation and failed-only retry. Long Vietnamese was checked at 320, 360, 768 and
  1280 in both themes. Observer-driven frames prove Select's 150ms and confirmation's
  200ms entry/exit; reduced motion is 0.01ms with no intermediate frames or settled running
  animation. The pinned popup's 120ms ease-out movement was measured separately. No title
  marquee or continuous motion exists in this scope. Initial scratch path/selector and
  pre-settlement resize/focus failures were retained and corrected in the harness; no
  production defect or existing-test change was needed.
- **T-R4.1c: scope and corrections.** FacetFilter depends on T-R4.2a's menu parts, not
  DataTable or ListState; the chosen-filter state is absent from the deck and follows DG-128.
  No page, primitive, existing grid, URL hook or ListState was changed.
- **T-R4.1c: FacetFilter** takes `label`, optional `title`, readonly `{ value, label }` options,
  readonly `selected` and `onChange(next)`, plus optional `clearable`, `disabled`, `align` and
  `menuClassName`. It controls no selection state, URL, request or query; the screen owns those.
- **T-R4.1c: the trigger** is a native button, dashed, 34px high under deck scale, with a 14px
  CirclePlus and no fixed label width. A nonempty selection adds the segmented control's 18px
  count pill, hidden from accessibility; `facet.active` says the count once in the button's name.
  Unknown selected values count too. Both the native button and Radix trigger receive disabled.
- **T-R4.1c: the menu** defaults to end alignment and 240px (`data-[scale=deck]:w-60` on its
  portalled content); the caller can replace that width, including the Students' 220px status
  menu. Optional title and truncated option labels compose T-R4.2a's parts unchanged.
  Ticks append or remove a value, preserve unknown values and other values' order, and stay open.
  Clear is disabled at zero and clears all values while closing; `clearable={false}` removes its
  row and separator. No options shows the muted `facet.none` label and keeps the trigger enabled.
- **T-R4.1c: CardGrid** takes `label`, readonly `items`, stable `itemKey`, numeric `min`, optional
  `gap: 10 | 12` (12 by default), `className` and a card-rendering child. Its named `ul role=list`
  has one keyed, min-width-zero single-cell grid `li` per item and renders nothing when empty.
  A file-local no-redundant-roles lint directive preserves that explicit role with marker-free styling.
  Tracks are `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(<min>px, 100%), 1fr))`: one card keeps its drawn
  column, and the inner min prevents the 300px Classes track overflowing a 292px phone content.
- **T-R4.1c: intentionally absent.** No menu search, single-choice facets, option counts,
  card chrome, loading/error/empty grid UI or pagination; screens compose their own cards and
  QueryStates. There are no marquee markers on these static labels and no reflow animation.
  Menu entrance/exit remains the approved primitive's fade; reduced motion is global.
- **T-R4.1c: strings and proof.** `facet.clear`, `facet.active` and `facet.none` sit after queue
  in vi then en. Two new unit files cover controlled menus, count semantics, keyboard/focus,
  geometry classes and keyed card state; `cases/r4-01c.tsx` supplies the comparison fixtures.
  Independent QA passes 55 distinct browser cases at five widths in both themes, with
  expanded/collapsed sidebar reference widths, measured tracks and gaps, 292px overflow,
  stable keyed state, native focus/keyboard and long Vietnamese. Actual menu entry and
  Escape/Clear/outside exit run for 150ms with native ease; reduced motion settles in 0.01ms.
  Trigger/check feedback is immediate and grid reflow is static; these labels have no marquee.
  The original 160px-grid fixture caps its caller at 560px; a separate genuine CardGrid
  fixture at the measured 722px picker width proves four 173px tracks without changing it.
  First scratch selector/easing-layer/caller-width failures remain in the QA report;
  this is component fixture acceptance, not a production-page or D4 comparison.
**Done when:**
- [x] `DataTable` is a CSS grid with ARIA table roles. Each column declares its track, a minimum
      and an optional `showFrom` content width; a cell is told which columns are shown
      (`cell(row, shown)`), and each screen's first column draws the sub-line the deck draws for
      that table: Assignments shows "classes · when" whole while either column is dropped, and
      Submitted and Status drop without adding to it. A table whose rule is not an ascending
      threshold on the content width (class members, on two thirds of it from 1000) passes the
      visible set itself; the horizontal padding and the menu track are the table's (16 and 44px
      by default, 18 and 32px for class members). Columns size to content with a minimum, never a
      fixed label width (DG-21)
- [x] T-R1.5's deferred `table` primitive (`web/src/components/ui/table.tsx`) gets its deck
      geometry here, alongside `DataTable`, the first table on a deck surface (#165). Its current
      importers render outside `[data-scale="deck"]` and keep their classes until their screens
      are rebuilt
- [x] Tri-state select-all header; selection is `useBulkSelection`'s map of id to item, kept
      across pages (the confirm list names items of other pages); row click and Enter open the
      row; the row menu stops propagation
- [x] A table with `card` (Assignments, Students) renders its cards below 768 with no checkbox
      and no row menu, and those two screens hide the bulk bar there (`hideOnPhone`). The
      roster, the bank and class members stay grids at every width, and the bank's and class
      members' bulk bars are drawn at every width
- [x] The header row is 40px. Each table passes its drawn body height (54, 56 or a 60px minimum;
      the bank's rows are padded 10px 16px). Compact takes 16px off a fixed height and halves a
      padded row's vertical padding (DG-37)
- [x] `Pager` reads "1–10 of 312 {noun}" (vi first), rows per page 10/20/30/50 in `?size=`,
      "Page X of Y", first/prev/next/last at 32px; phones drop first, last and rows per page;
      `usePage` gains `size` (`?size=`, sent to the API as `limit`); it already resets the page
      when its filters change. The bar's frame is the screen's at every width: `card` (in the
      card, sticky at its left edge) or `plain` (under separate cards: Assignments below 768,
      the import history)
- [x] `BulkActions` restyled as the deck's inverted bar ("{n} selected", 30px actions, × clear),
      keeping its confirm and partial-failure reporting
- [x] `FacetFilter` (dashed 34px "⊕ Class", check menu, "Clear filter") exists; `CardGrid` takes
      the deck's `minmax` width. There is no `FilterChips`: since the 2026-10-03 export no screen
      draws a row of filter pills, and a status filter is the segmented control with counts
      (T-R4.2)
- [x] Unit tests: column drop and inline fallback at a threshold (the Assignments fixture reads
      "classes · when" at 859 and at 720 and adds nothing at 559; the class-members fixture shows
      Joined at 900, hides it at 1040 and shows it at 1100), tri-state header, selection across
      pages, card list at 360 (`viewport("phone")`), keyboard row activation, pager ranges

### T-R4.2 — Console form and overlay composites
**Depends on:** R1 primitives
**Touches:** `web/src/components/shared/form/FormDialog.tsx` (new), `web/src/components/shared/form/fields/*` (new), `web/src/components/shared/ConfirmDialog.tsx`, `web/src/components/shared/form/{NumberStepper,RadioCard,ChipInput,TagCombobox,FileDrop}.tsx` (new), `web/src/features/question-bank/components/TagsField.tsx` (moved into `TagCombobox`), `web/src/features/media/useFileDrop.ts` → `web/src/hooks/useFileDrop.ts`, `web/src/components/shared/{DirtyBar,SettingsLayout}.tsx` (new; R3 built neither), `web/src/components/shared/{Sheet,CopyField}.tsx` (new; `Sheet` on `components/ui/dialog.tsx`, `CopyField` composing `components/shared/CopyButton.tsx`), `web/src/components/shared/RowMenu.tsx`, `web/src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx`, `web/src/components/ui/segmented.tsx`, `web/tests/support/deck-harness/` (new, T-R4.2a)
**Size:** M
**Delivered in parts:** three, each one branch and one pull request; T-R4.2b follows T-R4.2c to reuse its native local-Escape helper
**T-R4.2a:** menus (`dropdown-menu`, `RowMenu`), `Segmented` (icon, count, scroll), checkbox, tabs, and the deck harness. Depends on nothing in R4
**T-R4.2b:** `FormDialog`, `ConfirmDialog` on it, `FileDrop` (with the `useFileDrop` move, its own commit), the date and time field's deck geometry; the dialog primitive gains only its place in the z-order under deck scale (the tooltip with it), and the teacher's frame is the composite `DialogShell`. Depends on T-R4.2a and T-R4.2c
**T-R4.2c:** `Sheet`, `CopyField`, `DirtyBar`, `SettingsLayout`, `NumberStepper`, `RadioCard`, `ChipInput`, `TagCombobox`. Depends on T-R4.2a
**As built (T-R4.2a: menus, Segmented, checkbox, tabs):**
- **Touched beyond the list:** `components/ui/{dropdown-menu,checkbox,tabs}.tsx`,
  `features/auth/AccountMenu.tsx` (class strings of its `deck` form only),
  `lib/revealWithin.ts` (new), `tests/support/deck-harness/` (new),
  `tests/units/ui/{menu,segmented,checkbox,tabs}.test.tsx`, `tests/units/shared/row-menu.test.tsx`,
  `tests/units/lib/reveal-within.test.ts`. No locale key and no token was added.
- **Which variant sits on which element.** `DropdownMenuContent` is portalled and carries
  `data-scale` itself, so its geometry is written `data-[scale=deck]:` (card, radius 10, the
  float shadow, `--z-popover`, 5px of padding, no width, and a `max-height` of 60vh or of the
  room Radix reports beside the trigger, whichever is less, with its own scroll: Radix flips a
  menu but never moves it up or down to fit). There it also passes Radix a `collisionPadding` of
  8, the margin the deck's `openMenu` keeps from the window's edges before it flips or shifts.
  What sits inside it (label, items, the check box, separator) and what sits on a deck
  surface (the row trigger, Segmented, checkbox, tabs) is written `in-data-[scale=deck]:`. A
  caller replaces a primitive's deck class by restating it with the same variant, after it.
  What is new here and has no earlier look to keep is plain classes, the same on and off a deck
  surface: `DropdownMenuMeta`, `DropdownMenuItemText`, and in Segmented the count, the icon and
  its 7px gap in the large row, and what `sm` and `xs` set besides their deck height and radius.
- **The menu's new parts, all optional:** `DropdownMenuLabel` (the title row), `DropdownMenuMeta`
  (the muted, tabular figure at a row's end) and `DropdownMenuItemText`. On a deck surface a row
  is 13px on a 16px line (30px high) and a 9px gap, with a 14px icon; a disabled row is at 45%,
  `variant="destructive"` reads `--danger-ink`, the separator has no margin, and a check row
  draws the deck's 16px box.
- **A label truncates only inside `DropdownMenuItemText`.** A bare text child of a flex row cannot
  take an ellipsis, so it wraps when it is longer than the menu. A menu that lists names a user
  typed (classes, tags) wraps each in the part; a fixed label needs nothing.
- **The student's account menu restates what the primitive would now override.** On the content,
  `data-[scale=deck]:` `bg-card`, `shadow-float`, `z-(--z-popover)`, `w-60`, `rounded-lg`, `p-1.5`;
  on each item, `in-data-[scale=deck]:` `gap-2.5`, `p-2`, `leading-4`, `data-[disabled]:opacity-50`
  and the 15px icon. It keeps 240px, 6px of padding, 32px rows on a 10px gap; what it gains is
  that height cap and the 8px margin, and neither shows under the top bar.
  `tests/units/ui/menu.test.tsx` pins each restated class.
- **`RowMenu`** takes `title` and `label` (the trigger's name, `common.actions` by default). On a
  deck surface the trigger is 30px with a 16px icon and the menu 220px; another width is passed
  as `data-[scale=deck]:w-*`.
- **`RowMenu` stops the click, and only the click,** on its trigger and on its content, because a
  React event bubbles through a portal to the row. Pointer and key events still reach the row:
  a row that opens on Enter must act only when it is itself the event's target.
- **`Segmented`** exports `SegmentedOption` (`icon`, `count`) and takes `size` `sm` (28px, 12.5px)
  and `xs` (26px, 12px, radius 6 in a radius 8 track) and `scroll`. The count is the 18px primary
  pill, drawn at zero too, and is read as part of the button's name ("Đang mở 3"). With no icon,
  no count, no `scroll` and the default or large size, the markup is byte for byte R3's.
- **A line height is a whole pixel, as Chrome gives the deck's buttons:** 16px for a menu row and
  for the `sm` row and a scrolling track of Segmented, 15px for `xs` and the meta figure, 18px
  for a tab. The default and large rows without `scroll` keep R3's markup, so their label sits
  0.7px above the deck's in a box of the same size, as on R3's two screens.
- **A scrolling track moves its own `scrollLeft`** (`lib/revealWithin`), never `scrollIntoView`,
  which would also scroll the page: for the pressed option when it changes, and for an option
  that takes focus while the edge cuts it, which a browser reveals only when it is wholly out of
  view. Its buttons take a 1px outline offset, so the track does not clip the ring. The deck
  draws a 15px icon in the 32px row and 13px in the 26px one; the 30px row takes 15px and the
  28px row 13px.
- **Checkbox:** on a deck surface radius 4, the `--ring` colour on `--card`, an 11px tick. The bar
  of the mixed state is in the primitive, the only classes added off a deck surface;
  `BulkSelection` still passes the same six and tailwind-merge keeps one copy.
- **Tabs** on a deck surface are the deck's underline row. The list scrolls sideways and would
  clip the global focus ring, so a tab draws its ring inside its own box (`-outline-offset-2!`),
  and a tab that takes focus while the row's edge cuts it is revealed as a Segmented option is.
- **The deck harness** (`tests/support/deck-harness/`, never in the production build) renders
  `?case=<file>/<name>` under `DeckScale`, in a container of `?width=<px>` when given. A task adds
  `cases/<task>.tsx`, which exports `cases` and imports nothing from the harness. The harness
  gives a case no router, query client or user: a case that needs one sets it up. Its README has
  the commands.
- **Not built:** the Source control's fixed 168px options (DG-21); the plain count of the review
  filters (deck line 1814; T-R4.38 adds it if it needs it); toggle and toggle group (no deck
  surface uses them).
- **Compared with the deck** through the harness, light and dark: the row menu (768 to 1440; the
  deck draws cards and no row menu below) and its disabled rows, the class filter's check menu
  before and after a tick (360 to 1440), the Question type menu with its meta, Segmented with
  counts and the Imports filter (360 to 1440, the sidebar expanded and collapsed, in a track of
  the deck's own width), the `sm`, `xs` and large rows and the tabs (360 and 1280), the checkbox.
  Every box, type size, weight, colour, radius, shadow, padding and gap matches. The differences:
  the separator is its own 1px element, where the deck draws a top border on the next row; the
  row's button keeps the hover fill while its menu is open (the deck's shared menu does not know
  its trigger; the Student deck fills its own avatar button); a flipped menu sits 4px above its
  trigger, where the deck places it by an estimate of 34px a row (27px above for five rows); menu
  rows keep the platform's cursor, as every button of the product does; every scrolling track
  hides its scrollbar, which the deck does on Imports and the review and not on Assignments; the
  checkbox's tick is the product's filled shape in the deck's 11px box and its bar is 8 by 2px,
  both heavier than the deck's 11px glyphs, while a check row of a menu draws the glyph itself.
  Seen in a browser for the first time: near the window's bottom the menu opens above its
  trigger; at 1280 by 300 with its trigger midway it is 123px high, ends 8px above the edge and
  scrolls to its last row; a click or a key on an option cut by either edge leaves it whole,
  3px inside the track, and the page does not move. The student's account menu was measured on
  `/app` at 360 and 1280, light and dark, in both languages, against a build with the release
  branch's primitives: 43 computed values and the box of each of its nodes are equal, but for
  `max-height` (none, now 480px at an 800px window) and `overflow-y` on the content. Segmented
  on the student's result and settings is byte for byte and value for value the same.
  The states the deck does not draw were looked at in Vietnamese at 320, 360, 768 and 1280: a
  long title wraps, a long label in `DropdownMenuItemText` ends in an ellipsis and bare text
  wraps, nothing scrolls sideways, every control takes a visible ring from the keyboard.
**As built (T-R4.2b: dialog shell, form dialog, confirm dialog, file drop, date and time):**
- Teacher geometry belongs to `DialogShell`; the primitive only raises content and overlay to deck z85, preserving all three Student frames.
- `DialogShell` exposes Header, Body and Footer with connected focus return; Teacher content and overlay mount and close instantly. Its max-height changes without a transition, so the footer stays inside a newly shortened viewport from the first layout.
- `FormDialog<V>` owns draft and touched state; the caller owns submission and close. Equivalent props preserve typing; reopening resets, and callbacks reject closed, pending, disabled or unmounted forms.
- Its props are `open/onOpenChange`, title and description values or functions, icon/danger/width/className, initial/fields/validate, submit/cancel/hideCancel, pending/disabled/error, onSubmit/children/returnFocus.
- `FormField<V>` includes text, email, number, password, area, date, time, select, seg, chips, list, toggle, file, info and qr; `FormOption` carries value, label and meta. Date and time were omitted from the original plan list.
- Required and caller errors apply only to shown controls. Invalid submit remains pressable with aria-disabled and focuses the first invalid control; pending is native disabled and submission is guarded.
- Engine fields share a native form with its associated footer submit; legacy children stay outside it. Native Enter submits current values exactly once, respects invalid and locked states, and leaves the controlled form open.
- `ConfirmDialog` preserves confirming and notice callers, adds an optional icon and danger tile, and retains the confirming Button's data-variant.
- `Segmented` adds optional fill; all legacy defaults remain unchanged.
- `FileDrop` chooses or removes the first file and permits same-file reselection; file validation and upload are caller responsibilities.
- `useFileDrop` moves to `hooks/` and adds enabled reset and listener suppression. A page with its own window drop must disable it under dialogs in T-R4.35 and T-R4.65.
- `DateTimeField` keeps joined inherited controls off deck and a single picker on deck; T-R4.48 removes the inherited branch. Optional trigger ARIA and disabled props preserve absent defaults; owned portals dismiss on lock without reopening after unlock.
- Date commits immediately, including the highlighted current day of an empty required date; time and datetime wait for Done. Native mouse and focused Enter preserve this distinction. Wall-clock values, off-step minutes, parent Cancel, reopen and locale semantics stay intact; dismissal discards only the uncommitted picker draft.
- Tooltip raises deck content to z100 above dialogs with unchanged appearance. The preview field remains deferred to StudentPreview and future course work.
- `formDialog` and `datePicker` namespaces follow layout in vi and en. Independent native QA compared 130 frame/control states at 360, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440 in both themes and passed nine unchanged Student production regressions. Original reduced-picker and current-day failures, the resize observation and the fixture composition failure remain recorded. The scoped repair campaign passes 37 distinct native cases in 39 executions, retaining two scratch Cancel-label locator failures. The normal picker enters over 120ms ease-out without an exit; under reduced motion it has no animation from the first rendered frame. Teacher resize stays within 450px from the first layout. Shared Select and Student motion retain their existing rules; no title marquee applies to these controls. The corrected unshipped Extend demonstration uses a separate named help button to avoid Tooltip overwriting Switch state. Its measured frame/row differences are +13.03125/+14.90625px at 360 and −3.765625/−1.890625px at wider boards; widths, paint, focus and overflow pass. These disclosed fixture differences do not approve a future product Extend dialog. Long Vietnamese text remains reachable by native body scrolling. Exact-head CI and future screen adoption remain separate gates.
**As built (T-R4.2c: sheet, copy field, dirty bar, settings layout, form controls):**
- **Touched beyond the list:** `components/shared/form/chips.ts` (the two comparisons, `withChip`
  and the `useChipEntry` hook that `ChipInput` and `TagCombobox` share), `form/stepperValue.ts`,
  `form/tagOptions.ts`, `lib/localEscape.ts` and the nested sheet Escape regression;
  `ChipInput.tsx` also exports `ChipBox` and `CHIP_INPUT`, which
  `TagCombobox` composes. Ten keys in a new `controls` namespace, before `toast`: `unsaved`,
  `discard`, `decrease`, `increase`, `removeTag`, `addTag`, `tagSuggestions`, `createTag`,
  `newTag`, `tagKeys`. No token, no dependency, nothing under `components/ui/`, `features/` or
  `layouts/`. Everything here is plain classes, the same on and off a deck surface; a primitive's
  deck class is restated with `in-data-[scale=deck]:` where it changes.
- **`Sheet`** `{ open, onOpenChange, title, subtitle?, leading?, width?: 320 | 380 | 420 (420),
  footer?, children }` **takes Radix's dialog primitive** (`Dialog` from `radix-ui`) and imports
  nothing from `components/ui/dialog.tsx`: T-R4.2b writes that file's place in the z-order while
  this is built, and a deck class on its content or title would beat anything restated here. The
  primitive gives the focus trap, Esc and the backdrop; it marks the page behind `aria-hidden`
  and sets no `aria-modal`, and it leaves `aria-describedby` off by itself when there is no
  subtitle, so the sheet passes none.
- **The sheet's frame.** `min(<width>px, 100%)` at the right edge and the full height, so a phone
  gets the whole width with no second rule. 420: header 16 by 18 with a rule, body 18 with an
  18px gap, footer 14 by 18 with a rule. 380: header 14 by 16 with a rule, body 14, no gap. 320:
  a header with no rule and one 16 by 18 column with an 18px gap; its header stays in place where
  the deck's scrolls away with the column. The body is the only scroller. `title` is the `h2` that
  names the dialog (15px, 600), `subtitle` its description, the close button is `common.close`.
  Overlay and panel are at `--z-sheet`, under `--z-dialog` and `--z-popover`. It slides in from
  the right over 200ms and is still under `prefers-reduced-motion`; the deck draws no motion. It
  is portalled, so it carries `data-scale` itself when it is opened from a deck surface.
- **Focus returns as `DeckDialog` returns it** (the rule is copied, not imported): to the control
  that opened the sheet, or to the `<main>` it was in when that control has gone, unless the page
  has already put focus somewhere else.
- **`DirtyBar`** `{ dirty, saving?, error?, onDiscard, onSave? }` is new; the student's bar in
  `StudentSettingsSections.tsx` was the model and is not lifted. Nothing while not `dirty`;
  `error` takes the sentence's place as an alert; Save changes is `type="submit"` for the
  section's own form unless `onSave` is given; both buttons are off while `saving`. It wraps.
- **`SettingsLayout`** `{ label, active, sections: readonly { id, label, icon, to, content }[] }`.
  The navigation is links, so a section is a URL, with `aria-current="page"` on the current one.
  Every section's content is rendered in its own panel and hidden with the `hidden` attribute
  unless current, so a form keeps what was typed. Row and column are CSS at 768. The column is
  200px or as wide as its longest label (DG-21), where the deck fixes 200px and scrolls. The row
  hides its scrollbar and brings the current link, and a link that takes focus, into view with
  `revealWithin`; a link draws its ring inside its box, because the row clips.
- **`NumberStepper`** `{ label, value, onChange, min, max, step? (1), format?, disabled? }`. The
  value is an `<input type="text" inputMode="numeric">` with the spinbutton role, the one tab
  stop. **It can be typed although the deck draws no input,** so T-R4.27b loses nothing today's
  form can do: with the deck's step of 5 a limit of 47 minutes would be out of reach. Unfocused it
  shows the formatted text, focused the bare number, selected; only digits are kept; Enter or
  blur commits, clamped and never snapped to the step; Escape and an empty field restore the
  value; `onChange` is never called per keystroke. The arrows, Home, End and the two buttons step
  from the committed value and drop a draft. The buttons are out of the tab order and do not take
  focus from the field. A hidden copy of the text sizes the field, so it keeps the deck's 54px
  least width and grows with a longer unit.
- **`RadioCards`** (in `form/RadioCard.tsx`) `{ label, value, onChange, options: readonly { value,
  label, hint?, disabled? }[], className? }` is Radix's `RadioGroup`: one tab stop, arrow keys
  choose. Its own unmodified arrow handler focuses the enabled destination synchronously,
  skips disabled cards and wraps with the group's direction. A guarded native microtask click
  selects once, with pending work cleared before the caller callback and on stale input,
  focus, value or semantic option changes. Space, click and Home/End retain Radix behavior.
  This fixes the pinned library's early-keyup race without changing its primitive or CSS.
  `className` replaces the deck's grid.
- **`ChipInput`** `{ label, values, onChange, placeholder?, removeLabel, tone?, size?, frame?,
  commaAdds?, invalid?, same? }`. Enter, a comma and blur add; a paste adds every part before a
  comma; Backspace in the empty input removes the last value, once per press, not while the key
  is held. An entry is trimmed and put in NFC. **`same` decides what is already there.** The
  default, `sameIgnoringCase`, is the deck's lower-case equality, so accents count;
  `TagCombobox` passes `sameFolded`; and **T-R4.66 passes exact equality when "Match case" is
  on,** because the server grades accented and, then, cased answers as different. Both
  comparisons are exported from `form/chips.ts`. `commaAdds={false}` is for answers that may hold
  a comma (T-R4.66 decides). Removing a chip returns focus to the input; a long value ends in an
  ellipsis inside its chip.
- **`TagCombobox`** `{ label, tags, onChange, suggestions: readonly { tag, meta? }[],
  placeholder? }` is the chip field (neutral, framed, 22px) plus the list. `tagOptions` lists the
  suggestions that contain the draft under `fold`, not already chosen, those that start with it
  first and otherwise in the order given, six at most, then "Create" unless a suggestion or a
  chosen tag equals the draft under `fold`. The bold part is found on the suggestion's own text,
  combining marks included. Enter or Tab takes the active row, which is the first until an arrow
  moves it; Escape closes the list and keeps the draft; with the list closed Enter adds the draft,
  in a suggestion's spelling when it equals one. Blur adds the draft, as today's field does (the
  deck's only closes the list). The list is under the field at `--z-popover`, not portalled; the
  keys line is outside the listbox.
- **For the editor chain (T-R4.64 onwards), which swaps `TagsField` for `TagCombobox`:**
  `TagsField.tsx` and `QuestionEditor.tsx` are not touched here. Today's field offers recent tags
  before anything is typed; the deck's list opens only on a draft, and `TagCombobox` follows the
  deck. The data (the recent tags, `listQuestions`' `tags`, the counts for `meta`) stays with the
  caller, which also orders the suggestions.
- **`CopyField`** `{ label, value, failedMessage, copyLabel?, mono? }` **is built from the deck's
  parts; the Teacher deck draws no such field:** the form label, the 38px input, read-only, and
  the 28px bordered action button. `CopyButton` gained `label`, `onFailed` and `className` and
  nothing else: with none of them its markup is today's, pinned byte for byte, idle and copied.
  On a refused clipboard the field's whole value is selected and focused.
- **Borders and focus.** The stepper's box, the chip frame and the copy field take the `--input`
  border (DG-31), where the deck draws `--border`. A framed field's border turns `--ring` while
  its input has focus and the frame, not the input inside it, draws the 2px focus ring (DG-30).
  The stepper's buttons round their outer corners, so the hover fill stays inside the box.
- **States the deck does not draw:** a switched-off stepper and radio card (45%), an invalid chip
  frame (the danger border and `aria-invalid`), a footer on the 380 and 320 sheets, a long title
  (it wraps), the clipboard's refusal under the copy field.
- **Browser comparison and remaining layering.** Until T-R4.2b puts the dialog at `--z-dialog` and the
  tooltip at `--z-popover`, both open under a sheet (`z-50` against 70). A focused input marks
  `data-local-escape="true"` only while the tag list is open or the stepper has a draft (including
  empty). `Sheet` passes `preventLocalEscapeDismissal` to Radix's native `onEscapeKeyDown`:
  the first Escape closes the list or restores the value and keeps focus; the next closes the
  sheet. **T-R4.2b's `FormDialog` must pass the same helper to `DialogContent`'s
  `onEscapeKeyDown`**, so nesting follows the same rule without document listeners. The harness
  cases are `cases/r4-02c.tsx`. Independent QA compared the seven drawn composites and
  settings navigation at 360, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440, light/dark, with sidebar-derived
  expanded/collapsed or phone widths; 280 original fixture states and 80 distinct acceptance
  cases pass. Isolated composites do not retest the shell/drawer. Long Vietnamese has no
  settled sideways overflow. The phone DirtyBar's extra 5px follows the prescribed wrapping
  departure; editable 16px text, focus rings and the undrawn CopyField remain stated choices.
  Actual Sheet and overlay entry/exit are 200ms ease, with intermediate frames and removal;
  reduced motion is static with immediate removal. Other controls use their declared static
  states and shared hover transitions. No continuous motion or measured title marquee exists.
  QA found the rapid radio race; after the reviewed correction, 57 product checks plus one
  generic-Radix diagnostic pass, including native zero-delay arrows, callback counts,
  cancellation and 20 unchanged geometry/focus comparisons. Original failed traces remain;
  the generic primitive still reproduces its race. Existing tests are unchanged: the new
  sibling adds 24 cases, and all 34 radio cases pass; four repair mutants fail assertions
  after the earlier 30 control mutants, each with exact source restoration.
**Done when:**

- [x] Segmented takes an optional icon and an optional count per option (the deck's 18px

- [x] `NumberStepper` (36px, −/+, unit suffix, min/max clamp; its value can be typed, committed

- [x] `FileDrop` shows "Choose a file or drop it here", a limits line and remove; drag state per

- [x] `SettingsLayout`: 200px section column, a row on phones, sections stay mounted so a form

- [x] `RowMenu` restyled: 220px, flips above near the bottom, height capped at 60vh, meta,
