### T-R4.54 — Own rows in the teacher workspace's content lists (DG-53)
**Depends on:** —
**Touches:** `server/internal/shared/access/scope.go` and the six list handlers in `server/internal/modules/*/http`; the descriptions in `api/openapi.yaml` that say one of these lists widens with `scope.all` (`listWordImports`, the `scope.all` permission's own). The database-backed cases for tests, questions and media go in each module's `application/tests/`; only imports and assignments have `repositories/tests/`
**Size:** S
**As built:**
- **Touched beyond the list:** `shared/access/tests/access_test.go`; each module's
  `http/tests/scope_test.go` (new in `tests`, which had none);
  `server/tests/isolation_flow_test.go`; the generated files; in the contract also `WordImport`
  and `listAssignments`; `docs/plan/60-backend-architecture.md` (the kernel's `Scope`),
  `docs/quizzivy-spec-v0.3.md` (v0.49: §5, §16) and the scope bullet in `AGENTS.md`. One
  repository changed, against the brief: `assignments/repositories/assignments.go`, with
  `ListInput.EveryTarget` in `assignments/domain/commands.go` (the two assignment bullets below).
- **One method, six call sites.** `access.Scope.Own()` is the same user with `All` cleared. Each
  of the six handlers builds its list input from `httpapi.ScopeFromContext(ctx).Own()`, and its
  second queries (facets, tags, the bank's counts, the library's byte total) read that same
  input. No other handler calls `Own`.
- **The narrowing is in the transport.** A repository still lists every teacher's rows for a
  scope with `All`: every read and write by id shares those statements, and R5's Admin console
  will ask for the wide list. The cases named "scope.all" in the database-backed tests keep
  pinning that; the cases beside them pin the scope the handler now passes.
- **"Own" for an assignment is the teacher's reach:** created by the caller, or targeting a
  class the caller teaches (`visibility.AssignmentIDs`).
- **An Admin's own assignment names every target in the list.** The repository used the list's
  scope for the rows and for the targets a row names, so narrowing the rows alone left the
  Admin's assignment on another teacher's class with no class and a `targetCount` short of
  `submittedCount` ("5/0"). The handler sets `ListInput.EveryTarget` from the caller's
  `scope.all`: it lifts the reach from the targets and leaves it on the rows, so a listed row
  names and counts what `getAssignment` does. A caller without `scope.all` runs the statements
  it ran before.
- **`listAssignments?classId=` of a class the caller does not teach lists nothing, as a missing
  class does, unless the caller holds `scope.all`.** The Admin then reads those of their own
  assignments that target it (`EveryTarget` again), so the assignments card of another
  teacher's class (`listClasses` keeps `scope.all`) shows the Admin's own and no teacher's, as
  decided. A teacher's answer is unchanged, which the isolation suite relies on. T-R4.42 draws
  that card.
- **Other consequences, not worked around.** An Admin who edits another teacher's test by id
  picks from their own bank and media. The media binds. The bank does not: a draft holds only
  its owner's questions (T-R2.12a), so every question that picker offers is refused as an
  unknown one, and the owner's questions, the only ones the draft accepts, are not offered.
  T-R4.31b draws that picker. The legacy dashboard's count of open assignments still covers
  everyone until T-R4.19.
- **Media has no read by id in the contract.** Its by-id operation is `deleteMedia`, which
  keeps `scope.all`, and other modules bind an asset through `Readable` in the caller's scope.
  Both are pinned. In `tests`, `previewTest` and `publishTest` build their scope inline and are
  pinned at the caller's beside `getTest`.
- **For T-R4.19:** `liveAssignments` counts with the same narrowed scope the Assignments list
  uses, `httpapi.ScopeFromContext(ctx).Own()`.
- **The contract changed in words only:** `PermissionKey`, `WordImport`, `listWordImports` and
  `listAssignments`. The generated diff is comments and the embedded spec.
- **The isolation flow asks the Admin for the six lists** after it has opened a teacher's rows
  by id: each answers with no row of either teacher and counts nothing. The Admin then assigns
  to a teacher's class, and the listed row, and the list filtered by that class, must name and
  count what the assignment opened by id does. It is the one test that joins the handler and
  the repository; CI's "Server tests" job is its first run.
**Done when:**
- [x] `listTests`, `listQuestions`, `listQuestionGroups`, `listMedia`, `listWordImports` and
      `listAssignments`, with their facets and tab counts, show the caller's own rows for every
      caller, admins included: in these lists `scope.all` does not lift the owner filter
- [x] Reads and edits by id keep `scope.all` (70 §4.1); `listStudents` and `listClasses` keep
      `scope.all` lists until R5's Admin Classes and Users take them over
- [x] Tests: as the Admin, another teacher's test, question, group, media, import and assignment
      are absent from each list and its facets and still open by id (200); a teacher's lists
      are unchanged; R2's isolation suite stays green

      command sees it), and a normalised text of more than 100,000 code points or 20,000
      non-blank lines, the extraction block cap (413 `IMPORT_SOURCE_TOO_LARGE`). It does not
      refuse a text without questions: the blocking `NO_QUESTIONS` finding covers that. Its
      messages name the pasted text, not a file (vi first)
- [x] `listWordImports` does not match a text source's stored filename: the filename half of
      the search skips sources with `format = 'text'`; title and companion-file names
      still match, and the operation's description says so. Test: `q=txt` and `q=past` return no
      pasted import whose title lacks them, its title returns it, and T-R4.21's facets count
      the same rows
- [x] Extraction (`adapters/import_text.go`): the exported `adapters.TextEvidence(text,
      sourceID, role)`, as `PDFEvidence` is for a PDF, drops a BOM, turns CRLF and CR into LF,
      normalises the text to NFC and makes each non-blank line one paragraph block `l{n}`, n
      being the line's number in the pasted text, with no spans. The evidence version is
      `text-lines-v1`
      and the document carries one informational finding, `TEXT_MARKS_UNAVAILABLE`, as a PDF
      carries `PDF_MARKS_UNAVAILABLE`. Recognition accepts the version, and normalisation skips
      a text source
- [x] `PipelineVersion` becomes `word-pipeline-v3`, so a worker that predates text sources never
      claims a text run
- [x] `downloadImportSource` returns the stored `.txt` and `getWordImportSource` its line blocks,
      with no change to either operation; retention removes the object as it removes a file. A
      pasted import still takes an answer-key file through `uploadImportSource`, like any import
- [x] No rate-limit entry, as `uploadImportSource` has none: the import quotas and the intake
      slot bound it. If `uploadImportSource` gains a bucket in `PrincipalRateLimits()` (T-R4.53),
      `pasteImportSource` shares it
- [x] Pinned tests updated, none weakened: `permissions.golden` gains the operation,
      `imports_test.go` counts 15 protected import operations, and `isolation_cases_test.go`
      gains its row, so teacher B pasting into teacher A's import gets 404
- [x] Tests: `import_text_test.go` (BOM, CRLF, NFD input, block ids across blank lines, the
      20,000-line cap); `upload_test.go` (the command: 100,000 code points of Vietnamese text
      with an astral character accepted, 100,001 `ErrTooLarge`, invalid UTF-8 and a NUL
      `ErrInvalid`, a replay, a changed replay); `intake_test.go` (through the router: 100,000
      code points answer 201, 100,001 answer 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED`, 20,001 lines answer 413,
      a NUL 415, the quota and busy answers, a `.txt` upload refused);
      `text_pipeline_test.go` (a pasted test from
      intake to its draft, as `pdf_pipeline_test.go` does for a PDF, and a v2 worker leaving a
      v3 run unclaimed). The scope hand-off and the format branch in intake, normalisation and
      extraction are mutated once each, and a test fails each time
- [x] `20-data-model.md` records both migrations

### T-R4.56 — Recognition conventions for pasted text (rules-v3)
**Depends on:** T-R4.55
**Touches:** `server/internal/modules/imports/domain/recognition/{recognize.go,exam.go,keys.go,labels.go}`, `server/internal/modules/imports/domain/recognition/tests/{recognize_test.go,support_test.go}`, `server/internal/modules/imports/repositories/tests/text_pipeline_test.go`, `server/internal/core/adapters/tests/import_text_test.go`, `api/testdata/pasted-text-counts.json` (new)
**Size:** M
**Done when:**
- [x] The rules below apply only to an exam whose evidence version is `text-lines-v1`, except
      the one-line key, which applies to every evidence version (#233). A Word or PDF paper
      otherwise recognises exactly as under `rules-v2`: the existing `recognize_test.go` cases
      pass untouched, and the local corpus run reports no change other than keys that are now
      read
- [x] `*` marks the correct option: a `*` that is the last or the first non-space character of an
      option is taken out of its content and makes that option the answer. Several starred
      options of one question are one answer ("B, D"), not a conflict. A `*` anywhere else, in
      a stem or inside an option, stays content
- [x] An answer line under a question reads `Answer: B`, `Ans: B`, `Answer - B` and `Đáp án: B`
- [x] A one-line key, `Answer key: 1-B 2-C` (also `Key:` and `Đáp án:`), is read as the key's
      entries when it holds at least two pairs. A pair's separator is `-`, `:`, `.` or `)`, in a
      key only, so `1-B` never becomes a question label. `Đáp án: 1-B 2-C` is no longer attached
      to the last question as one value. This rule applies to every evidence version, file or
      text. `Answers: 1. B 2. C` is read the same way. A line with fewer than two pairs keeps
      today's meaning: after `Answer:`, `Key:` or `Đáp án:` its value is the answer of the
      question above it, and after `Answer key:` or `Answers:` it is not read
- [x] `recognize_test.go` covers #233 with the existing `recognize(t, exam(lines(...)))` helper:
      two three-option questions followed by each of `Đáp án: 1-B 2-C`, `Answer key: 1-B 2-C`,
      `Answers: 1. B 2. C` give answers B and C and no conflict; `Đáp án: B` under a question
      is still that question's answer, and `Answer key: B` under one still gives it no answer
- [x] Option letters may be lower-case; a bare `1:` is a question label; a run of three or more
      underscores is a gap, while dots and ellipses keep their threshold
- [x] True / False / Not given is not a question type until R6 adds `tfng`: `Answer: Not given`
      stays a short answer the teacher fixes in review, and a test pins that. T-R6.13 maps the
      type for pasted text as for files
- [x] `recognition.Version` becomes `rules-v3`
- [x] `api/testdata/pasted-text-counts.json` holds texts with their expected sections, questions,
      answered count and missing question numbers. A repeated-number case also carries
      `duplicates`, the numbers the page's note names; only `paste-scan.test.ts` asserts it,
      since the server reports no duplicates. A Go test in `import_text_test.go` builds each
      text's exam with `adapters.TextEvidence` (T-R4.55), never with a helper of its own, so
      the NFD case passes through the extractor's NFC, and compares them with the review
      summary and its `MISSING_ANSWER`
      findings; T-R4.57's unit test reads the same file. Cases: the deck's sample (3 sections,
      10 questions, 8 answered, 4 and 7 missing) and the page's example; each rule above;
      `Question 1` without punctuation, `Q1.` and `Cau 1.`; a bare roman heading, `Part A` and
      `Bài tập 1`; "0.25 points each"; a key heading alone on its line; numbering that restarts
      in each part; an answer at the end of a question line; decomposed (NFD) "Phần 1"; a `*`
      inside a stem; a bare number repeated in one part (`1.` `2.` `2.` `3.` is three
      questions) and a bare number out of order; `Question 2` twice (four questions)
- [x] `text_pipeline_test.go` takes the deck's sample from intake to its draft and finds the same
      four numbers
- [x] Each rule and the text-source gate is mutated once, and a test fails each time; the PR
      lists the mutations. The gate's mutation covers every rule except the one-line key, which
      has a mutation of its own: restricting it to text sources fails the #233 file cases

### T-R4.62 — Alt text for a question's image
**Depends on:** T-R4.16
**Touches:** `migrations/00097_add_questions_media_alt.sql`, `migrations/00098_add_test_version_questions_media_alt.sql`, `api/openapi.yaml`, generated code, `server/internal/modules/questions/{domain/question.go,domain/input.go,repositories/store.go}`, `server/internal/modules/tests/domain/diff.go`, `server/internal/modules/tests/repositories/{publish_snapshot.go,version_draft.go,duplicate.go,preview.go}`, `server/internal/modules/attempts/` (the student question and the result's question), `web/src/features/take-test/components/`, the result's question image, `web/src/features/tests/components/StudentPreview.tsx`
**Size:** M
**Delivered in parts:** two pull requests, the server half first; the web half merges before T-R4.30 edits `StudentPreview.tsx`
**As built, server half, T-R4.62a (2026-10-09):**
- **The migrations are `00097` and `00098`,** the next numbers after `00092` at `9e838c83`. The
  reservation of `00093` to `00096` for T-R4.11, 9 and 12 that stood when this was written is
  void: T-5 governs, so a task takes the next number above the integration branch's highest
  when its PR is opened, and renumbers if another PR merges first (T-R4.11 took `00099` and
  `00100`, T-R4.12a `00101`). `media_alt text` is nullable with no default and no backfill, and
  `questions_media_alt_check` and `test_version_questions_media_alt_check` hold 1 to 1000
  characters, added with the column, so there is nothing to validate. D-33 and §37 record it. No
  check ties the column to an image: the write path does that, and a database check would fail an
  R3 write that swaps an image for audio on a question that had alt text, during the rolling
  deploy.
- **The contract gains `mediaAlt` as an optional nullable string** on `QuestionInput` and
  `StoredQuestionInput` (1 to 1000 characters), `AdminQuestion`, `StudentQuestion` and
  `ResultQuestion`. `StoredQuestionInput` is beyond the plan's list and needed: the group
  endpoints read their members through it, and a member's alt text would be dropped by the first
  group read-modify-write without it. It is optional everywhere, so no fixture changed and the web
  build is untouched. `previewTest` returns `StudentQuestion`, so the version preview has it.
- **A value for a question whose media is not an image is a 400** `VALIDATION_FAILED` with
  `error.details.mediaAlt`, the answer every other field error of a question write has
  (`audio`, `transcript`), not a 422. `Input.Validate` also refuses a blank value, one over 1000
  characters and one with a NUL (the request schema already refuses `""` and the length). Publish
  validates the draft question again and the group check treats it as it treats the other text.
- **A write replaces the stored value,** as it does for `level` and `skill`: an update that leaves
  `mediaAlt` out clears it. T-R4.62b and T-R4.65 must send it back on every autosave. The bank
  store also writes it only beside an image (`imageAlt`), so clearing the media, or changing it to
  audio, leaves NULL whatever a caller handed the store. A file replacement (T-R4.17b) keeps it,
  because it never changes the kind.
- **Publish copies it in the INSERT that freezes the question.** `loadQuestions` selects it with
  the draft question and `freezeQuestion` writes it; only the column lists grew. `lockDraftContent`,
  the order of its row locks, `questions.LockForDraftUse` and `media.LockForVersionUse` are not
  touched, and `publish_snapshot_test.go` is untouched and green. Restore as draft copies it in
  `copySnapshotQuestion`'s `INSERT ... SELECT` and, for a group member, through `readFrozenMembers`.
  A test duplicate references the bank questions it lists, so standalone alt text travels by
  reference (`duplicate.go` needed no change); a duplicated group copies its members through the
  bundle, which carries `Input.MediaAlt`. The version preview and the student's paper read the
  frozen column.
- **The diff puts it in a question's `media` part** (`diff_paper.go`), so an alt-text edit is one
  `changed` entry under media and raises `unpublishedChanges` by one; the fingerprint that pairs a
  restored copy with its original includes it, so a restored copy still matches and a copy that
  lost its alt text reads as added and removed. A question published before the column reads NULL,
  so a teacher who adds alt text to it sees one change, which is true.
- **In attempts** the change is four lines: `domain.Question.MediaAlt`, the column in
  `questionsQuery` and its scan, and the two response mappings. `Questions` serves the paper, the
  session and the result. The payload-leak walks are unchanged and green.
- **Beyond the Touches list:** `questions/http/{input,questions}.go` and
  `questions/application/internal/support/shared.go` (a bank duplicate rebuilds its input through
  `InputOf`), `tests/repositories/{diff,group_frozen,group_store,publish_load,publish_groups}.go`,
  `tests/domain/{draft,diff_paper,group_validation,publish_validation}.go`, `tests/http/tests.go`.
- **The spec is v0.62.** §7 `Question` gains `mediaAlt`, the §13.3 sketches of `questions` and
  `test_version_questions` gain `media_alt`, and "Changes since v0.61" records the behaviour.
  T-R4.63 (#424) merged first with v0.61, so this entry took the next number.
- **The attempt review does not carry it yet.** The teacher's attempt review builds
  `AdminQuestion` from the frozen question in `attempts/http/admin_attempts.go` and leaves
  `mediaAlt` unset; T-R4.47, the attempt review, carries it. Its server read
  (`Reviews.questions` in `attempts/repositories/review_review.go` and the mapping in
  `admin_attempts.go`) is left out of this half so as not to collide with T-R4.11, which works in
  attempts.
- **Web writers that must send `mediaAlt` back,** because a write replaces the stored value and
  one that drops it clears it: the bank editor (`web/src/features/question-bank/questionSchema.ts`
  and `QuestionEditor`); the group composer's recovery
  (`web/src/features/question-groups/recovery.ts:40`, a zod object that strips unknown keys, so a
  draft restored through it loses the field unless the schema names it); the builder's inline
  question editing (`web/src/features/tests/pages/teacher/TestBuilderPage.tsx:1075`,
  `starterQuestion`); and T-R4.65's media block, which edits it. T-R4.62b carries the first three
  and T-R4.65 the last; their Done-when boxes say so.
- **For R5 (after v0.10.0): the image rule becomes a database invariant.** Clear `media_alt` on
  `questions` where the kind is not an image, assert that no `test_version_questions` row holds the
  pair (publish validation and the R3 binary make that so; a version is not rewritten), add
  `CHECK (media_alt IS NULL OR media_asset_kind IS NOT DISTINCT FROM 'image'::app.media_kind)` to
  both tables `NOT VALID`, then `VALIDATE` them. The kind is already denormalised on both tables,
  so the predicate needs no trigger or join. `20-data-model.md` §37 has the three files and why the
  predicate says `IS NOT DISTINCT FROM`.
- **Evidence.** Nineteen one-line mutations, one at a time, each failed a test of this change: the
  freeze, the draft read, the restore copy, the frozen group read, the group copy, the version
  preview, the paper, the student and result mappings, the bank store's image rule and its update
  write, request validation, publish validation, the bank duplicate, the version diff read and the
  diff term. The R3-shaped insert (neither column named) succeeds on both tables, and each check
  holds the bounds 1 and 1000 and refuses 0 and 1001.
- **Canary runs, high-risk area.** Same commands on the branch point `9e838c83` and on the last
  code commit `c066130e`, against a scratch PostgreSQL 18.6 database, every exit code 0, nothing
  skipped. Passing tests, branch point then last commit: the tests module's integration tier 342
  then 360; its application package, which holds `publish_snapshot_test.go`, 121 then 126; the
  attempts application package, which holds `TestTheSameClientSeqFromTwoSessionsBothPersist` and
  the payload-leak walks, 151 then 152; the attempts repositories with the grading-queue database
  provisioned, 14 then 14; the questions module, 85 then 113. The web canaries and the take-test
  unit tests read the same on both: `audio-player` 14 tests, `client.refresh` 10, `router-chunks`
  13, `autosave-unmount` 8 and `tests/units/take-test` 35 files with 516 tests. The nested
  `--- FAIL` lines in those logs are the fixture-lifecycle children that fail on purpose under a
  passing parent. The diff from `9e838c83` shows none of the five canary files, nor anything under
  `modules/access`, `features/take-test`, `features/integrity` or `features/media`.
**As built, web half, T-R4.62b (2026-10-10):**
- **One reader, `take-test/components/QuestionImage.tsx`**, takes the question (`media` and
  `mediaAlt`, the shape `StudentQuestion` and `ResultQuestion` share) and draws its image through
  `ContentImage` in R3's frame (DG-80), with `alt` the question's text or the generic
  `takeTest.imageLabel` when it has none; it draws nothing for audio or no media. One component
  rather than three `<img alt>` edits, so D4's preview frame (T-D4.16) and its caption (S-25,
  not built) change one place. No caption prop is added before D4 needs one.
- **Where it is drawn.** `QuestionSheet` (the engine) uses it in place of its inline image.
  `StudentPreview` already renders through `QuestionSheet`, so the builder's preview and the
  version preview take the alt text with no edit to `StudentPreview.tsx`, which T-R4.30 and then
  T-R4.31b edit. The result's `ReviewItem` now draws the question's image under the prompt row;
  it sits inside `[data-slot=result-answers]`, which already carries `PAPER_SURFACE` (DG-35).
- **The writers.** `QuestionValues.mediaAlt` is required (`.nullable().default(null)`, as `level`
  and `skill`), so a constructor that forgets it fails `pnpm typecheck` rather than clearing the
  stored value: `toFormValues` reads `question.mediaAlt`, and `emptyQuestion`, the builder's
  `starterQuestion` and the group editor's `memberValues` name it. The builder's inline editing
  goes through `toFormValues`. The group recovery's zod object names it (`z.string().nullish()`).
  `QuestionEditor` sets it to null beside `mediaAssetId` whenever the media changes, because the
  server refuses alt text on a question whose media is not an image: a swap to audio, or a
  removal, would otherwise fail the next save.
- **The loss this closes was latent, not live.** On `work/redesign-r4` at `00c7c45e` only the
  question API writes `media_alt` (create, update and the group endpoints, from the request
  body); the Word import, the seed and the migrations never set it, and no web screen does until
  T-R4.65. Only a direct API call could have stored a value for a web save to clear.
- **Tests:** `take-test/question-image.test.tsx` (alt, the generic label for null and absent,
  nothing for audio, retry); a preview case in `builder/group-preview.test.tsx`; a result case
  in `results/result.test.tsx` (the image under the prompt, on the paper surface, and the generic
  label beside it); round trips for the bank (`question-bank/metadata-roundtrip.test.ts`, set and
  null, through `toFormValues`, the schema and `updateQuestion`) and the group recovery and
  `memberValues` (`question-groups/metadata-roundtrip.test.ts`); and the editor's two media
  changes (`question-bank/editor.test.tsx`). Removing the recovery line fails its two cases, and
  removing the editor's line fails its two.
**Done when:**
- [ ] The 2026-10-04 builder draws "Alt text: describe the image for screen readers" under a
      question's image, and the model has no field for it (DG-111)
- [x] `add_questions_media_alt`: `media_alt text`, nullable, 1–1000 characters (the bound
      `ContentImage.alt` has), on `questions`. `add_test_version_questions_media_alt`: the same
      column on `test_version_questions`, nullable, no backfill. Down drops each
- [x] Contract: `QuestionInput` and `AdminQuestion` gain nullable `mediaAlt`. A value is refused

- [x] Publish copies `media_alt` into the version row, keeping the `media.LockForVersionUse` and

- [x] The version diff (T-R4.16) counts a changed `media_alt` as `changed`, under media, so

- [x] (T-R4.62b) The engine's and the result's question image (R3's frame, DG-80) and the

**Size:** M

the existing permission gates, own metrics, shared home query and independent live
query. Home/live poll every 30s; nav summary polls every 60s. Independent integrated
production-source review is approved. The focused six public suites pass 110 tests,
and current typecheck plus scoped fixture lint/format pass. Independent native QA
approved the staged frontend: 39 distinct Dashboard cases plus 23 shell/smoke,
64 passed executions including two corrected own permission probes. All ten settled
width/theme boards and six lower-card scroll views were viewed. Actual LiveDot and
skeleton hover/focus pause, reduced static motion and the retained chart's 200ms
height transition pass. Real home/live and summary requests confirm the 30s/60s
cadence. Hidden-tab behavior and actual Assign wizard composition were not newly
proved by these mocked native cases. Dashboard titles remain static.

fixture count. The explicit 2020.js entry preserves validator behavior; actual
collection and 126 public contract/dashboard tests pass. The repository fixture
now combines complete initial eligible identities with its owned rows, preserving
All semantics and exact own/Admin/zero boundaries. Full flagged identities span
100+5 public rows with no extras, duplicates or omissions. Source review and
independent app/migrate QA approve the one-file repair: each role passes six parent

is proved by trace assertions and computed styles; the final paint was not captured

### T-R4.25 — Assignment detail: header, stats, Students tab and the attempt sheet
**Depends on:** T-R4.4, T-R4.5b, T-R4.1a, T-R4.1b, T-R4.2a, T-R4.2c, T-R4.3b
**Touches:** `web/src/features/assignments/pages/teacher/AssignmentDetailPage.tsx`, `web/src/features/attempts/components/{Monitor.tsx,AttemptSheet.tsx (new)}`, `web/src/features/attempts/pages/teacher/AssignmentAttemptsPage.tsx` (retired, redirected), `web/tests/units/assignments/detail.test.tsx`, `web/tests/units/attempts/{monitor,papers}.test.tsx`, `web/tests/e2e/teacher-shell.spec.ts` (new, if this is the first route in the new shell)
**Reuses:** `Monitor` (roster data and live polling), `FocusLossCell`, the integrity `Timeline`, `TeacherNoteCard` / `setAttemptNote`, `StatStrip`, `Sheet`, `DataTable`
**Size:** M
**As built:**

- **Ownership:** the actual detail leaf declares its 1320px Teacher handle; small derivation/URL modules and a sheet-owned note controller accompany the page, roster, compact timeline, redirect, locale and public-test changes.
- **Full monitor:** one detail-level read owns live polling and all statistics, regardless of the roster's search/filter/page; average is the rounded mean of graded positive-denominator score ratios, and missing values remain unknown.
- **Paper identity:** workspace and permission gates precede private reads; a review must match the assignment, requested attempt and student before its sheet content or events appear, including historical deep links absent from the roster.
- **Notes:** authorized teachers share the existing teacher-only attempt note, so copy says “Not visible to students” under DG-84; grading edits, while grading or intervention reads, without changing API scope or the full-review autosave card.
- **Departure:** blur saves serialize immutable attempt payloads and drain later edits; switch, X, Esc, backdrop, tabs and ordinary SPA/Back departure await the latest save, retaining failures with generic retry, stay or explicit discard even when the assignment becomes unavailable or workspace permission is lost; unload warns but cannot promise asynchronous durability.
- **Default contracts:** compact Timeline reuses neutral paired events and omits its default note card; full mode remains unchanged; DataTable gains optional row tone/opening predicates and omits only directly empty row menus while preserving their header/cell/track and existing keyboard behavior.
- **URL:** both retired Teacher and case-insensitive Admin paper addresses translate the old filter to `roster`, set `tab=students`, preserve other query/hash values and replace history; sheet opening pushes, close replaces only `attempt`, and this roster opts into hash preservation for pager links, size setters and implicit filter resets while shared defaults stay unchanged.
- **Staged grading:** header Grade counts full-monitor pending answers and opens the first eligible paper's sheet, whose Grade link opens the existing full review; the literal filtered grading queue remains T-R4.14/28 work.
- **Staged settings:** pinned-version Questions and actual Settings facts remain useful; existing Publish/Edit, live row Extend/Reset/Void, Close early and closed Reopen are preserved; combined Everyone/Chosen students/Notify Extend and the redesigned dialogs remain T-R4.26 after 12/13.
- **Accepted close:** the page retains only the selected assignment/attempt IDs after an accepted same-page close and drives the existing controlled Sheet to false; Presence owns its exit and removal. Current identity and permissions still gate every private element, and a first late response cannot reveal content during exit. Outgoing reads, edits, retries and navigation stop, while the painted countdown retains its last active value. Cross-page unmount keeps its save/security contract without promising a global outgoing animation.
- **Native repair geometry:** deck search fields preserve icon clearance at both densities without changing off-deck controls; the fixed 54px phone row uses compact status and a truthful flag/count with a full localized accessible label. Missing counts remain unknown. Actual layout and focus still require browser verification.
- **Failed-note recovery:** ordinary blur reads the current draft and does not retry a known failure, including later edits; explicit Retry or departure-save still drains the latest value. A failed departure requests focus once on the current authorized invalid note, with no replay after identity, reopen or permission changes. Active-write Discard still waits for the real write outcome.
- **Origin and loading repair:** an additive per-row menu context forwards a stable Actions ref for event/effect use only; intervention close returns to that actual origin or its captured main fallback. Deck ListSkeleton hosts pause descendant shimmer on hover/focus-within without adding a tabstop or changing off-deck motion. The narrow ref-forwarding lint directive leaves the rule enabled elsewhere.
- **Verification boundary:** public deferred-save, identity, permission, recovery, filtering and polling assertions and isolated mutation restoration cover source behavior; the real-route shell spec is provided, but native geometry, focus, Sheet motion and CI remain independent acceptance gates.
- **Motion:** this title and row labels are static; no marquee or invented panel entrance was added, and reused Sheet/menu/control motion still requires actual intermediate/settled-frame and reduced-motion browser evidence.
**Done when:**
- [ ] Header: crumb, title, status pill, "{classes} · {when} · {duration}", "…" menu, Extend,
      primary "Grade {n} answers" when answers are pending
- [x] Stats Submitted x/y, In progress, Average, Flagged (danger ink above 0); four columns from
      640 content width, two below
- [x] Underline tabs Students / Questions / Settings in `?tab=`
- [x] Students tab: Student, Status (Submitted, In progress, Not started, Timed out, Needs
      grading), Score, Time, Focus lost, Submitted; drops at 560/820/700/940 with the inline
      "status · n× focus lost" line; flagged rows highlighted (D8); paginated; polling through
      `useIdlePolling` while live
- [ ] Row click opens the 420px attempt sheet at `?attempt=`: initials and name, Score and Time
      spent tiles, the attempt timeline, a private note ("Only you can see this", saved on
      blur), "Open full attempt" and primary "Grade answers" (→ Grading filtered to this
      student)
- [x] `/teacher/assignments/:id/attempts` redirects to `?tab=students`, and
      `LegacyTeacherRedirect` gains the same rule for `/admin/assignments/:id/attempts`
- [x] If T-R4.19 is not merged when T-R4.4 merges, this is the first route in the new shell: it
      adds `web/tests/e2e/teacher-shell.spec.ts` at its own route (the sidebar expanded by
      default, collapsed and persisted, the drawer below 768, the toggle operable by keyboard)
      and carries the first deck comparison of the shell with a real page
- [x] States for the page and each tab: skeleton, empty roster ("No students yet"), error
- [ ] Deck check at the five widths, light and dark; keys vi first, listed in the PR

### T-R4.27a — New assignment wizard: frame, test and students
**Depends on:** T-R4.4, T-R4.5b, T-R4.2b, T-R4.2c
**Touches:** `web/src/features/assignments/pages/teacher/AssignmentWizardPage.tsx` (new, replaces `AssignmentFormPage.tsx`), `web/src/features/assignments/components/WizardStepper.tsx` (new), `web/tests/units/assignments/`, `web/tests/e2e/support/live.ts`, `web/tests/e2e/admin-change-requests.live.spec.ts`
**Reuses:** `TestVersionPicker`, `ClassTargetPicker`, `StudentTargetPicker`, `useTargetRoster`, `TargetsLine`, R1's `MarqueeText`, `FormDialog`
**Size:** M
**Done when:**
- [x] Header "New assignment" / "Nothing is sent until you press Assign. Defaults are the safe
      choice."; width 1080; `?step=` in the URL; `/teacher/assignments/:id/edit` opens a draft
      in the wizard
- [x] Stepper: Test, Students, Schedule, Rules (four columns, two on phones); any step can be
      opened; done steps show ✓; each value line marquees on overflow only and is static under
      reduced motion
- [x] Step 1 "Which test?": published tests as radio rows with a wired search and meta "{skill} ·
      {q} questions · {n} need manual grading"; `?test=` preselects
- [x] Step 2 "Who takes it?": class cards with student counts; "Add individual students" dialog
      (searchable, "· already included" meta); the individual panel marks "Already included
      through {class}"; the total line "{N} students will get this test · {c} from classes, {e}
      added one by one"; `?class=` preselects
- [x] `?test=` and `?class=`: today's names are `testId` and `classId`; the four link sites and
      their tests change in this task
- [x] "Save draft" on every step persists every field (DG-65) and goes to the Drafts tab with
      "Saved as draft. Students cannot see it."; Cancel on step 1, Back after; leaving with
      unsaved changes asks
- [x] Tests: dedupe count, preselection from the URL, draft round trip; keys vi first

**As built:**
- **Steps 1 and 2 only, as W3 decided.** Schedule and Rules are in the stepper with their value
  lines, but cannot be opened yet, and Continue on Students is disabled above the sentence
  "Schedule and rules arrive with the next update" (`assignments.wizard.laterSteps`).
  `/teacher/assignments/:id/edit` still opens `AssignmentFormPage`. T-R4.27b opens both steps,
  routes the edit address to the wizard and removes the sentence; it ticked the first two
  boxes.
- **The draft is one shape for both pages.** `features/assignments/draft.ts` holds
  `AssignmentDraft`, `emptyDraft`, `draftBody` and `draftOf`, which keeps the stored review
  policy whole (`release` and `showClassAverage` included). `usePreselect.ts` holds the two
  URL preselection hooks. `AssignmentFormPage` imports both instead of keeping private copies.
- **Every field leaves on Save draft.** The wizard holds a whole draft from the defaults, so a
  draft saved from step 1 or 2 carries the window, the time limit, the attempts, the review and
  the integrity policy (DG-65). Save draft needs a test, and says so in the footer when there is
  none.
- **`?test=` and `?class=` preselect without counting as a change**, so leaving straight away
  does not ask. The wizard still reads `testId` and `classId`, because an old
  `/admin/assignments/new?classId=` bookmark redirects with its query. The four link sites
  (`TestCard`, `ClassAssignmentsCard`, `ClassesListPage`, `ClassDetailPage`) write the new names.
- **Step 1 picks the current version.** Each row reads its test's versions (one request a row,
  shared with the old picker's cache key) for the question and manual-grading counts, and stays
  disabled until they arrive; a row whose versions fail says so and offers Retry (QA-27a-2).
  The meta describes only the version being picked: "{q} questions · {n} need manual grading".
  The meta omits skills until TestVersion carries them (F-43): `Test.skills` is the draft's,
  and no version answer carries skills (QA-27a-1). When the field lands, T-R4.27b puts it back
  in `versionMeta`.
- **Save draft posts once.** A ref is set before the request and cleared only on failure, so
  two clicks in one tick send one draft (QA-27a-3).
- **`useTargetRoster` also returns `fromClasses` and `classesOf`.** The total line is built from
  them, and so is the panel's "Already included through {class}", which names the first picked
  class, in the order picked, that already holds the student. The dialog's "· already included"
  comes from each `StudentRow.classes`. The dialog loads every active student, 100 a page, when
  it opens.
- **Leaving asks.** `useBlocker` is on any change of path except `/login`, and
  `useBeforeUnload` is on while there are unsaved choices. A step change is a search-parameter
  change and does not ask.
- **The live helpers go through the wizard.** `assignToClass` and the change-requests spec pick
  the test and the class in the wizard, save a draft and assign it from the edit form. T-R4.27b
  moves the Assign to the wizard's last step.
- **Tests:** `web/tests/units/assignments/wizard.test.tsx` (the header and stepper, preselection
  from the URL, the dedupe count and the panel, Save draft's body, the leave dialog, and the draft
  round trip). `target-roster.test.tsx` gained the two new fields.

**Depends on:** T-R4.27a, T-R4.7, T-R4.11
**Touches:** `web/src/features/assignments/pages/teacher/AssignmentWizardPage.tsx`, `web/src/features/assignments/components/{ScheduleStep,RulesStep}.tsx` (new), `web/src/features/assignments/{studentRules.ts,components/StudentRulesPreview.tsx,input.ts}`, `web/tests/units/assignments/student-rules.test.ts`
**Reuses:** `DateTimeField`, `NumberStepper`, segmented control, `StudentRulesPreview`, `studentRules.ts`
**Size:** M
**Done when:**
- [x] Step 3: Opens and Closes in HCM, as every teacher wall-clock input is until T-R4.43 (spec
      v0.57); the switch to the account's zone is T-R4.43's; Time limit 30 / 45 / 60 / Custom (±5 stepper,
      1–600); Attempts 1–99 with "One attempt, no retakes" or "The best score counts. Each
      attempt has its own time limit."

      correct answers, explanations — dimmed with "Turn on correct answers first" until correct
      answers are on); when results show (after submitting / after the window closes); class

      intro (T-R4.11; placed per its DG entry)
- [x] Integrity per D8: Require fullscreen; Block copy and paste; Leaving the test No limit /
      Not allowed / Set a limit (1–99, "times allowed"); When the limit is passed Warn / Flag
      for me / Auto-submit, dimmed under No limit with "Choose a limit first. Every time is
      still recorded."; Ignore short absences 0–30 s; the footnote "A browser cannot prevent
      cheating. These rules record what happened so you can decide." Mapped to
      `IntegrityPolicy` (no limit 0, not allowed −1, limit n)
- [x] Defaults come from `preferences.assignmentDefaults`, falling back to the deck: 45 min,
      shuffle off, show score on, block copy on, fullscreen off, no limit, flag, 3 s. Existing
      assignments keep their stored policy (70 §6)
- [x] Aside: summary lines, "What students will read" from `studentRules.ts` (vi first), and
      "About {m×n} answers to grade by hand" or "Everything is marked automatically."
- [x] The score sentence follows `review.release` (T-R4.11): with `after_close` it says the
      score is seen once the test has closed, never "after submitting". The preview and the
      student's Test intro share `studentRules.ts`, so both change; `student-rules.test.ts`
      runs both values (73-r3.md T-R3.8 left this here)
- [x] Final "Assign to {N} students" publishes and goes to the Scheduled tab with "Assigned.
      Students are notified when it opens."
- [x] The rest of T-R4.27a's first two boxes: `/teacher/assignments/:id/edit` opens a draft in
      the wizard (`AssignmentFormPage.tsx` is then left unrouted for T-R4.48), Schedule and Rules
      open from the stepper, and the sentence "Schedule and rules arrive with the next update"
      and its key go
- [x] Tests: integrity mapping, dependent dimming, defaults precedence, the student text for each
      combination; deck check at the five widths, light and dark

**As built:**
- **The wizard is the edit form too.** `/teacher/assignments/:id/edit` loads the assignment and
  its test's versions into the same wizard (skeleton, then the load error with Retry). A
  published assignment's header is "Edit assignment" with "Changes reach students as soon as you
  save.", it has no Save draft, and its last button is "Save changes", which saves in place and
  opens the detail page. A draft keeps Save draft and assigns like a new one.
  `AssignmentFormPage.tsx` is unrouted and left for T-R4.48, with `form-edit.test.tsx`.
- **The window has one zone.** `draft.ts` reads and writes it through `WINDOW_ZONE`
  (`windowInput`, `windowInstant`), which is `APP_TIME_ZONE` until T-R4.43 moves every teacher
  wall-clock input together; the stepper's date line uses the same zone. The preview keeps HCM
  through `previewRules` (now in `studentRules.ts`, shared by the aside and
  `StudentRulesPreview`).
- **Defaults.** `emptyDraft(now, defaults)` takes the five stored fields of
  `preferences.assignmentDefaults` (read from the auth store) and falls back to the deck's; the
  rest are the deck's fixed starting point. `draftOf` never reads the defaults, so a stored
  assignment keeps its policy.
- **Rules beyond the deck.** "When results show" (After submitting | After the window closes)
  and "Class average" (DG-137's conditions in its hint) sit in the "After submitting" group;
  "Note to students (optional)" is a plain field under the groups (DG-71) with a counter to
  500, counted before trimming, and an empty note is sent as `null`. Turning correct answers off
  also turns explanations off, so a stored policy never holds explanations without answers.
- **Leaving the test** maps through `leavingMode` and `leavingValue` (`wizardValues.ts`): no
  limit 0, not allowed −1, a limit n, and a new limit starts at 2. Under no limit the action's
  three options are disabled and the row is drawn at 45%.
- **Assign.** The last step's button is "Assign to {N} students" from the roster count, or
  "Assign" while it is counted. A missing test, no one picked or a window that does not close
  after it opens opens that step with the reason in the footer. A window that has already
  opened goes to the Open tab with "Assigned. Students can start now."; one still to open goes
  to Scheduled with the deck's sentence. Save draft and Assign share the one in-flight guard.
- **Live specs.** `assignToClass` goes through all four steps; the change-requests spec sets the
  custom time limit, the attempts and the integrity options in the wizard and reads them back
  from the edit address.
- **Tests:** `web/tests/units/assignments/wizard-rules.test.tsx` (the leaving mapping, defaults
  and their precedence, explanations waiting for answers, the action dimmed under no limit, the
  student text for release, fullscreen and score, assigning, the gap, editing a published
  assignment and a draft). The shared stubs are `web/tests/support/assignmentWizard.tsx`.
  `student-rules.test.ts` already runs both release values.

### T-R4.28 — Grading
**Depends on:** T-R4.4, T-R4.5b, T-R4.14
**Touches:** `web/src/features/attempts/pages/teacher/GradingPage.tsx` (new, replaces `GradingQueuePage.tsx` and `components/GradeByQuestion.tsx`), `web/src/features/attempts/components/GradingCard.tsx`, `web/tests/units/attempts/{by-question,shared-review}.test.tsx`
**Reuses:** `GradingCard`, `AnswerReview`, `gradeAttempt`, `finishGrading`
**Size:** M
**Done when:**
- [x] Header "Grading" / "{n} answers left from {m} students."; By student / By question in
      `?mode=`; `?assignment=` and `?student=` narrow the queue
- [x] Queue aside 300px (full width and 180px high on phones): avatars or "Q{n}" tiles,
      "{n} left" or "Done", the active row with the lime inset bar
- [x] Card: 3px progress bar, type pill and "Question {n} · {max} pt", prompt, the answer,
      Accepted chips when present, the sample answer for the teacher; score buttons from 0 to
      max in 0.5 steps with their key numbers when that is at most nine values, a number field
      otherwise
- [x] Picking a score saves at once; comment "Comment (optional, student sees it)" with four
      snippets per kind (vi first) that append; Previous and "Save & next" follow the queue
      grouping (DG-64); no score picked shows an error-tone toast "Pick a score first (keys
      1–{n})"
- [x] After a successful last manual save and confirmed `pendingManual = 0`, explicitly call
      `finishGrading` (DG-64). Grade success is not Finish success. Keep the known attempt and
      Retry Finish after failure, without replaying Grade. Do not infer an absent optional
      pending count is zero. Score-release wording requires actual Finish and existing policy.
- [x] After reload, recovery exhausts all public `listAttempts` pages separately for submitted
      and timed_out with `pendingGrading=false`, deduplicates attempt IDs, then applies the
      assignment/student filters and offers current review plus explicit Finish. These candidates
      also include auto-only or unanswered papers: never label all as interrupted grading or
      finish them unattended. OFFSET scans are complete for a stable finite fixture; concurrent
      changes can shift pages and require rescan. No new completion endpoint or write is needed.
- [x] Verify saved-mark preservation, Finish failure and Retry, reload discovery beyond the first
      100 candidates and within student/assignment filters, independent review visibility and
      explicit re-grading. Task14 read approval does not close these consumer recovery gates.
- [x] j / k move and 1…n pick, never inside an input or textarea; the footer reads "Answer {i}
      of {n} for {given name}" with the key hint on desktop
- [x] States: skeleton, "Nothing to grade" empty, error; deck check at the five widths, light and
      dark; keys vi first

**As built:**
- **Queue order is stable** (QA-416-2). The server returns pending answers only, so a graded
  answer would leave its list and fall behind its group. `mergeQueueOrder` keeps every answer
  where the queue first showed it and slots a newly returned one after the answer the server
  returned before it; the aside lists groups in that order, with the server's complete counts.
  Save & next therefore reaches the same student's next answer by student, and the next
  student on the same question by question, and the footer's "Answer {i} of {n}" counts in
  that order.
- **StrictMode** (QA-416-1). The request controller is created in the effect that aborts it,
  so StrictMode's simulated unmount no longer leaves a dead controller; the shared grading
  test mount renders under `<StrictMode>`.
- **Keyboard** (QA-416-3). The shortcuts listen on the document while the page is mounted, so
  a key works wherever focus is not in an input, a textarea, a select, an editable element, a
  dialog, a menu or a listbox, and no modifier or composition is active. When a save settles
  with focus dropped to the page, focus returns to the card's chosen score (or its first
  score, or the card).
- **Copy and geometry** (QA-416-4): a graded group reads "Done"; the footer reads "Answer {i}
  of {n} for {given name}"; a score button is named "Give 1 point" or "Give {n} points"
  (vi "Chấm {n} điểm"); score digits 15px at deck scale; the sub line 14px; the heading's
  line height 36px; the type pill a block 22.8px high; card and aside on the card shadow
  token; the by-question tile 8px; Next
  padding 16px. The undrawn "Full paper review" link is an icon button named by its label, so
  the card's footer keeps the deck's single row (63px) from 768 to 1440. The filter selects are
  gone: a narrowed queue shows a removable chip (DG-136).
- **Concurrent grading is last write wins** (T-11). Grading is last-write-wins in R4. The
  second write replaces the score, the comment, `graded_by` and `graded_at`. Grading is not
  audited (spec §13.4 does not require it). The contract gets no concurrency token in R4. The
  grading queue refetches whenever its window regains focus (`refetchOnWindowFocus:
  "always"`), and the open card shows the refetched score and comment unless it holds an
  unsaved edit of its own. The queue holds only ungraded answers, so an answer graded in another tab
  leaves it; the open answer is kept on screen when that happens, and its score and comment
  are read from the attempt's review after each queue refetch (VER-416b, QA-416b-4).
- **Where Save & next and Finish go** (QA-416b-2). Both continue to the next ungraded answer
  after the current one in the mode's order, wrapping round (`nextOpenItem`); Finish skips the
  finished paper. By question, Finish on a student's last answer goes to the next student's
  ungraded answer, not to an answer already graded in this session.
- **Removing a filter chip** (QA-416b-3) navigates with `state.focus`, and the remounted page
  puts focus on the heading (`tabIndex=-1`).
- **Navigation guard** (REV-416, R416-1). A score is written on the click, but a comment only by
  the flush that `leave` runs. `useGradingQueue` reports `unsettled` while the selected answer's
  trimmed comment differs from its acknowledged one (or the queue's), or a comment has no score.
  While it is set, `useBlocker` stops any navigation that changes the path or the query and asks
  "Rời trang khi nhận xét chưa lưu?" with "Lưu và rời đi" (`leave`, which saves, then proceeds) or
  "Ở lại"; with no score yet the dialog shows "pick a score first" and stays. The page's own
  navigations (filter chips, the mode toggle, "Full paper review") go through `leave` and are
  let through, by a flag that lasts for that one navigation: it resets whenever the location
  changes, and a chip or toggle whose target is the current query does not navigate (R416-6;
  before, clicking the pressed "By student" left the flag set and the guard off). `beforeunload` warns while it is set. The j and k keys compare
  `event.key.toLowerCase()`, so Caps Lock moves too (R416-3).
- **Retired** (R416-2): `pages/teacher/GradingQueuePage.tsx`, which nothing imported; the route
  is `GradingPage`.
- **Deck geometry, VER-416b** (QA-416b-5): the card head's student name 14px/21px; the
  "Question", "Score" and "Comment" labels 18px high, the Comment label a full-width block; 8px
  between a score's digit and its key; the heading's letter spacing -0.02em (-0.48px). The
  tester's `matrix416b` shows none of the five at any of the 18 boards.
- **Tests:** `grading-order-keys.test.tsx` (StrictMode Grade, order by student and by question,
  Finish by question onto the next ungraded answer, focus on the heading after a chip is removed,
  a queue refetch on every refocus inside the stale time, another tab's grade on the open answer,
  keyboard-only 2 → j → 1, keys inside the comment and the number field, nine buttons up to four
  points and a number field above), `grading-recovery.test.ts` (`mergeQueueOrder`,
  `nextOpenItem`, `scoreOptions`), `grading-messages.test.ts` (the plural names), and the existing
  `grading-finish-recovery.test.tsx`, now under StrictMode.
- **Deck check** with the tester's matrix (`matrix416.mjs`) against the deck's Grading screen at
  360, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440, light and dark, sidebar expanded, collapsed and as a drawer:
  the header, sub line, toggle, aside, rows, card, type pill, score buttons, Next and the
  footer match in size, type and computed colour, apart from data (the seed's question numbers
  and answer lengths). Small differences remain: the 3px progress bar sits above the card's
  border, so it is 2px wider than the deck's, drawn inside it; the card's head row is 1.5px
  lower; and the outline buttons (scores, Previous) carry the button primitive's 1px shadow,
  which the deck does not draw (R5 sets the primitives' deck defaults).

### T-R4.29 — Tests list
**Depends on:** T-R4.4, T-R4.5b, T-R4.1c, T-R4.2a, T-R4.15, T-R4.16
**Touches:** `web/src/features/tests/pages/teacher/TestsListPage.tsx`, `web/tests/units/tests/`, `web/tests/units/imports/i18n.test.ts`
**Reuses:** `CardGrid`, `StatusBadge`, `TestStatusFacets`, `RowMenu`, `DeleteItemButton`, `features/imports/availability.ts`
**Size:** S
**As built (T-R4.29: tests list):**
- **Touched beyond the list:** `features/tests/pages/teacher/TestCard.tsx` (new; in the pages
  folder because `features/tests/components/` is lane 1's), the tests route line in
  `app/router.tsx`, which now declares a teacher handle (crumb `teacherShell.nav.tests`, width
  1320) so the page renders in `TeacherLayout` and follows the theme,
  `components/shared/DeleteItemButton.tsx` (below), the `tests` block of both locales,
  `docs/design/gaps.md` (DG-140) and `tests/e2e/admin-change-requests.live.spec.ts`, whose list
  half drove the old table. `tests/units/tests/list-rows.test.tsx` became `list-cards.test.tsx`.
  `router-chunks.test.ts` is unedited.
- **Spec §8's approved list change request (2026-09-22) wins over the deck here** (DG-140): a
  card has a selection checkbox before its pill, selecting shows the deck's bulk bar (Archive
  selected, Delete permanently), and Duplicate sits beside the "…" menu as a 26px button in the
  place of the deck's Share. Duplicating keeps the teacher on the list and marks the source and
  the copy "Just duplicated" until they leave.
- **The whole card opens the test's detail, for every status,** as the deck's card does; a draft
  no longer opens the builder from the list (New test and the detail's Open builder do). The
  card is a stretched link from its title; the checkbox and the footer's buttons sit above it.
- **The menu** holds Duplicate and Archive, or for an archived test Restore, Duplicate and Delete
  permanently (`deleteTest` takes only archived tests). A published test's menu also starts with
  "Assign to a class" (the wizard's `?testId=`), until T-R4.30 gives the detail its Assign
  button; 30 decides whether it stays. Delete opens `DeleteItemDialog`, split out of
  `DeleteItemButton`, which now composes it and behaves as before. A dialog opened from the menu
  gives focus back to its trigger, or after a delete (and an archive in a filtered tab) to the
  neighbouring card's trigger, else to New test.
- **The pill** is `StatusBadge` with the deck's dot and tones (Published success-soft, Draft
  transparent with a border-coloured dot, Archived the deck's "closed" muted tone), no border.
  **The skills** are `Test.skills` as chips with no remove button, labelled by `tests.skill.*`.
  **{assigned}** follows the deck's four forms in this order: "{n} live" while any is open,
  "Scheduled", "{n} closed", "Not assigned". Points are formatted in the locale ("12,5 điểm").
- **Tabs and search:** `Segmented` with the response's facets; `?status=published|draft` and
  `?q=` through `useListFilters`. The Archived tab is gone (archived tests are under All), and
  an old `?status=archived` reads as All. 24 tests a page with the `Pager`, as Media.
- **Not carried over:** the tag filter (the deck draws none, and the card shows skills, not tags;
  `listTests` keeps its `tag` parameter), the headphone count, the per-row Edit, "View as a
  student" and "Version history" items (the detail has them), and the header's "{n} tests · {n}
  drafts" line, which the deck's sub replaces. Mutation errors are toasts. Eleven `tests.*` keys
  that nothing reads any more were removed, in their own commit.
- **Deck check** (1440, 1280, 1024, 768 and 360, light and dark, sidebar expanded, collapsed and
  as a drawer), against the deck's `TESTS` as route stubs: geometry, type and computed colours
  match apart from DG-122 (every block 6px higher), the content the plan changes (points for the
  duration, the derived skills, Duplicate and "…" for Share, which lets a footer fit on one row
  at 1440 and makes "18 points" wrap where "25 min" did not at 1280) and `formatRelative`, which
  the rebuilt screens share: "3 days ago" and, past six days, a date, where the deck writes
  "3 d ago" and "1 w ago".
**Done when:**
- [x] Header "Tests" / "Build once, assign to any class."; ghost Imports, outline "Import from

- [x] Cards `minmax(290px,1fr)`: status, updated, title, derived skill chips, footer "{n}

### T-R4.30 — Test detail and versions
**Depends on:** T-R4.29, T-R4.16
**Touches:** `web/src/features/tests/pages/teacher/TestDetailPage.tsx`, `web/src/features/tests/components/{VersionHistory,VersionChanges}.tsx` (new), `web/src/features/tests/components/StudentPreview.tsx`, `web/tests/units/tests/`
**Reuses:** `StudentPreview` (computer and phone), `Sheet`, `FormDialog`, the existing version mutations
**Size:** M
**As built (2026-10-10):**
- **The route has a teacher handle** (Tests › the test's title, 1320px), so the detail renders in
  `TeacherLayout` and follows the theme; the builder's `#versions` link opens the history sheet
  below the two columns, as `?history=1` does.
- **Layout.** `useContentWidthAtLeast(1000)` picks the two columns (`minmax(0,1fr) 340px`, the
  history a sticky aside) or one column with "Version history" in the header and a 380px `Sheet`.
  At the five widths, both sidebar states and both themes, the grid's columns, the heading's place
  and the banner's height measure as the deck's.
- **The address** holds `?version=` (a version not in the list falls back to the default),
  `compare=1`, `device=phone` and `history=1`, each written with `replace`; choosing a version
  clears `compare` and closes the sheet, as the deck's `select` does.
- **Assign** links to `/teacher/assignments/new?test={id}` and is off while the test has no version
  or is archived. **The tests list menu loses "Assign to a class"** (T-R4.29 left that to 30), so
  assigning starts from the detail or from the wizard.
- **Publish** opens a `FormDialog` with the deck's title and body (how many assignments keep the
  default version) and an optional change note, trimmed and sent only when not blank. The next
  number is the highest version listed plus one: `Test` does not expose
  `last_published_version`, so after the newest version is deleted the banner names a number the
  server will skip. `PUBLISH_VALIDATION_FAILED` reports how many problems to fix in the builder.
- **Diffs.** `getVersionDiff(id, n, previous)` names the previous version by number, so its query
  key is immutable and cached for good. The compare band and the computed summary of a card with no
  change note share it; the first version reads "First version". `previewMarks` marks added
  questions "New" and changed or re-keyed ones "Changed" through `StudentPreview`'s new `marks`.
- **Departures** are DG-145: the preview is the student's paper, the band lists Removed rows,
  Assign is off when archived, and the off Restore and Delete stay focusable with their titles.
- **Keys** are the new `tests.detail.*` block; the old detail's `tests.*` keys and
  `tests.assignToClass` are removed in their own commit. Two live specs and `group-preview.spec.ts`
  follow the new labels and the 390px frame.
**Done when:**

      Version history (narrow only, a 380px sheet), Open builder, primary Assign (disabled
      without a version; → wizard `?test=`)
- [x] Draft banner when `unpublishedChanges > 0`: "The draft has changes that are not
      published…", Review draft, "Publish version {n}" with the confirm naming how many

- [x] Two columns `minmax(0,1fr) 340px` from 1000 content width; the history as a sticky aside

- [x] History: the draft row; version cards (Version n, Default, "{q} q · {pts} pts", date and

      unknown tag, `del`, `h4`, an `http:` link. The image case in `clipboard.test.ts` moves
      from the refused list to a case that asserts the count and that no image node or address
      survives. A paste with nothing left once its images are out opens no dialog and shows the
      file notice. A paste whose HTML holds text is previewed whatever files ride along, and a
      plain paste beside a file inserts its text; only a file with no HTML text is refused.
      Default, not confirmed by Thuong; the alternative keeps the refusal and does
      not build the left-out line (open item)
- [x] `readOnly` is a new prop: it renders `ContentView`, with no toolbar and no footer. The
      review, which mounts `ContentView` itself today, uses it (T-R4.38); R7's Can-use builder
      will
- [x] The editor stays a lazy chunk with today's skeleton; `router-chunks.test.ts` green and
      untouched
- [x] Tests: `content/content.test.tsx` covers the toolbar's order for each profile, the link
      popover (applied, refused, removed), the table row and its disabled titles, the words
      count and the read view; `content/paste-editor.test.ts` covers the notice band, its
      dismissal, the left-out line and the paths that open no dialog;
      `content-editor.spike.ts` green under `pnpm e2e:content`, and
      `rich-question-content.spec.ts` green under `pnpm e2e`
- [x] Deck check at the five widths, light and dark; keys vi first, en from the deck, listed in
      the PR

**As built:**
- **Files beyond "Touches":** `editor/{ToolButton,LinkPanel,EditorFooter}.tsx`,
  `editor/{roving,shortcuts,clipboardText}.ts` (new);
  `features/question-groups/components/MaterialTools.tsx` (its own Link and Remove-link
  controls and their dialog are gone, with the keys `groups.{link,removeLink,linkHint,linkURL,
  linkHTTPS}`, so the toolbar's popover is the one way to link in the group material editor;
  Tech Lead's decision); `tests/support/deck-harness/cases/r4-63.tsx`; spec §7.1 and v0.61
  (the image exception, brought forward from T-R4.50). `GroupComposer.tsx`, `MaterialContent.tsx`,
  `OptionField`, `RichBlankEditor`, `RichProseEditor` and the review's `QuestionEditor` are not
  edited: the profile defaults carry the frame.
- **Props.** `ContentEditor` adds `minHeight`, `fontSize`, `footer`, `placeholder`, `fileNotice`
  and `readOnly`; `tools` stays. Defaults by profile: `prompt` 96/15, `question` 84/14,
  `document` 256 (today's height) at the inherited size, all three with the footer; `option`
  keeps today's box, no footer. So until T-R4.64 passes props, a non-blank prompt (profile
  `question`) draws at 84/14. Height and size are CSS variables on the box
  (`--content-editor-min-height`, `--content-editor-font-size`) read by `.content-editor-body`.
  The file notice is a notice kind, `"file"`, resolved from the `fileNotice` prop at render, so
  the extensions built once in `useState` never hold a stale string; with no prop it says
  `contentEditor.fileNotice`. A document its profile cannot hold draws the box with the
  `invalidContent` notice and no editor.
- **Toolbar.** `role="toolbar"` named "{label} formatting"; one tab stop (`useRovingToolbar`:
  Left, Right, Home, End over the enabled buttons, wrapping); `aria-label` is the plain label,
  `title` adds the shortcut as the deck writes it, and `aria-keyshortcuts` names it (⌘ and
  Meta on Apple devices, from TipTap's `isMacOS`/`isiOS`), approved by the Tech Lead so the
  specs that find "Hoàn tác" by name stay untouched. Pointer and mouse presses are
  `preventDefault`ed. "Heading" sets level 3, or a paragraph when the caret is in any heading.
  The `option` profile keeps the marks and the history only.
- **Link popover.** `LinkPopover` is the button; `LinkPanel`, the modal Radix popover anchored
  to it by `virtualRef`, loads on first use, because Radix Popover and floating-ui took the
  spike's editor chunk past `bundle.test.ts`'s 160 KiB (to 166 KiB gzip); it is 156 KiB with
  the panel split out; the 4 KiB left under the budget is T-R4.64's to watch. The draft starts
  at the link's address or "https://", and the panel takes Radix's open focus itself to put the
  caret after it: Radix selects the field's text on open, so a typed host replaced "https://"
  and every bare address was refused (review F1). The input is `type="text"` with
  `inputMode="url"`, Enter submits the form. The button is a dialog opener only
  (`aria-haspopup`, `aria-expanded`); its look inside a link comes from `data-active`, not
  `aria-pressed` (F3).
- **Words count.** The footer selects the document node by reference, defers it, and builds the
  text and the count in one `useMemo`, so neither runs on the keystroke path (F2). The paste
  notice and the footer name the same platform shortcut through `{{shortcut}}` (F4).
- **Paste (DG-115).** `clipboardHTML` returns `{ content, imagesLeftOut } | null`; `inspect`
  covers a media element's whole source range, splices it from its parent and skips its
  children; `checkElement` is unchanged. With images out and no text and no table, `content` is
  null. The extension sends HTML that shows text (`clipboardHasText`, a linear scan without a
  parser that skips tags, comments and the contents of `style`, `script` and `title`) to the
  preview whatever files ride along; plain text with no such HTML is inserted
  through `view.pasteText`; a file or an HTML image with no text gets the file notice and no
  dialog. A refused conversion closes the preview and reports in the band.
- **Not built:** the deck's four file notices and its drop overlay (DG-109); the builder's and
  the bank's "Question media" file notice is their host's string (T-R4.64, T-R4.66).
- **Deck check** through the harness case against the deck served on 5185 (React, Babel and
  lucide served from the npm registry's tarballs of the deck's pinned versions, because the
  container cannot reach unpkg): the box, the toolbar, its buttons and separators, the editable
  area, the placeholder, the footer, the table row, the link popover and the notice band match
  in size and computed colour at 360, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440, light and dark. The words
  count uses English plurals ("1 word"); the deck writes "words" always.
- **Lesson: the task should have been two PRs.** About 1,110 changed lines of code and tests is
  over the ~800 that 70 §3 sets for one review, and the seam was clear: the paste half
  (`clipboardHTML`, `clipboardText`, the paste paths in `extensions` and `useContentPaste`, the
  left-out line, spec §7.1) could have shipped before the frame, toolbar, link and footer.
  Review approved it whole rather than splitting it after the fact.

### T-R4.64 — Content editor: the Rich text and Markdown modes
**Depends on:** T-R4.63
**Touches:** `web/src/features/question-bank/components/{RichProseEditor,QuestionProseField,BlankPromptField,PromptField}.tsx`, `web/src/components/shared/content/editor/markdown.ts`, `web/src/components/shared/content/editor/MarkdownToolbar.tsx` (new), `web/src/components/shared/Markdown.tsx`, `web/package.json`, `.env.example`, `web/playwright.config.ts`, `web/tests/units/content/`, `web/tests/units/question-bank/prompt-preview.test.tsx`, `web/tests/e2e/{rich-question-content,rich-blanks}.spec.ts`
**Reuses:** `RichProseEditor`'s conversion preview, `markdownToQuestionContent`, `Markdown` (the reader students get), `Segmented` (T-R4.2)
**Size:** M
**Delivered in parts:** four, each one branch and one pull request, each cut from the head of the one before (the mode work came to ~2,150 lines, so it was split three ways)
**T-R4.64a:** the Markdown engine: the GFM subset plugin, the parser's strikethrough and tables, the serializer, the reader's tables and strikethrough. Its boxes: "Markdown gains tables and strikethrough" and "`Markdown.tsx` is on the student's path"; of the tests box, the round trip and the rendering
**T-R4.64b:** the Markdown body: the toolbar, "Write | Preview", the textarea, the preview and the footer, and the Segmented's disabled option. Its boxes: "Markdown toolbar" and "Body"
**T-R4.64c:** the modes of a prompt and an explanation: the header's Segmented, both conversions in place, the always-live rich body, `question-prose-field.test.tsx` and `rich-question-content.spec.ts`. Its boxes: both conversions and the serializer's use; the header box is met except for the builder's and the group composer's starter prompt (R64c-1), so it stays open; of the always-live box, the prose fields
**T-R4.64d:** the fill-in-the-blank prompt (the gaps rule), the removal of `VITE_RICH_QUESTION_EDITOR` and the view-then-edit flow, `rich-blanks.spec.ts`, the deck check of the whole field, and the keys. Every other box
**Done when:**
- [ ] The field's header: its label, and on the right "Rich text | Markdown" with the deck's

- [x] The editor is always live. Today's view-then-edit flow ("Format: {field}", "Edit:
      {field}", "Done") and `VITE_RICH_QUESTION_EDITOR` go, from the code, from
      `.env.example` and from `web/playwright.config.ts`. `VITE_RICH_OPTION_EDITOR` is

- [x] Markdown to Rich text shows the result in place of the body: "Review the conversion below

      Markdown
- [x] Markdown toolbar: "Bold · \*\*text\*\*", "Italic · \*text\*", "Strikethrough · ~~text~~";
      "Heading · ### "; "Bulleted list · - ", "Numbered list · 1. "; "Link ·

- [x] Markdown gains tables and strikethrough, in `markdown.ts` and in `Markdown.tsx`, through

- [x] `Markdown.tsx` is on the student's path: the take-test unit tests run before and after

- [x] Tests: `content/question-prose-field.test.tsx` covers a field opening in its stored form,
      both conversions and both ways back out, and the disabled switch while gaps exist; a
      round-trip test takes a document holding every kept construct to Markdown and back and
      gets the same document; a table and `~~text~~` render in `Markdown`;
      `rich-question-content.spec.ts` and `rich-blanks.spec.ts` are rewritten to the
      always-live editor and the two-way switch, and are green under `pnpm e2e`; no case is
