- [x] Deck check at the five widths, light and dark; keys vi first, en from the deck, listed in
      the PR
**As built (T-R4.64a: the Markdown engine):**
- **One plugin, four packages.** `components/shared/content/markdownGfm.ts` exports `gfmSubset`,
  which registers the micromark syntax and the mdast conversion for tables and for
  strikethrough, and nothing else of GFM: no autolinked addresses, footnotes or task lists, and
  a single `~` is text (`singleTilde: false`). `markdown.ts`'s parser and `Markdown.tsx` both
  use it, so the editor and the student's reader read the same syntax. The packages are pinned
  exact: `micromark-extension-gfm-table@2.1.2`, `micromark-extension-gfm-strikethrough@2.1.0`,
  `mdast-util-gfm-table@2.0.0`, `mdast-util-gfm-strikethrough@2.0.0` (2.0.1 was under two
  weeks old). `remark-gfm` was not taken: it brings autolinks, footnotes and task lists with no
  way to leave them out. `markdown-table` arrives through `mdast-util-gfm-table`.
- **Parser.** `~~text~~` becomes the `strike` mark. A GFM table becomes a `table` block whose
  first row is the header, each cell one paragraph; a table with any column alignment is
  refused, since the model has no alignment. A run's marks come out in the model's order
  (bold, italic, strike) however the delimiters nest.
- **Serializer.** `editor/markdownSerializer.ts`, re-exported from `markdown.ts` as
  `questionContentToMarkdown`, writes text, bold (`**`), italic (`*`), strikethrough (`~~`),
  headings, lists, tables and links, and drops underline, superscript and subscript, keeping
  their text. Adjacent runs share their delimiters; a run's edge spaces sit outside them. It
  escapes the characters Markdown reads in text, guards a line start that would open a block
  (`#`, `>`, `-`, `+`, `=`, `|`, `1.`) and writes a line's edge spaces as character references;
  a hard break is a backslash. A list right after a list of the same kind takes the other
  marker (`*`, `1)`) so the two stay two. Lossy by design, and the confirmation keeps the deck's
  sentence, which does not name these: a merged table cell is written once and the cells it covered stay empty, the first
  row becomes the header, and a cell's paragraphs join on one line; an empty paragraph outside
  a table is not kept.
- **Reader.** `Markdown.tsx` draws a table as a horizontally scrolling block with bordered
  cells and a muted header row, from tokens. The take-test units and the four canaries ran at
  the branch point and at the head, green.
- **The budget measure was corrected first** (Tech Lead's decision, T-16). `bundle.test.ts`
  builds from inside vitest, where NODE_ENV is `test`, so Vite resolved the `development`
  export condition and counted micromark's development builds, assertions included: code no
  student downloads. The test now names the production conditions in `resolve.conditions`;
  `mode: "production"` alone did not change the resolution. The limits stay at 220, 160 and
  64 KiB. Lowering each limit just under its measured size fails the test, so it still bites.
- **Bundles**, gzip KiB, under the production measure:

  | Budget | Limit | Before (4257310f) | After 64a |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Reader closure | 220 | 212.6 | 215.3 |
  | Editor minus what the reader shares | 160 | 157.0 | 157.0 |
  | Clipboard minus the initial chunks | 64 | 62.4 | 62.4 |

  Under the old measure the reader was 219.9 before and 222.8 after, which broke 220; the
  editor was 157.2 and the clipboard 62.4, both before and after.
- **English strings load on demand (F-20, 2026-10-10).** `en` is a separate chunk fetched on
  the first switch to English, or before the first render on a cold English boot, so new keys no
  longer grow the reader (181.9 KiB after, 37.7 for the en chunk). The reader and clipboard
  budgets leave that chunk out, and `bundle.test.ts` pins that no chunk they count holds an
  English string.
- **QA-63-2's two tests** ride here: the words count over runs of spaces, line breaks and
  no-break spaces (`content.test.tsx`), and Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+V making the next paste ignore its
  HTML (`paste-editor.test.ts`).

**As built (T-R4.64b: the Markdown body):**
- **Where it lives.** `MarkdownToolbar.tsx`, `MarkdownProseEditor.tsx` and `markdownActions.ts`
  are in `features/question-bank/` (the W0 approach, so nothing new reaches the entry chunk
  through `components/shared`). The box shares `EDITOR_BOX` and `frameStyle` with the rich
  editor through `editor/frame.ts`, so it does not import TipTap's chunk; the textarea takes
  the rich box's focus halo (`.content-markdown:focus` in `content.css`).
- **Toolbar.** Bold, Italic, Strikethrough | Heading | Bulleted, Numbered | Link, Insert table,
  in the deck's order, each named by its plain label with the deck's syntax as its `title`;
  `ToolButton` and `useRovingToolbar` from T-R4.63, so it is one tab stop with arrow keys. An
  action wraps the selection or the deck's placeholder ("bold text", "italic text", "text",
  "link text") and selects it, prefixes the caret's line ("### ", "- ", "1. "), or inserts the
  deck's two-column table on lines of its own; the deck appends two blank lines after a
  table at the end of the text, the body does not. An action taken in Preview returns to
  Write and acts at the end of the text, since Preview has no selection, and selects what it
  added there. "Heading" prefixes "### " every time it is pressed, so a second press writes
  "### ### "; the rich toolbar's "Heading" toggles back to a paragraph. This is a known
  difference (review R64b-1), left as the deck's `mdLine` draws it.
- **Write | Preview** is a tab pair (`role="tablist"`, `aria-selected`, roving with the arrow
  keys) beside the toolbar rather than inside its scroller; both sit in one 43px row as the
  deck draws them. The panel is the textarea (monospace 13.5px, line height 1.65, padding
  12px 16px, the field's minimum height, resized vertically) or the preview through `Markdown`
  at the field's text size, with `blankSlots` for a fill-in-the-blank prompt; an empty preview
  says "Nothing to preview yet."
- **Footer.** "Markdown · \*\*bold\*\*, \*italic\*, ### heading, - list, [link](https://…)" and
  the words count, which counts as the deck does (syntax characters read as spaces) on a
  deferred value.
- **Segmented** takes `disabled` and `describedBy` per option, for T-R4.64d's gaps rule; an
  enabled option's classes are unchanged.
- **Deck check** through the harness case `r4-64` against the deck's Markdown mode, at 360,
  768, 1024, 1280 and 1440, light and dark, for the prompt (96px, 15px) and the explanation
  (84px, 14px): the box, the toolbar row, its buttons and separators, the "Write | Preview"
  track and tabs, the textarea, the preview and the footer match in size and computed colour.
  The deck's toolbar also has "Image" and "Audio" (DG-109), and its footer hint is longer by
  `![](media:file)`, so at 768 the footer wraps to two lines where the deck's wraps to three.

**As built (T-R4.64c: the modes of a prompt and an explanation):**
- **The mode is the stored form** (T-17, a). `QuestionProseField` opens a field with a document
  in the rich editor and a field with Markdown text in the Markdown body; a field with no text
  opens as Rich text, and its first edit stores a document without asking, since there is
  nothing to convert. The builder's starter prompt is stored Markdown, so a new builder
  question, and a new question in a group, open as Markdown until T-R4.31b and T-R4.34 create
  them as rich text. `useProseMode`
  (`features/question-bank/proseMode.ts`) holds the mode and the confirmation the field is in;
  `ask` opens a confirmation and never changes the mode, so the Segmented keeps showing the
  stored form until a confirmation is accepted. Each body is keyed by its mode, so a switch
  starts a new history.
- **Exactly two calls change whether content is stored.** "Switch to Markdown" calls
  `onChange(questionContentToMarkdown(content), null)`; for a field that never stored a
  document it only changes the mode. "Apply conversion" calls `onChange(plainText(document),
  document)`. A unit test edits in each mode and finds every other call keeps the field's form.
- **Header** (`ProseModeHeader`): the label (and the explanation's hint, moved out of
  `QuestionEditor`) and "Rich text | Markdown" as the Segmented at `xs` with the deck's icons
  (`Type`, `FileCode`), named "{label} editing mode". The fill-in-the-blank prompt keeps
  `QuestionEditor`'s label and today's flow until T-R4.64d.
- **"Switch to Markdown"** (`SwitchToMarkdown`) is the `alertdialog` under the toolbar on the
  warning tone, passed through `ContentEditor`'s `tools` slot. Its sentence is the deck's
  without "images and audio" (DG-109) and without "and gaps". "Cancel" takes focus when it
  opens; Escape and "Cancel" close it and return focus to "Markdown".
- **"Apply conversion"** (`ConversionPanel`) replaces the Markdown body under its toolbar, which
  is disabled meanwhile, and hides "Write | Preview": the info notice, the converted content
  through `ContentView` (at most 320px, scrolling), "Keep Markdown" and "Apply conversion". A
  Markdown the converter refuses shows today's message and "Keep Markdown" only. After either
  switch the new body takes focus.
- **The rich body** (`RichProseEditor`) is always live, at the field's frame: 96px and 15px for
  a prompt, 84px and 14px for an explanation, as the deck draws them.
- **Two live toolbars on one page.** An empty explanation now opens the rich editor, so the
  question editor holds two formatting toolbars (three with a rich option open);
  `rich-question-content`, `rich-blanks` and `rich-options` find a toolbar button inside its
  toolbar, by the toolbar's name.
- **Keys:** `proseMode.*`; `questionEditor.applyProseConversion` now reads "Apply conversion"
  (vi "Áp dụng chuyển đổi"), the deck's label.
- **Deck check** through the harness cases `r4-64/prose-rich` and `prose-markdown` against the
  deck's Prompt field, at 360, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440, light and dark: the header, its label,
  the track, both modes and their icons, the rich body, the "Switch to Markdown" band, its
  sentence and buttons, and the conversion panel (notice, icon, content box, buttons) match in
  size and computed colour. The band is one line shorter where the deck's sentence wraps,
  because ours leaves out "images and audio" and "gaps".

**As built (T-R4.64d: the fill-in-the-blank prompt, the flag and the docs):**
- **The blank prompt's two modes.** `BlankPromptField` uses the same header, `useProseMode`,
  `SwitchToMarkdown` and `ConversionPanel` as a prose field, opening in its stored form. While
  the rich prompt holds a gap, "Markdown" is disabled and the line under the header says why
  ("Remove the gaps to switch to Markdown: the gaps hold the accepted answers."), named by the
  button's `aria-describedby`. With no gap left, "Switch to Markdown" writes the serializer's
  Markdown, stores no content and unbinds the answers it kept (`gapId: null`), as the old
  "Convert to Markdown" did. "Apply conversion" binds each `{{n}}` to a new gap with
  `bindLegacyBlanks` and stores the document and the blanks together; a Markdown the converter
  refuses names the reason (today's two messages, unsupported Markdown or unmatched markers).
  The Markdown body previews the `{{n}}` placeholders as the student's slots (`blankSlots`).
  `RichBlankEditor` is always live and keeps its answer bindings as before.
- **Gone:** `VITE_RICH_QUESTION_EDITOR` (code, `.env.example`, `playwright.config.ts`), the
  view-then-edit flow ("Format: {field}", "Edit: {field}", "Done" for prompts and
  explanations; options keep theirs), `PromptField` and its test, whose starter-prompt case
  `markdown-body.test.tsx` carries, and the keys `questionEditor.{bold, italic, list, link,
  markdown, formatProse, editProse, returnToMarkdown, removeProseWarning,
  confirmRemoveProse}`. Spec §7.1 (v0.62), plans 17 and 19 and the open item say how a field is
  written now.
- **The textarea's name** is the field's label (T-17 b, DG-138), so the specs that find "Nội
  dung câu hỏi" by label are unchanged. The bank's new fill-in-the-blank test switches to
  Markdown before typing `{{n}}`, since a new prompt is rich text.
- **What the converter refuses** stays refused, with today's message: an aligned GFM table
  (`| :-: |`) renders for the student, since the reader reads it, but "Apply conversion"
  refuses it, because the model has no alignment; so do raw HTML, code, images, quotations and
  links that are not HTTPS. `ConversionPanel` takes the refusal's sentence from `convert`'s
  own result, computed once when the panel opens (R64d-3).
- **Removed after review:** `blankMarkdownProjection`, which had no caller once "Switch to
  Markdown" required no gaps (R64d-1).
- **Deck check** through `r4-64/blank-gaps` and `blank-markdown` against the deck's Prompt field
  at the five widths, light and dark: the track, both modes and the rich body match; the deck
  draws no disabled "Markdown" and no reason line, which are DG-110's. The Markdown preview
  draws one slot per placeholder at every width.

### T-R4.65 — Question media block and "Choose from Media"
**Depends on:** T-R4.2b, T-R4.2c, T-R4.17a, T-R4.35, T-R4.62, T-R4.66
**Touches:** `web/src/features/question-bank/components/{QuestionMediaField,AudioPolicyPanel}.tsx`, `web/src/features/question-bank/components/QuestionEditor.tsx` (the block's place only; T-R4.66 owns the answer areas), `web/src/features/question-bank/{audioPolicy.ts,questionSchema.ts}`, `web/src/features/media/components/AssetLibraryDialog.tsx`, `web/src/features/media/api.ts`, `web/tests/units/question-bank/editor-audio-expiry.test.tsx`, `web/tests/units/media/file-drop.test.tsx`, `web/tests/units/builder/narrow-settings.test.tsx`
**Reuses:** `QuestionMediaField`, `AudioPolicyPanel`, `AssetLibraryDialog`, `AudioPlayer`, `useFileDrop`, `useMediaUpload` (T-R4.35), `probe.ts`, `limits.ts` and the card's thumbnail (T-R4.35), `Segmented`, R1's Toaster
**Size:** M
**As built (2026-10-10):**

- **One block, both pages.** `QuestionMediaField` is the whole block and `QuestionEditor` draws
  it after the answer area and before the explanation, so the builder's editor column and the
  bank's Question editor show it in the same place (the W2 approach's ordered block list). The
  settings rail and its sheet hold points and tags only; the bank's old MEDIA section is gone,
  which is where this task's placement line differs from what was built: the bank keeps no
  aside copy, because DG-108 builds the builder's drawing of the question media on both pages.
  `narrow-settings.test.tsx` now also
  pins that the sheet holds no media.
- **Upload.** A hidden `FileInput` (`features/media/components/FileInput.tsx`) named "Chọn tệp
  từ máy" and `openFilePicker` (`features/media/filePicker.ts`), which sets `accept` before the
  click: either kind from "Choose file" and from "Upload new" on All, the attached kind from
  "Replace" and from "Upload new" on Images or Audio. A file's kind comes from its extension
  (`kindOfName`); a file of neither kind is refused before the hook runs. `useMediaUpload`
  gains `onRejected`: with it, a pre-check refusal is an error toast whose title is the
  deck's words with the decided limit ("Images can be up to 10 MB", "Audio can be up to
  50 MB", "Audio can be up to 5 minutes", "Choose an audio or image file") and whose
  description is today's message naming the file, and the block returns to its drop zone. The
  hook's checking state now carries the size and kind, so the block shows "{size} · checking
  length…" while an audio file's metadata is read; progress, cancel and failure are
  `UploadStatus` as before. A success toasts "Saved to Media · {name}". A window drop works
  as before, attached or not.
- **Attached card.** Icon tile in the info tone, name, `assetMeta` ("{m:ss} · {size}", "{w} ×
  {h} · {size}", the image's size from the library row or, after an upload, from the
  preview's natural size), "Replace" with the accessible name "Thay media của câu hỏi này"
  and "Remove media". Audio: `AudioPlayer` in its new size "row" (no frame of its own, a 32px
  button, the time beside the track; "default" and "sm" unchanged) on a `--sidebar` band,
  with the expired-link retry, then `AudioPolicyPanel`: "Plays" as a radiogroup of native
  radios (Once, Twice, Unlimited, plus the stored count while it is the value), "Allow
  skipping ahead", "Show transcript after submitting", and the transcript marked Optional.
  Attaching audio starts its plays from the library's `defaultMaxPlays` (0 is Unlimited),
  else Twice, unless the question already has a policy (DG-69). Image: the 220px preview (its alt is the alt text, else the file name),
  the alt field, and the helper "Học viên dùng trình đọc màn hình sẽ nghe câu này thay cho
  hình ảnh." under it through `aria-describedby`; a blank alt saves `null`.
- **Choose from Media.** `AssetLibraryDialog` on `DialogShell` (760px): title, "Files you
  uploaded.", Close; a Segmented kind filter with the facets' counts, which follow the search;
  "Search files" debounced 300ms; a `minmax(160px, 1fr)` grid paged 24 at a time through
  `LoadMoreSentinel`; tiles with thumbnail or audio icon, name, meta and "Used in {n}
  questions" / "Not used yet", `aria-pressed` selection, double-click to pick; loading,
  error-with-retry, empty-library and "No files match." states; "Upload new", "Cancel",
  "Attach". It takes `kind` (default "all"); the block opens it on All only.
- **R35-8 is split, not done.** This task ships `FileInput` and uses it in the question media
  block only. `MaterialAssetDialog` keeps its own input and its image path; that half moved to
  T-R4.34's "Done when" (lane 3 owns `features/question-groups/`).
- **Keys.** New: `questionEditor.questionMedia.*` and `media.picker.*`. Removed in their own
  commit: `questionEditor.{audioAllowSeek, audioMaxPlays, audioPlaysCount,
  audioPlaysUnlimited, audioShowTranscript, media, mediaChoose, mediaDrop, mediaFromLibrary,
  mediaHint, mediaRemove, transcript, transcriptPlaceholder}` and `media.pickTitle`.
- **Deck check.** Measured against the deck at 360, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440, light and dark,
  and at 1024 and 1280 with the sidebar collapsed; no horizontal scroll. What differs is
  decided elsewhere: the second switch (DG-111) and the longer limits line (DG-63) make the
  block taller; `Switch` is the primitive's 38×22 against the deck's 34×20; the transcript
  and alt fields keep the primitives' `--input` border (DG-31) and their 16px text below 768.
- **Live specs.** `author-and-publish.live.spec.ts` and `audio-plays.live.spec.ts` attach the
  audio from the editor column (no settings sheet) and read "Gỡ media" and the "Số lần nghe"
  radiogroup.

**Done when:**

      "From Media". The deck's "WAV" and "25 MB" do not ship (DG-63). The deck's line names no
      length; the limit stays (spec §11.1), so the line states it, as today's hint does
- [x] Pre-checks from `limits.ts` for each kind (type, size, audio length, unreadable file),
      each naming the file, as today. The toasts are error-tone, in the deck's words with the
      decided limit: "Choose an audio or image file", "Images can be up to 10 MB", "Audio can
      be up to 50 MB". An upload shows its progress and its failure (DG-83, DG-111); on success
      the toast reads "Saved to Media · {name}". `upload-panel.test.tsx` moves to the hook
      with T-R4.35; this task adds the image cases
- [x] Attached card: the icon tile, the file name, the meta, one form for an upload and for a

      the dialog shows them. "checking length…" shows during the pre-check, before the upload
      starts. Then "Replace" and "Remove media".
      "Replace" picks a file of the same kind for this question; its accessible name says so,
      because the Media page's "Replace file" changes the file itself (DG-09, DG-111)
- [x] Audio: the player row through `AudioPlayer`, with `.play()` in the click's tick and the
      expired-link refresh kept; `audio-player.test.tsx` green and untouched. "Plays" is a
      radiogroup "Once | Twice | Unlimited", defaulting from the asset's default and otherwise
      Twice (DG-69; the question's value wins); a stored value outside the three shows as a
      fourth option while it is the value. Under it "Allow skipping ahead" and "Show transcript
      after submitting". "Transcript", marked "Optional", with the placeholder "Paste the audio
      script". "Students can pause" is not built (DG-111)
- [x] Image: the 220px preview and the field "Alt text: describe the image for screen readers",

### T-R4.66 — Question editor: the answer area for each type
**Depends on:** T-R4.2c, T-R4.64
**Touches:** `web/src/features/question-bank/components/{QuestionEditor,OptionsEditor,OptionField,BlanksEditor,RichBlankEditor,BlankPromptField}.tsx`, `web/src/features/question-bank/components/{TrueFalseField,GradingNote}.tsx` (new), `web/src/features/question-bank/questionSchema.ts`, `web/tests/units/question-bank/{editor,fill-blank-editor,structure-validation}.test.ts(x)`, `web/tests/units/content/gap-bindings.test.tsx`, `web/tests/e2e/rich-blanks.spec.ts`
**Reuses:** `QuestionEditor`, `OptionsEditor`, `BlanksEditor`, `RichBlankEditor`, `ChipInput` (T-R4.2), `QuestionProseField` (T-R4.64)
**Size:** M
**Done when:**
- [x] One block shows for the question's type, then the grading note. They are built once in
      `QuestionEditor`, so the builder and the bank's Question editor show the same blocks
      (DG-108). The prompt and the explanation are `QuestionProseField` (T-R4.64). Matching has
      no block until R6 (DG-113)
- [x] Single choice: "Options · click the circle to mark the correct answer"; rows with the
      radio, the letter, the text (`aria-label` "Option {letter}"), "Correct" and remove; "Add
      option" up to eight, A–H, then "Up to 8 options" in an error-tone toast (DG-63); two at
      least. Option text is a plain input, as drawn. An option that already holds formatted
      content (an imported one) keeps `OptionField`'s formatting path, as today; no new option
      gets it while `VITE_RICH_OPTION_EDITOR` is off
- [x] Multiple choice: "Options · tick every correct answer"; a checkbox per row, "Mark {letter}
      correct". With none ticked, "Tick at least two correct answers.", which blocks saving as
      the missing answer does today. With one ticked, "Only one answer is ticked. Tick another,
      or switch to Single choice.", a warning that does not block: the server takes one
      correct answer
- [x] True / False: "Correct answer · students choose one", a radiogroup of two cards, "True"
      and "False". The selected card has the success border and the `circle-check` icon named
      "Correct". The "Include “Not given”" switch is absent until R6 (DG-113)
- [x] Fill in the blank, with a rich prompt: "Accepted answers · for each gap in the prompt."
      One row per gap, in prompt order: the gap's chip, its answers as chips, an input ("Type
      an accepted answer, then press Enter", then "Add another accepted answer"), and "Match
      case". Enter, a comma or leaving the input adds; Backspace in an empty input removes the
      last; a duplicate is refused whatever its case. "Capital letters and extra spaces are
      ignored." follows the label while no gap matches case (DG-108). A gap with no answer has
      the danger border and "Add at least one accepted answer for gap {label}."
- [x] With no gap in the prompt: the dashed card "No gaps in the prompt yet", "Put the cursor
      where students type the answer, then insert a gap." and "Insert gap", which inserts at the
      prompt's caret. Today's limit on gaps and its message stay
- [x] A fill-in-the-blank prompt still stored as Markdown keeps its placeholders and
      `BlanksEditor`, as today
- [x] Short answer: "Sample answer" with "Optional. Graders see it next to each student’s
      answer." and the placeholder "Write a model answer"
- [x] Grading note under the block, with the deck's icon: "Marked automatically when the student
      submits." for single choice and true / false; the same with "An answer is correct if it
      matches any accepted answer." for fill in the blank and with "Students must choose
      exactly the ticked options." for multiple choice; "Graded by hand. Each answer goes to
      the grading queue." for short answer
- [ ] While the prompt holds gaps the other types are disabled: in the builder's menu with the
      meta "Remove gaps first", in the bank's type control with today's note. The deck's toast
      for it cannot fire and is not built (DG-115)
- [x] The zod schema keeps its `Expect<AssignableTo<…>>` assertion against the generated request
      type; switching type keeps what still applies and drops the rest, as today
- [x] Tests: `question-bank/editor.test.tsx` covers each type's block and note and both
      multiple-choice sentences; `fill-blank-editor.test.tsx` and `content/gap-bindings.test.tsx`
      cover a row per gap in prompt order, the three ways to add, the duplicate refusal, the
      "Match case" sentence and the empty card's "Insert gap"; an option with stored content
      still opens its rich editor (`content/option-field.test.tsx` green);
      `rich-blanks.spec.ts` green
- [x] Checked inside the builder and the bank's Question editor at the five widths, light and
      dark; keys vi first, en from the deck, listed in the PR

**As built (T-R4.66a: the choice, true / false and short-answer blocks, the grading note):**
- **Shape.** `AnswerArea({ value, onChange, onInsertGap })` picks the block for the type and
  puts `GradingNote({ type })` under it; `QuestionEditor` renders it once, between the prompt and
  the explanation, and the aside no longer carries the grading sentence or the separator before
  it. `onInsertGap` is declared for 66b's empty card and not read yet. Every block reads and
  writes `value`, so a question or type swap loses nothing (`builder/autosave-unmount.test.tsx`
  green); `OptionsEditor` keeps its `editorEpoch` key.
- **Touched beyond the list:** `features/question-bank/questionType.ts` (new: `QUESTION_TYPES`,
  `CHOICE_TYPES`, `optionLetter`, `typeLocked`, `trueFalseOptions`, `retype`; the bank's type
  `Tabs` and `switchType` read `typeLocked` and `retype`),
  `components/{AnswerArea,SampleAnswerField}.tsx` (new), `tests/units/question-bank/
  {answer-area.test.tsx (new),metadata-roundtrip.test.ts}`, `tests/units/content/
  option-field.test.tsx` (names only: every control of an option, its formatting buttons and
  rich editor included, is named by letter, so "Nội dung lựa chọn 1" and "Định dạng phương án 1"
  are "Lựa chọn A" and "Định dạng lựa chọn A"; the flag's behaviour is unchanged),
  `tests/e2e/rich-options.spec.ts` (the same names), and the live specs `author-and-publish`
  and `audio-plays`, whose option helpers fill "Lựa chọn A" and check "Đánh dấu A là đáp án
  đúng".
- **Rules in one place.** `choiceProblems(value)` (`tooFew`, `tooMany`, `noneCorrect`,
  `oneCorrectOfMany`, `manyCorrectOfOne`) feeds both `validateChoice` and `OptionsEditor`'s
  sentence; only `oneCorrectOfMany` does not block. `gapProblems(content, blanks)` names the gaps
  whose answers are all blank, in prompt order, and `validateBlankStructure` refuses them;
  66b draws its rows from the same call. A one-tick multiple choice parses and `publishProblem`
  passes it while the warning shows (`answer-area.test.tsx`; making `validateChoice` issue on
  `oneCorrectOfMany` fails that case).
- **Eight options.** `options` is `.max(8)` with `questionEditor.errors.maxOptions`, as the
  contract's `maxItems: 8` and `ValidateAuthoring` already refuse a ninth. A legacy question
  stored with nine keeps all nine in the form and cannot be saved until one goes, which is the
  server's answer too; nothing is dropped silently. "Add option" stays enabled and at eight
  toasts "Up to 8 options" in the error tone; remove is disabled at two.
- **True / False** is a native radiogroup of two label cards around sr-only radios ("Đúng",
  "Sai"); the selected card carries the success border and background, the `CircleCheck` icon
  is decorative, and the radio is described by an sr-only "Đáp án đúng". Not given is absent
  (DG-113).
- **True / False reads by text, not position** (R66a-1, the rule of T-19). `readTrueFalse`
  takes the option stored as exactly "True" (`trueFalseLabelKey`'s test) as the true one, or
  failing that the one beside an exact "False"; only when neither is canonical (a legacy rename,
  an imported "Đúng" / "Sai") is the first option true. The Word import keeps a question's own
  order, so a pair stored False-first is real, and reading it by position showed the inverse of
  the key. `TrueFalseField` and `retype` both read through it. Writing goes through
  `trueFalseOptions(trueIsCorrect, answer)`, which emits the canonical pair, "True" first, and
  carries each option's id across (and, for an option that had content, the canonical text as
  plain content), so a legacy rename normalises to "True" / "False" (DG-139) without losing its
  rows. The ids are load-bearing: the server deletes and rewrites a question's options on every
  save and matches stored formatted content by id, falling back to the ordinal, so a reversed
  imported pair written without ids is refused with `options[0].content` ("Phương án đã có định
  dạng…"); with them it saves. The version diff (`unpublishedChanges`) fingerprints options by
  ordinal, text and content, not by id, so id churn never reads as a change; normalising a
  False-first pair to True-first does, because the stored order changes.
- **Geometry.** Rows are the deck's: 4/6/4/4 padding, a 9px radius, a 32px mark target with an
  18px ring, an 18px letter, a 32px bare input at 14px from 1024 (the 16px input floor holds
  below it), "Correct" at 12px, a 28px remove; "Add option" is 30px with a 7px radius. An
  unmarked row's border is `--input`, not the deck's `--border`: the row is the text field's
  only boundary, and DG-31 holds input boundaries at 3:1. The sample answer's textarea uses
  `--input` for the same reason.
- **Keys** (`questionEditor`): new `markCorrect`, `optionLetter`, `removeOptionLetter`,
  `correct`, `maxOptionsToast`, `tickTwo`, `onlyOneTicked`, `trueFalseLabel`, `trueFalseHint`,
  `sampleAnswerPlaceholder`, `gradingNote.{auto,manual,anyAccepted,exactlyTicked}`,
  `errors.maxOptions`; new values for `optionsHint`, `optionsHintMulti`, `sampleAnswerHint`,
  and `formatOption` / `editFormattedOption`, which take `{{letter}}`; removed `autoGraded`,
  `manualGraded`, `removeOption`, `removeOptionN`, `optionTextLabel`, `optionPlaceholder`.
- **Left for 66b:** the per-gap rows, the empty card and "Insert gap", `onEditor`, the
  builder menu's "Remove gaps first" (31b), and the fill-in-the-blank half of the tests and of
  the deck check.

**As built (T-R4.66b: the gap rows and "Insert gap"):**
- **Which block.** `AnswerArea` answers a fill-in-the-blank with `GapAnswers` while the prompt
  is rich text: stored as rich content, or open in the rich editor, which is exactly when the
  host passes `onInsertGap`. A prompt in Markdown, the builder's new question included (its
  starter text opens the Markdown mode, and clearing it on focus does not change the mode),
  keeps `BlanksEditor`. An empty prompt's rich editor loads lazily, so for that first moment
  the Markdown block shows. `BlanksEditor` lost its rich branch
  (the rich preview, the orphan rows and their confirm) and is the Markdown editor only:
  `{{n}}` rows, the placeholder check, "Add blank", as before.
- **`GapAnswers`** (`features/question-bank/components/`): one `role="group"` row per gap,
  named "Ô trống {label}", in prompt order (`questionGaps`); each row is the gap's dashed chip,
  a `ChipInput` (success tone, 24px, no frame of its own: the row is the frame, `--input` per
  DG-31) named "Đáp án được chấp nhận cho ô {label}", and "Match case" (`Switch` named "Phân
  biệt hoa thường cho ô {label}"). Enter, a comma and leaving the input add, Backspace in an
  empty input removes the last, a duplicate is refused whatever its case, and with "Match case"
  on the comparison is exact, so "London" and "london" are both kept (the server grades them
  apart). A comma always adds: an accepted answer cannot hold one (the plan let 66 decide;
  "Done when" lists the comma). The placeholder is "Type an accepted answer, then press Enter",
  then "Add another accepted answer". The header's "Capital letters and extra spaces are
  ignored." shows while no gap matches case. A gap with no answer has the danger border, its
  input `aria-invalid`, and "Add at least one accepted answer for gap {label}." below, linked
  through `ChipInput`'s new `describedBy`, drawn from `gapProblems` (66a), the predicate the
  schema refuses with. Answers whose gap left the prompt keep today's alert and an explicit
  "Delete answers for gap {n}" behind the confirm; a bound gap's row has no remove: the gap is
  removed in the prompt.
- **The empty card and "Insert gap".** `ContentEditor` takes `onEditor`, called with the
  live TipTap editor and with `null` when it goes; `RichBlankEditor` and `BlankPromptField`
  pass it through, and `QuestionEditor` keeps the prompt's editor in state and hands
  `AnswerArea` an `onInsertGap` only while one exists. The command is `insertGap`, moved from
  `ContentToolbar` to `editor/gapCommands.ts` and exported, with the prompt's own `gapLabel`
  (`nextBlankOrdinal`), so the card and the toolbar insert alike, at the prompt's last caret.
  `gapCommands.ts` imports only a type from TipTap, so `QuestionEditor`'s chunk does not grow
  by the editor. The gap limit and its notice are the content budget's, unchanged.
- **Commits beside the task's:** `ChipInput`'s `describedBy` and the content editor's
  `onEditor` / `gapCommands.ts` are each their own commit (W3 §2.4, composites); the removed
  key `questionEditor.richBlankAnswersHint` is its own commit.
- **Keys** (`questionEditor.gapAnswers`): `label`, `hint`, `caseIgnored`, `group`, `inputFor`,
  `placeholderFirst`, `placeholderMore`, `removeAnswer`, `matchCase`, `matchCaseFor`, `empty`,
  `noGapsTitle`, `noGapsText`, `insertGap`, `removedGroup`, `removeOrphan`.
- **VER-66a's two findings, fixed here.** QA-66a-3: the Markdown blanks' hint printed
  `{{'{{1}}'}}` raw, an escape i18next does not have; `questionEditor.blanksHint` now takes
  `{{first}}` and `{{second}}` and the component passes the markers. QA-66a-2: a legacy pair
  stored as [Sai (correct), Đúng] showed "Đúng" correct, because no text was canonical and the
  first option was taken as true. `readTrueFalse` now reads a truth word before position: the
  import's `trueFalseValue` set (`t`, `true`, `đúng`, `dung`; `f`, `false`, `sai`) and yes and
  no (`yes`, `có`; `no`, `không`), trimmed of spaces and full stops at either end as the
  import trims them, in any case.

### T-R4.31a — Test builder: title bar, outline, resizing and drag
**Depends on:** T-R4.4, T-R4.5b, T-R4.3c
**Touches:** `web/src/features/tests/pages/teacher/TestBuilderPage.tsx`, `web/src/features/tests/components/{OutlineTree,OutlineGroupRow,AutosaveStatusLabel}.tsx`, `web/tests/units/builder/`
**Reuses:** `OutlineTree`, `OutlineGroupRow`, `BuilderGroupPane`, `BuilderWrites`, `useAutosave`, `SplitPane`, `MarqueeText`, `@dnd-kit` (already a dependency)
**Size:** M
**As built:**
- **Touched beyond the list:** `components/shared/SplitPane.tsx` (it measures its row from the
  first paint and on every resize, so the separator's `aria-valuenow` is the rendered width and
  `aria-valuemax` the real ceiling before any key or drag; the builder is its only consumer),
  `features/tests/components/QuestionPickerDialog.tsx` (`returnFocus`: Escape gives focus back
  to the button that opened it, which Radix missed when the click had not focused it), the
  locales, and the live specs that read builder copy (`builder-groups`,
  `admin-change-requests`, `author-and-publish`, `audio-plays`).
- **Words (DG-135).** The deck's builder calls a section a "group"; the product says "section"
  in en and "phần" in vi, because shared-context groups sit in the same outline. Everything
  else follows the deck: "Outline" / "Dàn bài", "{n} questions · {pts} pts", "{n} · {pts} pts",
  "1 pt" / "2 pts" on a row (`builder.points` is a plural now), "Add question" with "to {section}"
  on the same line, "From question bank", "Instructions", "Drag questions here", "Preview",
  and "Question removed from this test. It stays in the bank." as a toast after Remove from test.
  vi keeps the short "{n} · {pts}đ" in a section header, as its rows read "1đ"; the outline's
  header says "{n} câu · {pts} điểm".
- **Save label.** In its `deck` form `AutosaveStatusLabel` says "Saved" from load and after every
  save, with no time, "Saving…" while a write is out and "Not saved" while an edit waits; the
  failed and stale states are unchanged. It carries `data-state` (`idle`, `dirty`, `saving`,
  `saved`), which the live specs wait on now that the text no longer changes after a save.
- **Title bar.** Two groups, as the deck: the title group (`flex: 1 1 320px`: Back, the title,
  the outlined Draft pill, the save label) and the actions (Question settings and Versions as
  icon buttons with a tooltip, Preview, Publish). It keeps one row wherever the deck does, in
  both languages: Publish at y 80 and the outline at y 130 at 768 collapsed, 1024, 1280 and
  1440, and both on a second row only at 360 and 768 expanded, where the deck wraps too.
- **Outline geometry at deck scale.** 13px rows in `--fg`, the section header 28px with
  2px 4px 2px 2px padding and a 7px radius, a 12px count, the summary at weight 500, the
  instructions line as the deck's 26px button with its scroll icon, the footer's 10px padding
  and 8px icon gap, and `--shadow` on the outlined footer buttons and Preview (0.3 in dark).
  Measured against the deck at all five widths, both themes: the outline is 34px taller than
  the deck's, which is the fourth footer button (DG-135), and 18px more at 360 for the selected
  row's 44px actions.
- **Title and row widths** (R414-3). The deck sizes the title for its text plus a space, room
  for the caret, so the title button's right padding adds 0.21em; the title's hover frame sits on
  its wrapper, as the deck's does, so the button is 34px high. A row's hidden actions cancel the
  flex gap they would add (`-ml-1` until shown). Title and row titles now measure the deck's
  276.7×34 and 168.8×22 at 1280 and 1440, light and dark.
- **Rows.** A row with a publish problem keeps its title, adds the warning icon, and names the
  problem as the title button's accessible description. Hover, or focus that is visible, swaps
  the points for Move up, Move down and Remove from 768; a mouse click on a title no longer
  reveals them. Below 768 only the selected row shows them, at 44px.
- **Drag.** Over a section header the header tints `bg-brand-soft` (`--color-brand-soft`, which
  is `--accent-soft`; `data-outline-drop-into`) instead of drawing a line above it, and so does an
  empty section's drop zone; rows keep the 2px primary line.
- **Focus.** Escape on the instructions dialog returns focus to the line or the "…" button that
  opened it, and on the bank picker to "From question bank". "Add section" adds the section at
  once with its name in an input, focused. A rename that loses focus to a pointer commits at the
  click that follows the press, or after pointerup when no click follows (a tick for a mouse,
  400 ms for touch or a pen, whose compatibility mousedown moves focus only after a zero timer has
  run; a pointercancel commits at once), not on blur: the header shrinks when it leaves rename mode, and committing at
  pointerdown moved the next control from under the pointer, so "Add question" right after "Add
  section" lost its click (the live `admin-change-requests` spec), and on touch the click comes
  after pointerup. The rename is keyed by the section's identity and commits through the
  outline's current state, so a reorder or removal during the press renames the right section
  (R414-2, R414-4). Measured in Chromium, a tap fires pointerdown, touchstart, pointerup,
  touchend, then a zero timer, then the compatibility mousedown (which blurs the field), mouseup
  and click; the click-capture release covers that path, and the 400 ms only a press with no click.
- **Not done here:** the deck's 8px button radius (the primitives draw 9px at deck scale until
  R5) and its `normal` line height on buttons.

**Done when:**
- [x] Title bar: inline title (Enter or Esc blurs; empty becomes "Untitled test"), marquee on
      overflow only, status pill, Saving… / Saved, Preview, Publish (`builder/deck-frame`,
      `builder/autosave`)
- [x] Outline header "Outline" / "{n} questions · {pts} pts"; group header with grip, chevron,
      title (double-click renames, "↵ save · esc cancel"), "{n} · {pts} pts" and a "…" menu
      (Rename, Instructions, Move up / Move down disabled at the ends, Remove group with the
      deck's confirm); the instructions line under the header (`builder/section-menu`,
      `builder/deck-outline`; "Remove section", DG-135)
- [x] The deck's "group" is the product's section; the shared-context groups in the outline
      (`OutlineGroupRow`, `BuilderGroupPane`) keep their behaviour and are restyled
- [x] Question rows: grip, number, type icon, title, a warning icon when publish would fail on
      it, points; on hover Move up, Move down, Remove from test ("It stays in the bank")
      (`builder/reorder`, `builder/outline-deck-states`)
- [x] Drag with `@dnd-kit`: a 2px primary drop line, a group header as a drop target, "Drag
      questions here" in an empty group, groups reorderable; every drag has a keyboard or menu
      equivalent; menus are never clipped (`builder/deck-outline`; the header tints
      `bg-brand-soft` rather than drawing a line, as the deck does)
- [x] `SplitPane` outline clamped to [220, content − 374], default 300, stored in
      `quizzivy.builder.outline`, double-click resets; split when
      `useMediaQuery("(min-width: 768px)") && useContentWidthAtLeast(594)`, stacked otherwise
      (`builder/deck-frame`, `shared/split-pane`)
- [x] Footer: "Add question to {group}" (15 characters then "…"), From question bank, Add group
      ("Add section", DG-135)
- [x] Both autosave flushes stay (unmount and publish); `builder/autosave-unmount.test.tsx` green
- [x] Deck check at the five widths, light and dark; keys vi first

### T-R4.31b — Test builder: editor pane, dialogs and publishing in place
**Depends on:** T-R4.31a, T-R4.15, T-R4.16, T-R4.65, T-R4.66
**Touches:** `web/src/features/tests/pages/teacher/TestBuilderPage.tsx`, `web/src/features/tests/components/{PublishDialog,DraftPreviewDialog,QuestionPickerDialog,GroupPickerDialog,StudentPreview}.tsx`, `web/src/features/tests/components/OutlineTree.tsx` (type icons), `web/tests/units/builder/`, `web/tests/integration/builder-question-switch.test.tsx`
**Reuses:** `PublishDialog`, `publishProblem.ts`, `DraftPreviewDialog`, `QuestionPickerDialog`, `QuestionEditor` with its answer areas and grading note (T-R4.66), `QuestionProseField` and `ContentEditor` (T-R4.63, T-R4.64), `QuestionMediaField` and "Choose from Media" (T-R4.65), `TagCombobox`
**Size:** M
**As built (2026-10-10):**

- **The pane.** `BuilderQuestionEditor` (`features/tests/components/`) is the editor pane for a
  standalone question, composed from the shared blocks and not from `QuestionEditor`'s frame
  (W2 §1.2). Its header has "Question {n}", the type menu, Points and the "…" menu; its body
  has the prompt, `AnswerArea` with `onInsertGap` from the prompt's editor, the question media
  block, and `<details>` "More options" holding the explanation above `TagsField`. The type
  menu is a `DropdownMenu` titled "Question type", 250px, with `QUESTION_TYPE_ICONS`
  (`question-bank/components/questionTypeIcons.ts`, also on the outline's rows), "Current",
  and "Remove gaps first" on the items `typeLocked` disables; it calls `retype`.
- **Points and tags moved into the pane,** so the builder's settings button, sheet and dialog
  are gone at every width. `narrow-settings.test.tsx` and `settings-dialog-width.test.tsx`
  tested that dialog; `editor-pane.test.tsx` keeps what they pinned that still holds (points
  and a tags draft across widths, the flush between questions) against the pane.
- **The "…" menu.** Duplicate calls `duplicateQuestion` and inserts the copy after the
  original. "Move to section" (the deck's "Move to group": the deck's group is the product's
  section) is a `FormDialog` with the sections, and moves the question to the end of the
  chosen one. "Delete question" confirms with "It stays in the question bank if it came from
  there." and removes it from the outline, opening the next question.
- **Starter.** The builder's starter question is created with `promptContent`. A rich prompt
  whose text is still the starter opens empty under the placeholder "Write the question
  students will read", and the starter stays stored until the first edit, as the Markdown
  field already did (`RichProseEditor`'s `starter`). The explanation's placeholder is "Explain
  the correct answer."; its hint is the existing rule (DG-108).
- **Add from question bank** is a 560px `DialogShell` with its own list: the search goes to
  the server (`listQuestions({ q })`, debounced 250ms), pages of 50 load as the list
  scrolls, and each row shows "{type} · {level} · used in {n} tests" from `usedInTests`.
  Ticks survive searches and pages; "Add {n} questions" sends every ticked id in the order
  ticked. A question already in the test shows "In this test" and cannot be ticked.
  "Choose a complete question group" stays.
- **Student preview** is a 720px `DialogShell` over the existing `StudentPreviewPane`, with no
  new preview logic (W3 (c); D4's T-D4.2c replaces it). Previous and Next step through the
  questions and scroll the dialog's body alone (never `scrollIntoView`, which also scrolled
  the frame and left a band under the footer) to put each at the top with its passage above
  it, rather than drawing one
  question alone, because the reader draws a group's passage only above its first question;
  it opens on the question being edited (DG-143). `StudentPreview.tsx` is untouched (lane 2's
  T-R4.30).
- **Publishing in place.** Publish flushes both autosaves and opens "Publish test?"
  (`FormDialog`): the checks are `publishProblem` for each loaded question until the server
  answers, then its violations, each with "Fix it"; when nothing fails, "Every question has a
  correct answer" and "Every question has points", and "All groups have instructions or a
  title" whenever the test holds a group. Missing explanations are listed and never
  block. While a check fails, Publish refuses with an alert instead of sending (the deck
  greys it; DG-144). The optional change note goes to `publishTest` (trimmed; empty is no note). On
  success the builder stays open, takes the version's `testUpdatedAt` as its outline base,
  and toasts "Published. You can now assign it." with Assign
  (`/teacher/assignments/new?test=`, the wizard's name since T-R4.27a). `publish-in-place.test.tsx` pins the base, the note,
  the toast and a genuine `STALE_WRITE` from another tab.
- **Live specs** publish through `publishInBuilder` (`e2e/support/live.ts`) and author new
  questions in rich text through the type menu.
- **Shared-context groups (T-R4.31b-2, its own PR after this one).** `OutlineGroupRow` is
  drawn in the outline's own rows: a 32px header with the grip, the disclosure, the Layers
  icon, the title and "{n} · {points}", then Move up, Move down and Delete as 24px icon
  buttons; its members are question rows (number, the type's icon, the prompt, points),
  indented under a rule. Below 768px the group's actions show at 44px only while the group or
  a member is selected, and its summary gives way to them, as a question row does (DG-135).
  The deck draws no shared-context group, so these follow the question rows. `BuilderGroupPane`
  takes the question editor's frame: a header with the group's title over its save state, and
  "Save a copy to the bank" as a 30px outline button, then the alerts and the embedded
  `GroupComposer` (lane 3's, unchanged) in the editor's body padding. Every label and
  `data-outline-group` the tests use is kept.
- **From VER-65.** QA-65-1: "Choose from Media" gives focus to the attached card's "Replace"
  after Attach or a double-click (its `returnFocus`), and back to "From Media" on Escape or
  Cancel. QA-65-4: a file's name in the block wraps to two lines and carries its full name as
  a `title`.

- [x] The deck's "group" is the product's section; the shared-context groups in the outline

### T-R4.32 — Question bank
**Depends on:** T-R4.4, T-R4.5b, T-R4.1a, T-R4.1b, T-R4.1c, T-R4.2c, T-R4.15
**Touches:** `web/src/features/question-bank/pages/teacher/QuestionBankPage.tsx`, `web/tests/units/question-bank/`
**Reuses:** `DataTable`, `Pager`, `BulkActions`, `BulkTagDialog`, `AddToTestDialog`, `TagCombobox`, `Sheet`, `BankNavigation`
**Size:** M
**As built (T-R4.32: question bank):**
- **Touched beyond the page:** five new files beside it in `features/question-bank/pages/teacher/`
  (`bankFilters.ts`, `BankFilterPanel.tsx`, `BankTagFilter.tsx`, `BankCells.tsx`,
  `bankColumns.tsx`; not in `components/`, which is lane 1's), the question-bank route line in
  `app/router.tsx`, which now declares a teacher handle (crumb `teacherShell.nav.questionBank`,
  width 1320) so the page renders in `TeacherLayout` and follows the theme, the `bank` block of
  both locales, and `tests/support/questionBank.tsx` (new). `TagCombobox` is not used: the tag
  filter is a search over the server's tags, not an entry field, so it is its own combobox.
- **The three contract gaps, built with what the contract has:** Audio is the `hasAudio`
  parameter and shows no count, because `QuestionTypeFacets` carries none; the tag list shows
  no per-tag counts; and a row's tag chips have no remove button, because `tagQuestions` only
  adds (a tag is removed in the editor). The page never calls a missing operation.
- **Column thresholds** are the deck's on the content width, raised where the 220px aside and
  its 14px gap would leave the table narrower than its tracks need (`bankThresholds`): Level
  from 598, Type from 750, Used from 842 and Updated from 944 beside the aside, the deck's 540,
  640, 780 and 900 below 1024. Where the deck's tracks fit, the columns match it (all four at
  1280 expanded); where the deck overflows its card by up to 62px (1024 to 1247), a column
  drops instead. Below the Type threshold the prompt carries "{type} · {level}".
- **Labels:** the types are the five the product stores, with the editor's names (Short answer
  for the deck's "Open answer", DG-60), plus Audio; levels are Pre-A1 to C2 and skills the six
  of DG-62. The search placeholder keeps "Search the question text": `q` searches the prompt
  only, so the deck's "Search prompts, answers, tags" would promise more than it does. Share
  (R7, DG-90) and Import (R6, DG-72) are absent. `BankNavigation` (Questions | Groups) stays
  under the header, the only way to the groups screens (DG-68).
- **Not carried over:** the per-row menu (Open, Add to test, Duplicate, Delete), the in-row
  duplicate, the audio preview row and the usage row. The deck draws none of them on the list;
  its question editor carries Add to test, Duplicate and Delete (T-R4.33). Until 33, Delete is
  the bulk bar's, which confirms with the deck's sentence and names any question a draft still
  holds with the server's message; Duplicate waits for 33. `AudioPreviewRow` and
  `QuestionUsageRow` are unused until 33 decides. The old `list`, `row-menu`,
  `bulk-selection` and `delete` tests went with them; `bank-list`, `bank-tag-filter` and
  `bank-bulk` replace them.
- **Deck check:** the deck could not be rendered here (it loads React and Babel from unpkg,
  which the proxy refuses), so the page was measured against the deck's markup at 360, 1024 and
  1280, light and dark, on the seeded API: the 24px h1, the 220px aside and 14px gap, the 34px
  search, the 40px header band and 10px row padding, no overflow at any width. The full matrix
  is the tester's (gate policy G-2).
**Done when:**
- [x] Header "Question bank" / "{n} questions you can reuse across tests." + New question (→
      editor); the Import button is absent in R4 until a release owns question import (open
      item)
- [x] Filters: a 220px sticky aside from 1024, a "Filters" sheet `min(320px,100%)` below with a
      count; Type (Open answer is `short_answer`, Audio is `hasAudio`), Level (Pre-A1…C2) and
      Skill with server counts; Tags search popover (Enter toggles the first match, Esc
      closes), chips, Clear, and Match Any | All from two tags
- [x] Columns: select, Question (+ tags), Type ≥640, Level ≥540, Used ≥780 ("{n} tests"),
      Updated ≥900; type and level inline below 640; a row opens the editor
- [x] Bulk: Add to test (Test and Group), Add tag, Delete ("Tests that already use them keep
      their copy.")
- [ ] States: skeleton; "No questions have these tags." + Clear tags; error; filters and page in
      the URL; deck check at the five widths, light and dark; keys vi first

### T-R4.35 — Media library
**Depends on:** T-R4.4, T-R4.5b, T-R4.17a, T-R4.17b, T-R4.1c, T-R4.2b, T-R4.2c
**Touches:** `web/src/features/media/pages/teacher/MediaPage.tsx` (from `MediaLibraryPage.tsx`), `web/src/features/media/components/{MediaCard,UploadDialog,ReplaceDialog}.tsx` (new; `UploadPanel.tsx` retired, its pre-check, probe and upload path moved to `web/src/features/media/useMediaUpload.ts` (new), which `UploadDialog`, `QuestionMediaField` and `MaterialAssetDialog` call), `web/src/features/question-bank/components/QuestionMediaField.tsx` and `web/src/features/question-groups/components/MaterialAssetDialog.tsx` (the upload call only), `web/src/features/media/limits.ts`, `web/tests/units/media/` (`library-blocked.test.tsx` among them)
**Reuses:** `AudioPlayer` semantics, `Waveform`, `CardGrid`, `FileDrop`, `useFileDrop`, `probe.ts`
**Size:** M
**As built (T-R4.35: media library):**
- **Touched beyond the list:** `components/{RenameDialog,DeleteMediaDialog,UploadStatus}.tsx`
  (new), `media/{api,format,probe}.ts`, the media route line in `app/router.tsx`, which now
  declares a teacher handle (crumb `media.title`, width 1320) so the page renders in
  `TeacherLayout` and follows the theme, its row in `tests/units/app/teacher-routes.test.ts`,
  the `media` namespace of both locales, and `tests/e2e/media-library.spec.ts` (new, stubbed).
  `router-chunks.test.ts` is unedited: its admin pattern names all of `features/media/`, and
  the hook is not exported from `features/media/index.ts`.
- **`useMediaUpload({ onUploaded, kind?, send? })`** runs the pre-check (`quickCheck`: the
  kind's extensions, then its size; `precheck` adds an audio file's duration), the upload with
  its progress, and a refusal, failure or cancellation as a message. It draws nothing:
  `UploadStatus` draws the state (the old panel's checking line, progress row with cancel and
  alert with "Choose another file"), and each host keeps its own file input. `start(file,
  { kind, send })` overrides the kind and the sender per upload, which is how the upload
  dialog sends `defaultMaxPlays` and the replace dialog `replaceMedia`. A new start supersedes
  the one in flight; unmounting aborts it, where `UploadPanel` let it finish into an unmounted
  host. Nothing retries.
- **The two hosts** render `<input aria-label="Chọn tệp từ máy">` and `UploadStatus`
  themselves, so the live suite's selector holds. `MaterialAssetDialog`'s audio path moved to
  the hook; its image path, with its own size check and `groups.imageLimit` message, is
  unchanged. The upload's cancel button is now named "Huỷ tải lên" / "Cancel upload": "Huỷ"
  collided with the group dialog's own Cancel.
- **Image pre-checks (DG-63) are new:** JPG, PNG or WebP (no GIF) up to 10 MB, with their own
  two messages. The upload and replace dialogs run `quickCheck` as field validation, then the
  full pre-check on submit.
- **The page:** `?tab=` (`audio`, `image`, `unused`) and `?q=` live in the URL through
  `useListFilters`, a change resets the page, and the query sends `kind` or `unused=true`
  with the debounced `q`; the tab counts are the response's facets. 24 files a page with the
  `Pager`. A window drop opens the upload dialog with the file chosen (its kind from the
  extension); the drop is disabled while any dialog is open. The storage line's sizes come from
  `formatStorage`, which writes gigabytes from 0.1 GB as the deck does and keeps each number
  with its unit; the line wraps instead of the deck's `nowrap`, for the longer vi copy.
- **The card:** audio plays from its own `<audio preload="none">`; `.play()` runs in the
  click's tick and the page is told after, and a card that stops being the page's current one
  pauses, so one file plays at a time. The waveform is seeded by the asset id and fills with
  the playback position. A media error re-reads the library for a fresh signed URL. An image
  shows its thumbnail (`object-cover`) and falls back to the deck's icon and size when the URL
  fails; its meta line is "{w} × {h} · {size}", so the size in pixels stays visible under a
  thumbnail. The menu trigger is the deck's 26px button, named "File actions for {name}".
- **Replace** (DG-09 copy) sends `replaceMedia` once; after any answer, failures included, it
  invalidates the media, question, group, test and preview queries, because a failed replace may
  have committed. **Delete** asks with the deck's copy ("No question uses this file." or "Used
  in {n} questions. Those questions will show a missing file."); a file with `usageCount > 0`,
  or a 409 that names versions, opens the notice "Used by a published version, so it can't be
  deleted" with links to those versions instead. **Rename** is `updateMedia` with the trimmed
  name.
- **Download** is a link to the signed URL in a new tab (`rel="noopener noreferrer"`): a
  cross-origin URL cannot be named by `download`, so the file keeps its storage name. Follow-up
  (backend): a `response-content-disposition` on the signed URL would name it.
- **The upload dialog's play limit defaults to "2 plays"** (decision T-14): DG-69 makes the
  card's value the default of new questions, and the builder's default is Twice. The deck's
  form would start at its first option, "Unlimited".
- **Abort on unmount is deliberate** (REV-35): the old panel let an upload finish into an
  unmounted host, leaving an orphan file that the owner's quota counted.
- **Review round (REV-35):**
  - R35-1: `useFileDrop` keeps its window listeners bound for its lifetime and always cancels
    a drop that carries files; `enabled` gates only the drag state and the callback. Before,
    a file dropped on a dialog's backdrop, with the page's drop off, navigated the tab to the
    file and killed the upload in flight. `FileDrop`'s disabled test follows the new contract.
  - R35-3: a card pauses only an element that is playing. `pause()` on an unloaded element
    starts resource selection, so every card had fetched its recording on render; the e2e
    spec now pins that nothing is requested before a click.
  - R35-4: `readDuration` settles as unknown after 10 s (`PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`) and revokes its
    object URL either way; `cancel` ends the check as well as the upload, and `UploadStatus`
    offers it during the check.
  - R35-6: a thumbnail that fails tells the page once per URL, as the audio does. The page
    re-reads the library only when its data is over a minute old, without cancelling a read in
    flight, so a file that fails on a fresh URL cannot loop the list.
  - R35-7: `UploadStatus` reads the deck tokens.
- **Verification round (VER-35):**
  - QA-35-1: Rename, Replace and Delete open from an item of a menu that has already closed,
    so they had no trigger to return focus to and focus fell to `<body>`. Each card's menu
    trigger carries `data-media-menu={id}`; the page records it when an item opens a dialog
    and passes it as the dialog's `returnFocus`, so Esc, Cancel and a rename return to it.
    A delete points focus at the next card's trigger, else the previous card's, else the
    Upload button when the page empties. A replace returns to the old trigger on close and
    moves to the new file's trigger once the library has re-read, because the replacement is
    a new card.
  - QA-35-2: a cancel is its own `cancelled` state, drawn as the neutral status line
    "Đã huỷ tải lên." / "Upload cancelled." with "Choose another file", not under the refusal
    title.
  - QA-35-3: a size refusal goes through `formatOverLimit`, which gives the exact bytes,
    grouped for the language ("52.428.900 B"), when the usual figure would equal the limit
    ("50.0 MB" against 50 MB).
  - QA-35-4: tests for the four surviving mutants (a second start aborts the first, a 409
    naming versions shows them, a drop under a dialog does not open Upload, a fresh URL that
    fails starts no fetch), each checked against its mutant.
- **Finding (QA-35-1, not fixed here):** the same menu-item-to-dialog pattern, with no
  `returnFocus`, is on the rebuilt `AssignmentDetailPage` ("Close early" from its row menu,
  and the Reopen menu's choices) and on the not yet rebuilt `TestsListPage`,
  `ClassesListPage`, `ClassDetailPage`, `GroupsListPage`, `QuestionBankPage` and the
  builder's `OutlineTree`. `Monitor` already passes its menu trigger as `returnFocus`.
- **Follow-up (R35-5, the DG-09 copy owner):** a 409 `MEDIA_REFERENCED` that names only groups
  (`details.groups`, no versions) falls to the generic error line in the delete dialog; the
  notice names published versions only. This predates the task and is not fixed here.
- **Retired:** `UploadPanel.tsx`, the table, bulk delete and image viewer of the old page, and
  27 `media.*` keys that nothing reads any more.
- **Tests:** `upload-panel.test.tsx` became `use-media-upload.test.tsx` with its nine cases
  and their names, plus seven (image pre-checks, the result handed on, progress and cancel,
  abort on unmount). New: `media-card-play.test.tsx` (the second play site, same tick),
  `media-page.test.tsx` (15), `upload-hosts.test.tsx` (both hosts: upload, over-length,
  failure, cancel), `format-storage.test.ts`; `library-blocked.test.tsx` goes through the
  menu; `limit-strings.test.ts` covers the four new limit sentences. `media-library.spec.ts`
  checks the five widths in both themes and a real play in Chromium.
**Done when:**
- [x] Header "Media" / "Audio and images used in questions." + Upload; storage card "{used} of
      {quota} · audio {a} · images {i}" with an accent and an info segment
- [x] Tabs All / Audio / Images / Not used with counts; a wired search. The counts are
      `listMedia`'s facets (T-R4.17a), so they follow the search
- [x] Cards `minmax(240px,1fr)`: audio play/pause through the `AudioPlayer` path (`.play()` in the
      click's tick) with the decorative waveform; image thumbnail and dimensions; name, meta,
      "Used in {n} questions" or "Not used" (warning), the play limit with a repeat icon
- [x] Menu: Rename, Replace file (copy per DG-09, vi first: bank questions and drafts get the new
      file, published versions keep theirs; limits per kind, DG-63), Download, Delete (refused
      with "Used by a published version, so it can't be deleted" when it is)
- [x] Upload dialog: Audio | Image, file, Play limit for audio; client pre-checks from `limits.ts`
- [x] `useMediaUpload` holds what `UploadPanel` did: the pre-check, the probe, the upload and
      its progress and failure. `QuestionMediaField` and `MaterialAssetDialog` upload through
      it with their behaviour unchanged, and `upload-panel.test.tsx` moves to the hook with no
      case dropped
- [x] `audio-player.test.tsx` (the `AudioPlayer` canary) green and untouched
- [x] States: skeleton; "No media yet" + Upload; error; deck check at the five widths, light and
      dark; keys vi first

### T-R4.36 — Imports: history and upload
**Depends on:** T-R4.4, T-R4.5b, T-R4.1b, T-R4.2a, T-R4.2c, T-R4.3b; built on today's contract, and T-R4.21's and T-R4.55's pull requests wire the facets, the counts and the `text` format into it
**Touches:** `web/src/features/imports/pages/teacher/{ImportsListPage,NewImportPage}.tsx`, `web/src/features/imports/components/{SourceIntake,FileSlot,ImportStatusBadge}.tsx`, `web/src/features/imports/status.ts`, `web/src/features/imports/api.ts`, `web/tests/units/imports/`
**Reuses:** `ImportsGate`, `SourceIntake`, `useSourceIntake`, `FileSlot`, the segmented control with counts (T-R4.2), `Pager`
**Size:** M
**Implementation checkpoint:** The current-contract presentation is accepted
after independent source review, 136 imports unit tests, strict TypeScript,
scoped lint and formatting, successful hosted CI, and 43 passing independent
Chromium cases with no retries. History and upload were compared with the deck
at 360, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440 in both themes; history also covers both sidebar
states and the real 15px main scrollbar (961px outer width at 1280 expanded).
Native verification covers same-key polling failure and recovery, quiet upload
cancellation, the actual main scroller and sticky footer, keyboard controls,

by its existing suites. The following deferred contract additions keep their

intake. Until then no counts, text rows or paste calls are invented: Processing

**As built (2026-10-07):** The processing page keeps the real import lifecycle in
one 720px deck frame. Five rows map the actual stages; elapsed time, retry attempts,
waiting, revision/idempotency guards, cancellation and committing states remain.
Ready uses the current review counts and findings, and source/download/remove gates
retain actual source IDs. Text presentation follows the exam source's role/format,
with completed character and plain-text receipts. Its failed headings and recovery
copy do not guess which source failed. Restart opens the existing paste-mode
placeholder; functional paste intake is T-R4.57, recognition conventions T-R4.56.
No run-event history or prototype formatting-kept line is synthesized.

Affected public/file/locale/style checks pass (123 checks across nine suites), with
strict typecheck, lint and formatting clean. Existing file-state native acceptance
covers 32 cases and 25 reviewed images, followed by four bilingual breadcrumb checks.
The pasted-text wave covers 36 cases and 28 individually reviewed images at
360/768/1024/1280/1440, vi/en, light/dark, plus Start/cancel/review/retention and
spinner rotation, hover/focus pause and reduced-motion checks. Its file-only
failure-heading defect was retained and corrected; a fresh 16-case production wave
covers both error codes, both languages, companion present/absent, phone/light and
wide/dark, with all 16 images independently reviewed. Older failing diagnostics stay
preserved; unchanged geometry/motion evidence is reused only for unchanged behavior.
This task's static source title has no marquee. Current-code required CI passed at

**As built (T-R4.57: paste a test):**
- **Touched beyond the list:** `features/imports/usePastedText.ts` (new: the text, its deferred
  count and title, and the re-check before sending), `api/testdata/pasted-text-counts.json` (74
  cases added: the page's example and four rule cases, the tester's 42 texts from VER-57 and 27
  aimed at one rule each; the Go parity test passes on all 120), `tests/units/imports/history.test.tsx`
  (the pasted row), `finding-notice.test.tsx` (`TEXT_MARKS_UNAVAILABLE`, whose text T-R4.56
  had already written) and `upload-flow.test.tsx` (its deferred-paste case now asserts the box).
  `router.tsx` gives `imports/new` the deck's trail, Tests › Imports › the page (QA-57-3); in
  paste mode `PageHead`'s `crumb` renames the last crumb. `api.ts`, `findings.ts`, `fixtures.ts`
  and `i18n.test.ts` are unedited: every new key is a literal `t()` call that `i18n.test.ts`
  already resolves.
- **`paste.ts` follows the server's text path** (`recognition/exam.go`, `keys.go`,
  `inline_keys.go` and `build.go`; DG-13: the server's review stays the authority). It reads
  each line in the server's order: a one-line key, a key section, the title, another paper
  (which ends the count), a part heading (roman with or without a mark, or a named part), a
  question label the sequence admits (sub-labels under their parent, which becomes a group), the
  open question's next option, its answer line, then text, which can open an instruction or a
  passage group. Without a heading, parts are inferred where the instruction changes. Keys follow
  the server: entries start with a real label, several to a line, a following line continues the
  last value, and a label two parts share is ambiguous unless the key names the part. A
  question's answer counts only when every value resolves to the same answer (an option by
  letter, text or truth; a short answer; split blanks). A conflict, an ambiguous key and a letter
  with no options count as neither answered nor missing, so the count never claims an answer
  the server will not (QA-57-2). Not ported: numbered blanks inside a passage (open cloze) and a
  key reached only through a numbered blank. Mutation runs: 79 rules in `paste.ts`, each killed
  by a fixture case; five more mutants change no count and are not run (the "next number"
  clause the forward jump covers, the one-section guard on inference, consuming the title line,
  the title's instruction rule and passage groups, which matter only for open cloze). The page,
  the hook and the history row: 24 rules, each killed.
- **Departures:** the tips go beside the example by a container query on the card (DG-142) at 594px, its
  inner width at a 900px viewport with the sidebar expanded, so that state matches the deck
  exactly (measured at 899 and 900). Where the card is wider than the deck's viewport rule
  expects, the two columns arrive early: from 624px with the sidebar as a drawer, and from 768px
  with it collapsed, where the deck still stacks them below 900. The clipboard refusal reads "Ctrl+V (Cmd+V on Mac)": FONT-1 keeps
  the command glyph out of locale strings. The ready hint uses the file flow's "Processing takes
  about a minute. You can leave this page." (DG-16). The title's placeholder in paste mode is
  the dated fallback it will use. The duplicate note has a plural English form ("Numbers 2 and
  4 appear twice"). The source switch keeps `fill` and loses the fixed 168px option width
  (DG-21).
- **Behaviour worth knowing:** a retry after a failed process does not send the text again; a
  refused send (400, 409, 413, 415) drops the upload identity, and a 409 re-reads the import
  first. Ctrl or Cmd+Enter submits the form, and is ignored while `isComposing`. The
  monospace box has no brand font, so on a machine whose monospace font lacks precomposed
  Vietnamese letters the placeholder's marks may stack (seen with the Linux fallback in the
  sandbox; Menlo and Consolas carry them).
- **Headless Chromium leaves `readText()` pending without a permission**, it does not refuse,
  so `paste-import.spec.ts` denies `clipboard-read` through CDP (`Browser.setPermission` for
  the context) to take the real refusal path.
