### T-R4.45 — The bell in both consoles, and the student's settings
**Depends on:** T-R4.10a for T-R4.45a; T-R4.4, T-R4.10b, T-R4.7, T-R4.8 for T-R4.45b
**Touches:** `web/src/features/notifications/` (new: `components/NotificationsPopover.tsx`, `useNotificationSummary.ts`, `kinds.ts`, `api.ts`), `web/src/app/modules.ts` (notifications on), `web/src/layouts/TeacherLayout.tsx`, `web/src/layouts/StudentLayout.tsx`, R3's student Settings page, `web/src/features/dashboard/NotificationsButton.tsx` (removed), `web/tests/units/notifications/` (new)
**Reuses:** `IconTile`, `useIdlePolling`, `Sheet` on phones
**Size:** M
**Delivered in parts:** two, each one branch and one pull request
**T-R4.45a:** the notifications web client (`features/notifications/{api,kinds}.ts`, `useNotificationSummary`). Depends on T-R4.10a. T-R4.41, T-R4.44 and T-R4.46 use its files and do not need the bell
**T-R4.45b:** the bell in both shells and the student's settings. Depends on T-R4.4, T-R4.10b, T-R4.7, T-R4.8 and T-R4.45a
**As built (T-R4.45a: the notifications client):**

- **Files and types:** seven unmounted modules under `web/src/features/notifications/`
  and nine support/test files under `web/tests/units/notifications/`. `api.ts` exports
  `Notification`, `NotificationKind`, `NotificationEvent`, `NotificationParams`,
  `NotificationTarget`, `NotificationPreference` and `MySummary` as their same-named
  `components["schemas"]` types; `NotificationPage` is
  `operations["listNotifications"]["responses"][200]["content"]["application/json"]`.
  No API, generated type, module flag, route or mounted surface changed.
- **Wire exports:** `notificationKeys` has readonly tuples `all: ["notifications"]`,
  `list: ["notifications", "list"]`, `summary: ["notifications", "summary"]` and
  `preferences: ["notifications", "preferences"]`. Calls are
  `listNotifications(params?: Readonly<{ before?: string; limit?: number }>, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<NotificationPage>`,
  `markNotificationsRead(ids?: readonly string[]): Promise<void>`,
  `getMySummary(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<MySummary>`,
  `getNotificationPreferences(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<NotificationPreference[]>`
  and `updateNotificationPreferences(all: readonly NotificationPreference[]): Promise<NotificationPreference[]>`.
  Absent list params default to `{}`; reads forward optional cancellation.
- **Mark read:** absent IDs send `{}` to mark all; `[]` sends no request. More than 100
  IDs travel sequentially in batches of at most 100, stopping on the first failure;
  204 resolves void. `useMarkNotificationsRead(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, readonly string[] | undefined, unknown>`
  invalidates `notificationKeys.all` after either outcome, without optimistic changes.
- **View exports:** `NotificationTone = "neutral" | "info" | "success" | "warning" | "danger"`;
  `NotificationView = Readonly<{ icon: LucideIcon; tone: NotificationTone; text: string; to: string | null }>`;
  `KIND_LOOK: Record<NotificationKind, Readonly<{ icon: LucideIcon; tone: NotificationTone }>>`;
  `audienceOf(kind: NotificationKind): "teacher" | "student"`;
  `notificationPath(target: NotificationTarget | null): string | null`;
  `describeNotification(notification: Notification, t: TFunction, locale: Locale, options?: Readonly<{ canGrade?: boolean }>): NotificationView | null`.
  An unknown kind or missing actual sentence input returns null; consumers skip it.
  Known zero counts and an empty-but-present class-name list remain valid.
- **Looks and sentences:** waiting submitted papers use SquarePen/warning and
  “{toGrade} papers in {title} need grading”; ordinary hand-ins FileCheck/neutral and
  “{count} papers handed in for {title}”; flagged Flag/danger and “{studentName} left
  the test {focusLost} times”; closing Clock/warning and “{title} closes soon and
  {notSubmitted} students have not handed in”; joined UserPlus/neutral and
  “{studentName} joined {className}”; rotated RotateCw/info and “The join codes of
  {count} classes were replaced: {names}”; opened ClipboardList/info and “{title} is
  open and closes at {when}”; due soon Clock/warning and “{title} closes at {when}”;
  extended Clock/info and “{title} now closes at {when}”; ready CircleCheck/success and
  “Your result for {title} is ready”. Plain hand-in, closing, rotated, opened and
  extended variants are undrawn and reuse the decks' icons for those things.
- **Words:**15 new `notifications.kind` keys after `integrity` and before `pagination`,
  Vietnamese first, with one/other in both languages for all five plural sentences.
  Names stay plain text; `{when}` uses the app's existing timezone formatter. The local
  sentence helper masks string values during translation and restores them in one
  callback pass, preserving literal braces, dollar syntax and opaque-token text while
  numeric counts still select the plural. The 15 fixed kind templates per locale must
  contain no reserved token representation; public guards hold that bounded contract.
  Class names join with comma-space and gain “…” only when count exceeds the supplied
  names. DG-129 records papers rather than answers, leaving the test rather than only
  fullscreen, and a ready result without a score.
- **Destinations:** assignment→`/teacher/assignments/:id`, attempt→`/teacher/attempts/:id`,
  grading→`/teacher/grading`, classes→`/teacher/classes`, result→`/app/attempts/:id/result`,
  studentAssignment→`/app/assignments/:id`; missing/malformed UUIDs and unknown/null
  targets return null. Submitted with positive toGrade goes to Grading regardless of
  the merged target, settling T-R4.10a's question. Absent canGrade defaults true; false
  changes only that destination to the safe server target.T-R4.45b passes canGrade from
  `useCan` with the Grading nav item's own any-of rule. Attempt remains standalone
  until T-R4.25/46 provide assignment-sheet integration; no assignment ID is invented.
- **Summary:** `SUMMARY_POLL_MS = 60000` and
  `useNotificationSummary(enabled?: boolean): { unread: number }`, default enabled,
  read `/me/summary` through existing idle/resume polling. Polling stops while hidden
  or idle ten minutes; resume reads immediately. Unread is 0 while disabled, pending,
  refreshing or failed, including failure with prior cached data; disabled sends no
  request. This client is not mounted, so no bell-dot browser result is claimed.
- **List:** `useNotificationList(enabled?: boolean)` defaults enabled and returns
  `{ items: Notification[]; hasMore: boolean; loadMore: () => void; loadingMore: boolean; isPending: boolean; isError: boolean; refetch: (options?: RefetchOptions) => Promise<QueryObserverResult<InfiniteData<NotificationPage, unknown>, Error>> }`.
  Twenty items per cursor, no polling, no loaded cursor repeated, null ends the list;
  repeated loadMore calls coalesce. Disabled list/loadMore sends no request.
- **Preferences:** `TEACHER_EVENTS: readonly ["attempt.submitted", "attempt.flagged", "assignment.closing"]`
  and `STUDENT_EVENTS: readonly ["assignment.due_soon", "result.ready"]`;
  `withPreference(all: readonly NotificationPreference[], event: NotificationEvent, patch: Readonly<Partial<Pick<NotificationPreference, "inApp" | "email">>>): NotificationPreference[]`;
  `sameRows(a: readonly NotificationPreference[], b: readonly NotificationPreference[]): boolean`.
  These retain all five rows, order, other console and unedited channels.
  `useNotificationPreferences(): { query: UseQueryResult<NotificationPreference[], Error>; save: UseMutationResult<NotificationPreference[], Error, readonly NotificationPreference[], unknown> }`
  caches the actual PUT answer. Email is stored/returned; no email is sent.
- **Consumers still to build:** T-R4.44 uses the events, withPreference, sameRows and
  preferences hook for DirtyBar; T-R4.41 uses list/unread join_codes.rotated/read mutation;
  T-R4.46 edits KIND_LOOK. T-R4.45b mounts the bell/popover and student's settings, enables
  `app/modules.ts`, adds the `/me/summary` default MSW handler, invalidates the separate
  teacher nav-count query too, removes the old dashboard bell, and reads the teacher
  shell's `/teacher/summary`. Those surfaces retain their native keyboard/motion gates.
- **Evidence:** the original foundation passed 246 tests across 18 files and 19 mutation
  targets, including one explicit type gate. The literal-value repair passed 268 tests
  across 19 files, three restored behavioral mutants and final 24-case sibling checks
  including two added template guards; these cover 270 distinct cases across the green
  runs. Actual typecheck and scoped lint/format pass. Independent source review, focused
  QA, exact-head CI and integration remain recorded gates. Future bell and settings
  Done-when checkboxes remain open.

**Done when:**
- [ ] The bell shows a 7px dot only while something is unread (DG-82); the popover (340px, max
      100vw − 24): "Notifications" and "Mark all read"; rows with a tone tile per kind, text from
      `kind` and `params` in the reader's locale, relative time, a lime unread dot; a click marks
      read and navigates (a hand-in with papers to grade goes to Grading, or to its assignment
      for a reader who cannot open Grading); "Load more"; empty "You're all caught up."; an error line
- [ ] The teacher shell polls `/teacher/summary`, the student shell `/me/summary`, both every
      60 s through `useIdlePolling`
- [ ] Student Settings: Notifications with "Test due soon" and "Result ready" (Messages from
      teachers in R7, Daily practice reminder in R10); Profile gains Change photo and a
      server-saved Language; "Larger text in tests" saves to `preferences.largerTestText`,
      migrating R3's local value once
- [ ] The student bell and the student's Notifications section are built here; R3 shipped
      neither. `web/src/app/modules.ts` turns the notifications module on, the one availability
      map R3's shell reads
- [ ] Tests: dot only when unread, mark all read, the route for every kind, a student sees no
      teacher kind; a shell test that the bell appears only with the notifications flag on; deck
      check of both shells at the five widths, light and dark; keys vi first

### T-R4.51b — Publish snapshot fixture lifecycle
**Depends on:** — (verification follow-up; does not replace T-R4.51)
**Touches:** `server/internal/modules/tests/application/tests/{publish_harness_test.go,mediakinds_test.go,publish_snapshot_test.go,publish_fixture_lifecycle_test.go,publish_fixture_probe_failure_test.go}`
**Size:** S

The five single-session snapshot cases used a committed Admin fixture whose cleanup ignored
errors and attempted audit deletion. In an isolated database it could leave the last Admin
behind. Their setup now owns a genuine Teacher and publication inside one outer transaction;
the production application still begins and commits its nested savepoints. The snapshot
commands, names, expected values and behavioral assertions are byte-identical after reversing
only the five setup substitutions and removing four unused setup locals. This is a disclosed
fixture-only change to a canary file; release evidence must not call the whole file byte-identical.
The other four canary files are unchanged by this task.

**Done when:**
- [x] Cleanup is registered before the first insert; exact owned identities are captured,
      rollback errors are checked, and absence is verified before the pool closes. No audit or
      attempt-event UPDATE/DELETE, privilege, role, trigger or production change
- [x] Normal and actual Fatal-subprocess publication prove scoped rollback. A transient
      absence-query error suppresses the success marker and is refused by the same Fatal
      verifier even when later parent reads succeed; the new regression failed before repair
- [x] Independent source review and QA approve the exact repaired surface: eight top-level
      integration cases and one lifecycle subcase pass on PostgreSQL18.6 with the actual app
      role, zero parent failures/skips. Expected child failures remain negative evidence
- [x] Scoped vet/staticcheck/format pass. Independent before/after59 app-table counts agree
      with the clean purpose baseline, app append-only ACL is unchanged, and connections close.
      The purpose database is intentionally preserved; counts are not a full-row/storage proof
- [x] Exact-head CI result passes and the task PR merges into `work/redesign-r4`

The original committed `pubMakeAuthor` helper stays byte-identical for its other callers.
Its ignored-error/audit-delete cleanup and their committed-concurrency lifecycle remain separate
work; these single-session snapshots do not prove committed visibility or lock ordering. No
historical/quarantined data repair, database teardown, main merge or deployment belongs here.

### T-R4.52 — Release v0.10.0
**Depends on:** T-R4.1 … T-R4.51, T-R4.53 … T-R4.57, T-R4.62 … T-R4.66
**Touches:** `docs/plan/`, release notes
**Size:** S
**Done when:**
- [ ] The release checklist below is complete, with Thuong's go before `main`

---

## Release checklist

- [ ] 70 §8.1: every CI step locally, in order, judged by exit code — `make lint`, `make
      gen-check`, `make test-api`, goose up/down/up, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm
      format:check`, `pnpm test:unit`, `pnpm test:integration`, `pnpm build`, `pnpm e2e`, `pnpm
      e2e:content`, `pnpm e2e:live` (join codes, imports and the take-test start path changed),
      `node scripts/check-design-deck.mjs`
- [ ] Canaries green and untouched: `publish_snapshot_test.go`, `audio-player.test.tsx`,
      `attempts/application/tests/events_test.go`, `client.refresh.test.ts`, `router-chunks.test.ts` (regexes
      updated only); `builder/autosave-unmount.test.tsx` green
- [ ] Security: the isolation suite covers every new `/teacher/*` operation with an `x-resource`
      entry for every uuid in its path, query or body (kinds `notification` and `override`
      added), and its two-user pair covers `/me/*`; the payload-leak walk covers
      `/me/notifications`, the result's class average and the note to students; the
      credential-limit test lists `resetStudentsPasswords` and `listClasses`; the
      `STUDENT_SHARED` escalation case passes; `/me/*` responses are `no-store`
- [ ] Migrations rehearsed on a Neon branch of production: timings recorded; questions with more
      than eight options reported; the number of legacy join codes to rotate recorded; with
      T-R4.49, the two contract migrations rehearsed and the `legacy_admin_path` count read
- [ ] Exam windows checked: no assignment closing within the deploy window
- [ ] No import run is queued or running at the deploy: `word-pipeline-v3` retires a v2 run,
      which fails `PIPELINE_RETIRED` after the ten-minute grace and is retried by its teacher
- [ ] Cloudflare "Add visitor location headers" enabled before the deploy (DG-58); `.env.example`
      and Fly config carry `MEDIA_OWNER_QUOTA_MIB`; `VITE_RICH_QUESTION_EDITOR` is gone from
      `.env.example` and from the Pages build's variables
- [ ] Deploy API, then Pages; the API log shows `legacy_join_codes_rotated` with the rehearsed
      count; smoke `/healthz`, `/livez`, a sign-in as the owner (lands on `/teacher`) and as a
      student; the owner builds, publishes and assigns a test, writing one prompt with a table
      and attaching an image with alt text, opens an import in review, pastes
      a test and reaches its review, and grades one answer; an old `/admin/tests` bookmark lands
      on `/teacher/tests`
- [ ] Smoke of the fixes `develop` has gathered since v0.9.0, which first ship here: a student
      on a phone answers one question in airplane mode, closes the tab, turns the network on and
      presses Continue, and the answer is there (#330); a reload of the engine opens the question
      that was open (#320); the owner's roster shows a recording's end and the events of a
      student who left the test with the ✕ (#314, #331)
- [ ] The checks v0.9.0 left with the owner are done here if they are still open: a sign-in as
      an admin and as a student on production, a short test on a phone and on a laptop, and
      `SELECT has_database_privilege('quizzivy_app', current_database(), 'TEMPORARY')` answering
      false (#194, `docs/setup/operations.md`)
- [ ] Release notes: the teacher workspace moved to `/teacher` and old links redirect; **every
      class has a new join code — share it with students who have not joined yet; students
      already in a class are not affected**; the bell and notification settings; per-student
      extensions and reopening; the version history and change notes; media rename and replace;
      a test can be pasted as text, with the marks the page lists for answers; the question
      editor formats text, tables and links, keeps formatting pasted from Word and Google Docs,
      and attaches an image as well as audio
- [ ] Release notes, the fixes since v0.9.0 (no fix-only release was cut, by Thuong's decision of
      2026-10-04): for students, answers typed without a network are kept when the student
      comes back through Continue (#330), the engine reopens on the question that was open
      (#320), the timer stops when time is up (#329), and a recording shows one length in the
      test and on the result (#321); for teachers, the timeline shows when a recording ended or
      was blocked and keeps the events of a student who leaves the test (#314, #331); for
      everyone, a sign-out no longer leaves "sign in again" over the sign-in page (#328)
- [ ] Hand-off to R5: remove the legacy join-code lookup branch; give `/admin/*` to the Admin
      console and narrow `LegacyTeacherRedirect` to known teacher paths; drop `openAssignments`,
      `activeStudents`, `totalStudents` and `recentAttempts` from `Dashboard` (T-R4.19); grant
      `quizzivy_maintenance` `SELECT, UPDATE (closes_at)` on `assignment_student_overrides` in
      `grant_maintenance_role` (T-R4.12)
- [ ] Hand-off to R6: T-R6.13's `tfng` mapping covers pasted text as it covers files; the
      builder's "Matching" and "Include “Not given”" (DG-113); images and audio inside a prompt
      or an explanation, if Thuong confirms the open item's default (DG-109)
- [ ] Neon compute hours measured for the week after the release and recorded against NFR-A10
      (PR-7)
- [ ] `release/0.10.0` → `main` `--no-ff` with Thuong's go; tag `v0.10.0`; back-merge to
      `develop`

## Open items

- **Audio length and WAV (DG-63, spec §11.1).** R4 raises the audio size cap to 50 MiB and keeps
  MP3/M4A and the five-minute limit; the deck's "MP3 or WAV" copy is written as "MP3 or M4A". A
  listening section longer than five minutes, and WAV, need Thuong's answer before the cap on
  duration moves. The 2026-10-04 builder adds "MP3, M4A or WAV up to 25 MB" for question media
  and GIF for an image in the text. Default: every screen states DG-63's limits, and GIF is not
  taken.
- **Images and audio inside a prompt or an explanation (DG-109, D1).** The 2026-10-04 editor
  draws "Image" and "Audio" in its toolbar and an image pasted or dropped into the text. The
  contract's `QuestionContent` holds no media. Building it means a contract change, asset
  references inside content in the publish snapshot and in media replace and delete, signed
  URLs in the student payload, and the engine and the result rendering them, with a rule for
  how inline audio is limited and recorded. Default: not in R4; T-R4.63 leaves the two buttons
  out and refuses a pasted or dropped file. It is built, as D1 requires, in R6, whose track
  already runs through those areas; `76-r6.md` gains the tasks when Thuong confirms. The
  alternative is to build it in R4, as at least three more tasks.
- **A pasted image no longer refuses the paste (DG-115, spec §7.1).** The deck's paste preview
  says "{n} images in the copied content were left out.". Today `clipboardHTML` refuses a paste
  that holds an image, `clipboard.test.ts` pins it, and spec §7.1 says to reject it as one
  paste. Default: T-R4.63 leaves images out and counts them, keeps every other refusal, and
  T-R4.50 changes the spec line. The alternative keeps the refusal and does not build the
  left-out line. Thuong confirms.
- **"Choose from Media" lists own files only (DG-112, DG-90).** The dialog's sub speaks of files
  "other teachers shared with you", and media is not a shared kind. Default: the caller's own
  files and the sub "Files you uploaded."; shared files wait for R7's sharing to cover media
  and for a drawing.
- **The rich editor for every teacher.** `VITE_RICH_QUESTION_EDITOR` is off in production.
  Default: R4 removes the flag and ships the editor as the deck draws it (T-R4.64; removed
  in T-R4.64d); rich
  option text stays behind `VITE_RICH_OPTION_EDITOR`, off, because the deck draws plain
  option inputs. Thuong confirms the first, and whether rich options are ever turned on.
- **What the Markdown mode is (DG-110).** Default: the mode is the stored form, nothing converts
  without the teacher's confirmation, and Markdown gains tables and strikethrough through
  the micromark and mdast extensions for the two, behind one plugin: up to four direct
  dependencies, which also load on the student's path.
- **The bank's Question editor was not redrawn (DG-108).** Default: T-R4.33 builds the builder's
  2026-10-04 drawing of the form on that page, and keeps the page's own frame. A later export
  that redraws the screen is followed.
- **"Students can pause", "Matching" and "Not given" (DG-111, DG-113).** None has a model in R4.
  Default: "Students can pause" is not built, and the two existing playback switches stay;
  "Matching" and the "Include “Not given”" switch arrive in R6 with their types.
- **Alt text for a question's image (DG-111).** Default: T-R4.62 adds one nullable column to the
  question and its snapshot. The alternative is alt text on the media file, set once in the
  library; the deck draws it on the question.
- **The review's editor (DG-114).** Default: T-R4.38 keeps gaps, stored content and the read-only
  view, and builds no Markdown mode there. The deck draws the opposite on each point, in a
  prototype that saves plain text.
- **Join codes without a use limit (spec §6, O-06).** The deck's "Leave empty for no limit"
  conflicts with O-06's default of 40 and the non-nullable request field. R4 keeps O-06.
- **Username sign-in (spec §5).** The deck's Add student asks for "Username or email"; sign-in is
  by email, so R4 asks for Email.
- **Question import in the bank (D1).** The deck's Import dialog (CSV, XLSX, `.quizzivy`, a tag
  for every question, "Skip questions that already exist") has no release in 70, and D1 says
  what the deck draws is built. Default: the button stays absent in R4, and the dialog with
  `POST /teacher/questions/import` (`content.questions.write`, formula-safe, bounded in the API
  or run by the worker) goes to a named release: R5 after T-R5.13 brings XLSX, or R6 after
  T-R6.3 so the importer knows every type. Otherwise Thuong records a D1 exception in 70 and a
  DG entry.
- **Import controls without a backend (DG-67, D1).** Saved profiles, source conventions, the Word
  template and the audio slot, plus "Create another test from this file", "Delete import",
  "Process again" after a cancel, Move / Split / Merge and the processing history. No release
  R5–R11 has their backend yet. Default: they stay absent in R4; their backend and UI tasks go
  to a named release (R6 beside T-R6.13, or R11's reconciliation), or Thuong lists them as an
  approved post-1.0 exception in 70 §2 and DG-67's "Needed by" moves to it.
- **Note to students.** The Student deck's Test intro draws a teacher note; the Teacher deck
  draws no field to write it. Default: T-R4.11 adds `assignments.student_note` and the
  contract, and T-R4.26 and T-R4.27b add a plain "Note to students (optional)" field, placed by a
  new DG entry in `docs/design/gaps.md`. If Thuong decides not to build it, T-R4.11's note
  bullet goes and the element is recorded as not built.
- **Resetting or re-addressing a shared student (security posture).** A teacher may reset, or
  give a new email to, only a student no one else reaches, by T-R2.13's rule (72-r2.md): every
  class of theirs, archived ones included, is the teacher's; no other account created them; no
  other account's assignment targets them individually; and with no class, the teacher created
  them. Anything else answers `STUDENT_SHARED` and goes to an admin (T-R2.13, T-R4.20).
  Thuong confirmed the refusal, the safer choice, on 2026-09-28.
- **Wave 2's defaults (2026-10-04).** Seven decisions the wave-2 briefs took where the plan and
  the deck leave a choice; each is built unless Thuong says otherwise.
  - T-R4.63: a paste that carries a file beside HTML text (an Excel or Word selection) is
    previewed, and Ctrl+Shift+V inserts its plain text. The alternative refuses any paste that
    carries a file.
  - T-R4.22: a rotated legacy code answers through today's code paths, with no special case:
    "revoked, ask your teacher" where self-join is open, the unknown-code answer where it is
    closed or the class archived (spec §6).
  - T-R4.2c: the stepper's value can be typed, so a 47-minute limit stays possible although the
    deck draws only − and +.
  - T-R4.2c and T-R4.66: "nghe" and "nghé" can both be entered as accepted answers, and "London"
    and "london" under Match case, because the server grades them as different.
  - T-R4.17b: an import in review that names a replaced file keeps the old file and binds it at
    commit; the file is live but outside the library.
  - T-R4.22: a class still unrotated after its one retry waits for the next API start-up, so the
    release check re-runs the count query after the deploy.
  - T-R4.16: a draft whose group graph cannot be read answers `unpublishedChanges` null, so the
    builder shows no banner for it.
- **The Admin's own-row lists (DG-53, D3).** In the teacher workspace the Admin's content lists
  show only their own rows; everything stays reachable by id and, from R5, in the Admin
  console (T-R4.54). Default: build it so; Thuong may prefer an "All teachers" filter, which
  would be a deck change.
  As built (T-R4.54), an Admin who opens another teacher's draft by id cannot add a question
  to it: the picker lists the Admin's bank, a new question is the Admin's, and a draft accepts
  only its owner's questions (T-R2.12a), so the save answers "The test refers to a question
  that does not exist." Default: leave it; T-R4.31b builds the picker for the owner. Both
  alternatives need the contract, which names no owner on a test: an owner flag so the
  builder hides its add controls, or a bank list scoped to the draft's owner.
- **Student activity (DG-54).** "Not signed in", a true "Last active" and the password's "Last
  changed" line wait for R5's activity tracking.
- **What a paste keeps of automatic numbering.** Whether the plain-text clipboard of Word and
  Google Docs keeps auto-numbered question numbers and option letters is not known, and it
  decides how well a pasted test recognises. T-R4.51 records it with real documents. Default:
  nothing changes, since the page's tips already ask for typed numbers and letters.
